# 03 · The inconsistent flows, with their reasons

How this list was made: the code was read subsystem by subsystem, which gave 290 candidate problems; the code was then searched across subsystems from five angles (one decision / many paths; stage × control asymmetry and hidden state; state outside the graph; vocabulary; model roles and visibility); duplicates were merged into distinct issues; each issue was then re-checked against the cited files. Line numbers are against commit `59eea5d`.

Result: 112 distinct issues. 62 confirmed, 5 confirmed, cosmetic, 45 not yet verified. Severity after verification (refuted excluded): 17 high, 68 medium, 27 low.

Severity: **high** = a reviewer can lose a decision, pay twice, see stale state as current, or the software crashes; **medium** = a flow that confuses or duplicates; **low** = naming or documentation.

## 1. Ten reasons behind the hundred symptoms

Almost every issue below traces to one of these. The last column says which part of the plan (04) removes the reason.

| # | Reason | Issues | Removed by |
|---|---|---|---|
| R1 | **No single registry of stages.** Stage keys, labels, order, lock rules, tags and UI rows are six hand-kept lists that disagree, so the Overview, the Run page, the lock, the estimate and the job runner each see a different pipeline. | 22: I30, I35, I36, I37, I38, I39, I40, I48, I50, I52, I72, I73, I74, I75, I76, I77, I78, I79, I80, I81, I83, I84 | 04 §1 and phase 1 (one `StageSpec` registry; every list generated) |
| R2 | **Review state kept outside the dependency graph.** Gates, answers and bookkeeping live in `store.meta` keys, files and flags used as state machines; `graph.stale` cannot see them change, so the preview is incomplete and the To-do, the Overview and the engine disagree about what is out of date. | 35: I03, I04, I07, I09, I11, I17, I29, I31, I32, I33, I40, I42, I43, I45, I46, I49, I51, I53, I54, I55, I56, I57, I59, I60, I62, I64, I65, I66, I67, I68, I70, I78, I103, I105, I106 | 04 §3 and phase 2 (every influencing state becomes a node with edges; meta keys migrated) |
| R3 | **One `upsert` for recomputation and for human annotation.** Every changed write stamps a node current with all its inputs, so a person's answer or a bookkeeping write erases pending re-assessments; proposals record outputs but not the inputs they read. | 10: I17, I26, I58, I59, I61, I62, I63, I69, I71, I80 | 04 §3 and phase 2 (`store.write` vs `store.annotate`; proposals record consumed versions) |
| R4 | **Human decisions implemented once per entry point.** The same decision is written by `todos.apply`, `actions.edit`, `todos.bulk`, the CLI and `planning.apply_protocol` with different options, marks, flag handling, logging and report refresh. | 20: I01, I02, I03, I04, I05, I06, I07, I08, I11, I12, I13, I14, I15, I16, I18, I19, I23, I28, I84, I85 | 04 §4 and phase 3 (one `@operation` per decision; CLI and UI are wrappers) |
| R5 | **Each caller re-implements 'do one study'.** Pipeline wrappers, the engine, `sync.process_pdfs`, feedback and per-study re-run each wrap `extract_one` / `rob_one` / `screen_one` with their own skip rules, fetch logic, proposal mode and policy, so the same change is applied on one path and proposed on another. | 18: I06, I26, I27, I29, I30, I31, I32, I41, I57, I63, I66, I71, I85, I89, I102, I105, I107, I111 | 04 §2 and phases 2-3 (`StageSpec.items/run/recompute`; one engine entry `engine.update`) |
| R6 | **Model chosen by entry point, not by stage and role.** `ctx.stage` beats the tag, `job_agent` beats everything, the verifier is resolved by five code paths, and cost is attributed by tag prefix with a 'feedback' catch-all. | 15: I06, I21, I22, I34, I35, I36, I44, I47, I48, I65, I76, I77, I86, I95, I98 | 04 §6 and phase 5 (`model_for(stage, role)`; cost and cache keyed by stage × role) |
| R7 | **The UI grew per feature.** The To-do page is an inbox and a settings page, the Run page a job launcher, the study page the only place with an impact preview; no stage has a page and no control exists for every stage. | 17: I13, I14, I25, I27, I28, I30, I40, I45, I49, I52, I70, I81, I82, I99, I100, I101, I106 | 04 §5 and phase 4 (stage pages from one template; 'Needs you' index; preview everywhere) |
| R8 | **Writes outside the queue and LLM calls in the HTTP thread.** Two 'busy' predicates, auto-applied rules on GET, protocol/search model calls under the App lock, and an in-memory answer queue that drops failures. | 10: I10, I18, I19, I20, I24, I25, I34, I55, I96, I97 | 04 §4 and phase 3 (single store lease; no LLM in HTTP threads; conflict reported, never dropped) |
| R9 | **Second readings not designed as one mechanism.** The verifier is anchored on the writer's answer, reads a different slice of the paper, runs for two of nine check points, and the escalation rule (reason → verifier → person) is implemented for RoB and the eligibility doubt only. | 11: I68, I77, I86, I87, I88, I90, I91, I92, I93, I94, I104 | 04 §6-7 and phases 5-6 (one verifier mechanism, blind by default, same budget as the writer) |
| R10 | **Documentation written per feature.** README, GUIDE, configs/README and UI help text each restate behaviour that has since changed. | 4: I108, I109, I110, I112 | every phase regenerates its docs from the registry; GUIDE sections 6-7 rewritten last |

## 2. The issues, by kind

Each entry: verdict · severity · the issue as confirmed against the code (or the first-pass statement when not yet checked) · evidence · reasons. `reach` means the change affects downstream outputs.

### Same decision, several implementations (20)

#### I08 · Protocol change has five entry points that disagree on revision history, actor, provenance action name and decision log

*confirmed · high · human-loop · reasons: R4*

Protocol change has five entry points with diverging side effects. The To-do answer `protocol:edited` (todos.py:425-433) and `sragent rescreen` (cli.py:69-80) read protocol.yaml, upsert as actor 'user', sync_criteria and rescreen_rule_only, write no protocol_revisions entry and leave protocol_approved set; only the To-do path writes human_decisions.jsonl (todos.py:566); its provenance action is `protocol:edited` (todos.py:572) while the CLI records `protocol_edited` (cli.py:77). The UI 'Edit criteria' / 'Ask the agent' go through planning.apply_protocol (planning.py:91-110), which also rewrites protocol.yaml, appends protocol_revisions, pops protocol_approved and records `protocol_edited` (user edit) or `protocol_revision_requested` (agent revise, upserted as actor 'agent'), with no human_decisions line (server.py:624-653). planning.plan at the next run picks up a hand-edited protocol.yaml, records `protocol_edited` (30-31) but upserts it with the store's default actor 'agent' and reason 'protocol updated' (50-55) with no revisions entry, no approval pop and no human_decisions line; plan() itself does no rule-only rederive, although pipeline._screen (pipeline.py:26-32) later reassesses affected screen decisions through the update engine (LLM calls possible). The provenance summary therefore counts the hand-edit act under two names (`protocol:edited` vs `protocol_edited`) and the interface edit under the same `protocol_edited` as the CLI, and the approval state after a change depends on which entry point was used.

Scenario: Entry point 1 (To-do): reviewer picks 'I edited protocol.yaml, re-apply it' -> POST /api/answer {todo_id:'protocol', option:'edited'} -> todos.apply: _category -> human_judgment -> require_judgment_allowed; branch at todos.py:425-433 reads protocol.yaml, st.upsert('protocol', actor='user', reason='protocol edited by user'), sync_criteria(actor='user'), screening.rescreen_rule_only; no protocol_revisions entry, st.meta['protocol_approved'] untouched; then log_answer (566) writes human_decisions.jsonl and provenance.record(..., f"{tid.split(':',1)[0]}:{opt}") at 572 writes action 'protocol:edited'. Entry point 2 (CLI): `sragent rescreen` -> cli.cmd_rescreen (69-80): require_judgment_allowed, provenance.record(action 'protocol_edited', no 'changed'), upsert actor='user', sync_criteria(actor='user'), rescreen_rule_only; no human_decisions line, no revisions entry, protocol_approved kept. Entry points 3/4 (UI 'Edit criteria' / 'Ask the agent'): pcEditSave/pcAsk (index.html:850-860) -> POST /api/protocol -> server.protocol_change (624-653) -> planning.edit_criteria (actor='user') or revise_protocol (apply_protocol with actor='agent') -> apply_protocol (91-110): writes protocol.yaml, upsert, sync_criteria, rescreen_rule_only, provenance action 'protocol_edited' or 'protocol_revision_requested', appends protocol_revisions, pops protocol_approved; no log_answer anywhere in that path. Entry point 5 (hand edit + run): planning.plan (23-32) detects the differing file, records provenance 'protocol_edited', then st.upsert at 54 with store.upsert's default actor='agent' and reason 'protocol updated', sync_criteria(st, protocol) with default actor='agent'; no revisions entry, no approval pop, no human_decisions line, no rule-only rederive in plan() itself (pipeline._screen at pipeline.py:26-32 later reassesses screen decisions through engine.plan/execute if the run continues past search). provenance.summary (provenance.py:100) then counts by_action, so the same hand-edit act appears as 'protocol:edited' (To-do) or 'protocol_edited' (CLI, next run), and the interface edit shares 'protocol_edited' while the agent revision is 'protocol_revision_requested'.

Evidence:
- `sragent/todos.py:425` — 'edited' branch: yaml load, upsert actor='user', sync_criteria(actor='user'), rescreen_rule_only; no protocol_revisions append, protocol_approved not popped
- `sragent/todos.py:572` — provenance.record(ctx, 'human_judgment', f"{tid.split(':',1)[0]}:{opt}") -> action 'protocol:edited'; log_answer at 566 is the only human_decisions write among the five paths
- `sragent/cli.py:69` — cmd_rescreen: provenance 'protocol_edited' (no changed=), upsert actor='user', sync_criteria, rescreen_rule_only; no log_answer, no revisions, approval kept
- `sragent/stages/planning.py:91` — apply_protocol: file write, upsert, sync_criteria, rescreen_rule_only, provenance 'protocol_edited'|'protocol_revision_requested', protocol_revisions append (107), protocol_approved pop (109)
- `sragent/stages/planning.py:26` — plan(): edited file -> require_judgment_allowed + provenance 'protocol_edited' (30-31); upsert at 54 without actor (store default 'agent') reason 'protocol updated'; sync_criteria default actor 'agent' (55)
- `sragent/store.py:76` — upsert signature: actor: str = 'agent' default
- `sragent/ui/server.py:624` — protocol_change: op revise -> revise_protocol, op edit -> edit_criteria; ctx.save/self.saved only; no todos.log_answer
- `sragent/pipeline.py:26` — _screen: after plan, engine.plan finds reassess/rederive items and executes them (so the hand-edit+run path does get decisions updated, via the engine rather than rescreen_rule_only)

#### I41 · 'Process added PDFs' re-screens, extracts, drafts RoB and propagates model revisions on the live store with policy=False, bypassing update_policy and proposals; the same change is proposed on every other path

*not yet verified · high · graph-engine · reasons: R5*

sync.process_pdfs runs review_one, extract_one, rob_one and then feedback._propagate on the live ctx with cs id pdf_<rid> (sragent/sync.py:34-48), which calls engine.execute(..., policy=False) and IMPACT + _revise_dependent on the live store (feedback.py:418, 425-442); the change set is only logged. actions.rerun and engine.propose work on store.clone(), and update_policy is applied only in engine.execute with policy=True (engine.py:219-231). configs/README.md:183 says update_policy governs every out-of-date update. A single PDF for an 'awaiting' study can rewrite synthesis statements and the report without any approval, while the same extraction change from feedback or a re-run is a proposal.

Evidence:
- `sragent/sync.py:34` — process_pdfs: extract_one, rob_one, then feedback._propagate(ctx live, ...,'pdf', ...) at 45-47
- `sragent/feedback.py:418` — engine.execute(sb, cs, roots, tag, render=False, max_rounds=2, policy=False) then IMPACT revisions
- `sragent/engine.py:219` — pol = update_policy if policy else {}
- `configs/README.md:183` — update_policy described as governing all out-of-date updates

#### I55 · Screening rules are governed by meta.rules_applied and a GET-triggered auto-apply from six call sites; the engine re-applies rules only when some other screen item is stale, and newly rule-excluded nodes have their open flags resolved with no proposal

*confirmed · high · screening · reasons: R2, R8*

Screening rules are governed by meta.rules_applied, rewritten by every rules.apply call from six call sites (screening.py:435, 510, 575; engine.py:242; snowball.py:130; server.py:115). App._auto_rules runs from App.ctx() on the first request (including the UI's state poll and the studies/study/preview/history GETs) that recreates the Ctx after the enabled rules or RULES_VERSION changed, guarded only by job is None, protocol present and screen nodes present; it upserts screen nodes with actor 'rule', resolves every open flag on them, and saves, so a code bump or a rule-set edit changes eligibility decisions and closes flags when the page is opened, with no job, proposal, to-do or impact preview (the change is visible only afterwards in 'Excluded automatically' and the changelog). engine.execute calls rules.apply only when some screen item is already stale (engine.py:241), which a rule change never causes because the screen -> cfg:screening_rules edge documented in graph.TYPES (graph.py:41) is never written by screen_one, rules.apply, reassess or migrate. Stage runs always re-apply while the UI applies lazily, so 'Excluded automatically' can change between a CLI run that does not touch screening and the next UI open.

Scenario: Call sites: rules.apply is invoked from screen_all (screening.py:435), fulltext_review (510), rescreen_rule_only (575), engine.execute (engine.py:242), snowball (snowball.py:130) and App._auto_rules (server.py:115); each call ends by overwriting st.meta['rules_applied'] = {rules, version, n} (rules.py:114). GET-triggered apply: App.ctx() (server.py:93-102) recreates the Ctx whenever store.json's mtime changed or no Ctx exists and then calls _auto_rules(); _auto_rules returns early only if self.job is not None, there is no protocol, no screen nodes, or meta.rules_applied already matches (enabled rules, RULES_VERSION); otherwise rules.apply(ctx) + ctx.save(). App.state() (server.py:847-851, the UI's poll) and the GET handlers for studies/study/preview/history (server.py:1087-1109) all call ctx(). So after a RULES_VERSION bump (rules.py:21) or an edit of topic.screening_rules, opening the UI runs rules.apply: for each record with no human_decision / human_decision_pending_fulltext / rule_restored that classify() now matches, the screen node is upserted with decision 'exclude', actor 'rule' (rules.py:99-104), and every open flag on it is resolved with actor 'rule' (rules.py:105-107), including an 'awaiting classification' or 'eligibility_doubt' flag; no job, no changeset/proposal, no to-do and no engine.preview precede it (the only trace is the 'Excluded automatically' group with a Restore button, and the changelog). Engine gating: engine.execute calls rules.apply only when some screen item is in todo and the cfg:screening_rules node exists (engine.py:241-242); items come from graph.stale (engine.py:69) which compares each node's depends_on versions. graph.TYPES documents a screen -> cfg:screening_rules edge (graph.py:41), but no code writes it: screen_one builds deps [rec, protocol, crit:*, (ft)] (screening.py:247-249), rules.apply reuses sc['depends_on'] or [rec, protocol] (rules.py:102), reassess keeps existing deps (screening.py:296-298), and migrate() adds cfg edges only to extraction/rob/synthesis/protocol/search (graph.py:171-186); a repo grep finds 'cfg:screening_rules' only in graph.py:41/61 and engine.py:241. Hence a changed rule set bumps the setting node's version (graph.sync_settings) but no screen node becomes stale, engine.plan yields no screen item and engine.execute never re-applies the rules; only stage runs (always) or the UI (lazily, when rules_applied differs) do. The CLI-vs-UI difference follows: a CLI command that does not call rules.apply (e.g. extract/rob/synthesize) after a RULES_VERSION bump leaves the old exclusions; the next UI open applies the new ones.

Evidence:
- `sragent/ui/server.py:93` — ctx(): recreates Ctx when store mtime changed, then _auto_rules()
- `sragent/ui/server.py:104` — _auto_rules: early return only if job is not None / no protocol / no screens / rules_applied matches; else rules.apply + ctx.save
- `sragent/ui/server.py:851` — state() (the UI poll) calls self.ctx(); GET studies/study/preview/history also call app.ctx() (1087-1109)
- `sragent/rules.py:103` — upsert actor='rule'; lines 105-107 resolve every open flag with actor='rule'; line 114 rewrites meta.rules_applied
- `sragent/rules.py:102` — deps = sc['depends_on'] or [rec, protocol]: cfg:screening_rules never added
- `sragent/engine.py:241` — rules.apply only if a screen item is already in todo and cfg:screening_rules exists
- `sragent/graph.py:41` — TYPES documents screen -> cfg:screening_rules, but migrate (171-186) and screen_one (screening.py:247-249) never write that edge
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:435` — screen_all; fulltext_review 510; rescreen_rule_only 575; snowball.py:130

#### I63 · Pipeline extract_all/rob_all use their own staleness (source_version) and skip rules that disagree with engine.plan; a confirmed RoB is re-versioned without content change; only screen and synthesize wrappers reconcile with the engine; five different wrappers around extract_one/rob_one

*confirmed · high · pipeline · reasons: R5, R3*

extract_all re-runs only on `ext is None or source_changed` (sragent/stages/extraction.py:362-368, source_changed 356-360) and rob_all likewise (rob.py:185-186), comparing data.source_version with ft.version instead of graph.stale/dep_versions; a node with status 'excluded' is not None, so a re-included study whose full text did not change is skipped (and its extraction stays inactive, so a first synthesis omits it via table_text), and new field:/dom: settings nodes are not checked, while engine.plan emits 'reactivate' (engine.py:142-145) and detects new settings (117-132). pipeline._extract/_rob never call the engine; only _screen (when screen items are stale) and _synthesize (only if an unedited synthesis exists) do (pipeline.py:23-68), so reactivations, new settings and retirements are reconciled only at the synthesis step; a new full text and snowball additions are not examples of this gap, because source_changed and the None check do catch them. For a human-confirmed RoB whose full text changed, rob_all and sync.process_pdfs both call rob_one, which re-upserts the confirmed data with a new source_version (rob.py:22-31); the store sees different data and bumps the version (store.py:90-108), so every citing statement becomes stale and is revised with model calls (feedback.py:554 is reached before any change check, with identical editable views) plus verification, whereas engine.plan would 'keep' the pinned node without a version bump (engine.py:104-105). The same study step runs under five wrappers with different fetch/inclusion/cache/proposal rules: extract_all/rob_all, engine._extract/_rob (fetch_one if no ft), actions.rerun (sandbox, cache off, note, _propagate), feedback._include_study (fetch, only when missing or inactive), sync.process_pdfs (live, unconditional, _propagate only if the extraction changed).

Scenario: (A) Re-included study skipped by the pipeline. ext:X and rob:X exist with status 'excluded' (set by rules.exclude at rules.py:145-147, engine._statuses at engine.py:355-361, or verify_existing at screening.py:418-421). The reviewer sets X to include in the Studies page: actions.edit (actions.py:111-128) upserts screen:X with human_decision and does not touch ext/rob status (the only reactivations in the package are feedback._include_study, feedback.py:580 and 603). Next `sragent run`: _screen -> screen_all finds nothing; engine.plan shows no reassess/rederive screen item (screen:X's own deps did not move, and graph.stale skips the non-active ext:X), so the engine is not run; _fulltext -> needs_fulltext_review is False for a human decision; _extract -> extract_all (extraction.py:362-368) builds rids from `st.get(ext) is None or source_changed(...)`: ext:X is a node (not None) and its data.source_version equals ft:X.version, so X is skipped; _rob -> rob_all (rob.py:185-186) likewise skips; _synthesize: with no synthesis yet, synthesis.synthesize -> table_text (extraction.py:377-379 skips nodes whose status != active) writes the first synthesis without X and records X in synthesis_considered (synthesis.py:112); with a human-edited synthesis it only logs. Only the branch 'a synthesis exists and is not edited' runs engine.execute, whose plan emits 'reactivate' for ext:X (engine.py:142-145) and whose _statuses flips the status. The same holds for an extraction field or RoB domain added via an accepted settings change: engine.plan 117-132 emits extract/assess for the new field:/dom: node, extract_all/rob_all never look at depends_on. (B) Confirmed RoB re-versioned. rob:Y has human_confirmed; the reviewer drops Y.pdf into fulltext/ and runs `sragent pdfs` (or the full text is re-fetched by review_one with refetch/local file). sync.process_pdfs (sync.py:34-38) calls extraction.extract_one and rob.rob_one unconditionally for included Y; equally, `sragent run` -> rob_all selects Y because source_changed(rob:Y) is true (rob.py:185-186). rob_one's confirmed branch (rob.py:22-31) builds d = old data with the new source_version and upserts it; ReviewStore.upsert (store.py:90-108) sees data != old data, appends history, bumps version. Every statement citing Y depends on rob:Y (synthesis.statement_deps) with dep_versions at the old version, so graph.stale lists it; engine.plan emits 'revise' (engine.py:108-112) for every unpinned statement; engine._synthesis (415-421) calls feedback._revise_dependent, which makes the model call at feedback.py:554 before any change check, with before/after views that are identical (editable() for rob omits source_version), and then synthesis.verify_one for each touched statement. engine.plan on rob:Y itself (stale against ft:Y) returns 'keep' (104-105) and would not have bumped the version. (C) Five wrappers around extract_one/rob_one verified: extract_all/rob_all (live store, None-or-source_version rule, ctx.map); engine._extract/_rob (engine.py:364-383: fetch_one if no ft, records before/after in the change set); actions.rerun (actions.py:256-307: sandbox clone, llm.cache=None, reviewer note, stage set, then _propagate); feedback._include_study (573-585: fetch if no ft, reactivate by status or call only when the node is missing); sync.process_pdfs (16-49: live, unconditional extract_one+rob_one, _propagate only if the extraction's editable view changed).

Evidence:
- `sragent/stages/extraction.py:364` — rids = only or [rid for included if st.get(ext) is None or source_changed(st, ext)]; source_changed (356-360) compares data.source_version with ft.version only
- `sragent/stages/rob.py:185` — rob_all: rob is None or source_changed(rob:rid); a confirmed rob with a changed full text is selected
- `sragent/stages/rob.py:22` — human_confirmed branch: d = dict(old data, source_version=ft.version); st.upsert(... reason='source changed; the assessment a person confirmed is kept')
- `sragent/store.py:90` — upsert: same only if data == old data; otherwise history appended and version += 1 (105)
- `sragent/stages/synthesis.py:77` — statements upserted with depends_on=statement_deps(st, cites) = ext:/rob: of each cited study (+cfg:question)
- `sragent/engine.py:104` — plan: a pinned rob that is stale -> 'keep'; synthesis stale -> 'revise' (108-112) unless pinned; reactivate for an excluded-status ext of an included study at 142-145; new field:/dom: deps at 117-132
- `sragent/feedback.py:554` — _revise_dependent makes the model call before comparing before/after; editable() (79-93) omits source_version so the views are identical
- `sragent/pipeline.py:46` — _extract/_rob call extract_all/rob_all only; _screen runs the engine only when screen items are stale (28-30); _synthesize only when a synthesis exists and is not human-edited (62-68)

#### I01 · Eligibility decision is written by two functions (todos.apply elig vs actions.edit screen) with different options, flag handling, marks, report refresh and log ids

*confirmed · medium · human-loop · reasons: R4*

The human eligibility decision has two implementations with different peripheral behaviour. todos.apply 'elig:' (todos.py:434-452; reached by /api/answer and by /api/bulk from the To-do table, index.html:1032) accepts include/exclude/pdf, resolves every open flag on the screen node whatever its source (446-448, including 'rerun ... pending review', manual_edit and source_changed flags), never calls report.write_reports, and logs todo_id 'elig:<rid>' (566) with provenance 'elig:<opt>' (572-573). actions.edit kind=screen (actions.py:111-134; reached by /api/edit from the study page, index.html:1642-1647) accepts include/exclude/awaiting (115), moves 'rule' into rule_restored (124-125), resolves only screening/None/feedback flags not marked 'pending review' (130), rewrites report.md/report_evidence.html and the report node when a synthesis exists (223-224), and logs todo_id 'edit:screen:<rid>' option manual_edit (226) with provenance 'manual_edit:screen' (228). Only the study page shows the impact preview (index.html:1531, 1625-1628) and only it offers 'awaiting'. Corrections: the 'rule' divergence is not reachable from the UI because todos.build lists only decision=='awaiting' records (todos.py:113), and the report the UI serves at /report is rendered live (server.py:1085-1088), so 'report freshness' concerns only the written files/report node until the next sync (sync.py:94). The core human_decision/decision/because data written by both paths is identical; the divergence is in flags closed, awaiting availability, report files, and log ids (Activity shows both ids as-is, index.html:1729).

Scenario: Path A (To-do list): the reviewer picks Include/Exclude/Need full text in the eligibility table and presses Save -> saveElig posts /api/bulk {kind:eligibility, items} (index.html:1032) -> App.mutate -> _apply_op -> todos.bulk -> todos.apply for each item (todos.py:434-452). apply writes decision/because/human_decision, upserts screen:<rid>, then resolves EVERY open flag on the node regardless of source (446-448: includes 'rerun ... pending review', 'manual_edit', 'source_changed', 'screening_unclear' flags), never calls report.write_reports, appends human_decisions.jsonl {todo_id:'elig:<rid>', option:'include'} (566) and provenance 'elig:<opt>' (572-573). Path B (study page): the reviewer clicks the Include/Exclude/Awaiting segment (index.html:1540) and 'Save (no LLM)' -> saveEdit posts /api/edit {kind:screen,...} (1642,1647) -> actions.edit (actions.py:111-134): accepts 'awaiting' (115), writes the same human_decision shape (123), moves any 'rule' into rule_restored (124-125), resolves only open flags with source screening/None/feedback whose text lacks 'pending review' (130), then at 223-224 calls report.write_reports when a synthesis exists, appends human_decisions {todo_id:'edit:screen:<rid>', option:'manual_edit'} (226) and provenance 'manual_edit:screen' (228). Only editBar on the study page renders impactLine()/api/impact (1531, 1625-1628); the To-do table has no impact preview. Activity > 'Your decisions' prints x.todo_id verbatim (index.html:1729; studies.py:393 reads human_decisions.jsonl), so the same kind of decision appears as 'elig:1003 include' or 'edit:screen:1003 manual_edit' depending on the page.

Evidence:
- `sragent/todos.py:434` — elig: branch; 437-438 include refusal; 439-445 upsert; 446-448 resolves all open flags with no source filter; 450-452 pdf
- `sragent/todos.py:566` — log_answer writes todo_id elig:<rid>; 572-573 provenance f'{tid.split(':',1)[0]}:{opt}' = elig:<opt>; no write_reports anywhere in the branch
- `sragent/todos.py:113` — build lists an elig: to-do only when decision == 'awaiting' and no human_decision, so a rule-excluded record (decision 'exclude') never reaches the To-do path: the 'rule' key divergence is not reachable from the UI
- `sragent/actions.py:111` — kind=='screen'; 115 include|exclude|awaiting; 118 include refusal; 123 human_decision; 125 rule_restored; 130 flag filter screening/None/feedback and not 'pending review'
- `sragent/actions.py:224` — report.write_reports(ctx) only if st.by_type('synthesis'); 226 human_decisions todo_id edit:screen:<rid> option manual_edit; 228 provenance manual_edit:screen
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1032` — saveElig -> /api/bulk kind eligibility; OPT at 1001 include/exclude/pdf; study page seg at 1540 include/exclude/awaiting; saveEdit -> /api/edit at 1642-1647; impactLine only in editBar (1531, 1625-1628)
- `sragent/ui/server.py:1085` — /report is rendered live by report_html(ctx) (1088): the page the UI shows is never stale; what goes stale on the To-do path is report.md / report_evidence.html and the 'report' node written by write_reports (report.py:926-933)
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1729` — Activity 'Your decisions' shows x.todo_id and x.option verbatim from human_decisions.jsonl

#### I04 · Restoring a rule exclusion has three paths with different outcomes, a misleading toast, an automatic restore attributed to the user, and rules.exclude guards weaker than rules.apply

*confirmed · medium · screening · reasons: R4, R2*

Restoring a rule exclusion happens through three code paths with different results: (1) the 'Restore' button in the 'Excluded automatically' group calls todos.bulk('restore_rule') -> rules.restore, which sets the decision back to decision_before_rule only when the record was decided at full text with a full text present and otherwise to 'forward', records rule_restored with actor='user' and no human_decision; the UI toast then says 'Restored to the eligibility table' although a 'forward' record is listed by neither the eligibility to-do (todos.py:113 requires 'awaiting') nor needed_pdfs (fulltext.py:227-234) and only shows up as 'waiting for full-text review' until the next run's fulltext_review; (2) a study-page edit on the same record (actions.py:112-131) pins include/exclude/awaiting with human_decision and rule_restored; (3) rules.apply itself calls restore for every exclusion of a rule that was switched off (rules.py:109-113), so the changelog attributes an automatic change to actor 'user' with no human_decisions.jsonl or provenance entry, and this runs from App._auto_rules on the first GET that recreates the Ctx. In addition rules.exclude (used by snowball's date check, snowball.py:52) skips only same-rule or human_decision (rules.py:136-137) while rules.apply also skips human_decision_pending_fulltext and rule_restored (rules.py:90-91), so a restored outside_date_range exclusion (whose 'rule' key was popped by restore) is re-excluded on the next snowball run, silently undoing the restore.

Scenario: (1) To-do path: reviewer clicks 'Restore' in the 'Excluded automatically' group (index.html:1148) -> restoreRule() POSTs /api/bulk {kind:'restore_rule'} (index.html:1153) -> server.mutate -> todos.bulk (todos.py:587-592) -> rules.restore (rules.py:118-129). For the common case (a title/abstract-stage rule exclusion, stage != 'full_text') full_text_decided is False, so decision becomes 'forward', because=[], rule_restored={rule, note}, no human_decision, upsert actor='user'. The UI then toasts 'Restored to the eligibility table', but todos.build lists an eligibility item only when decision == 'awaiting' (todos.py:113) and fulltext.needed_pdfs lists only fulltext_unavailable / parse_failed / awaiting+pdf_wanted (fulltext.py:227-234); a 'forward' record appears in no to-do; it is only counted as 'waiting for full-text review' in the step summary (steps.py:42) and on the Studies list (studies.py:82), and is screened by the next fulltext_review via needs_fulltext_review (screening.py:458). The toast is correct only for the rarer case stage=='full_text' with before in (include, awaiting) and a fulltext ft node. (2) Study-page path: actions.edit kind 'screen' (actions.py:112-131) sets include/exclude/awaiting, writes human_decision, and converts rule -> rule_restored={'note':'manual decision'} (actions.py:124-125): a pinned outcome, unlike path 1. (3) Automatic path: rules.apply lines 109-113 call restore(ctx, rid, note='rule X is no longer enabled') for every exclusion of a disabled rule; restore upserts with actor='user' (rules.py:129), so store._log records a person as the actor of an automatic change, with no log_answer/provenance entry (those exist only in todos.bulk). This runs from App._auto_rules (server.py:104-118) inside App.ctx() when the enabled rules or RULES_VERSION differ, i.e. on the first GET that recreates the Ctx. (4) Guard asymmetry: rules.exclude skips only same-rule or human_decision (rules.py:136-137) whereas rules.apply also skips human_decision_pending_fulltext and rule_restored (rules.py:90-91). Concrete overwrite: a snowball-added record excluded as 'outside_date_range' (snowball.py:52) is restored by the reviewer (rule popped, rule_restored set, decision 'forward'); on the next snowball run the date check at snowball.py:47-52 runs again for every meta['added'] record, the same-rule guard no longer matches (rule was popped by restore) and human_decision is absent, so rules.exclude re-excludes it, silently undoing the restore and retiring ext/rob (rules.py:144-146). The same hole applies to a record with human_decision_pending_fulltext.

Evidence:
- `sragent/rules.py:118` — restore(): decision = before only if stage=='full_text' and before in (include, awaiting) and ft kind=='fulltext', else 'forward'; because=[]; rule_restored; actor='user'; no human_decision
- `sragent/rules.py:109` — apply(): for every exclusion of a rule no longer enabled (except outside_date_range) -> restore(...) -> upsert actor='user'
- `sragent/rules.py:136` — exclude(): skips only sc.rule == rule or human_decision; apply() at 90-91 also skips human_decision_pending_fulltext and rule_restored
- `sragent/stages/snowball.py:52` — rules.exclude(ctx, r, 'outside_date_range', ...) re-run for every meta['added'] record on each snowball run
- `sragent/todos.py:113` — eligibility to-do requires decision == 'awaiting'; a 'forward' record is not listed
- `sragent/stages/fulltext.py:227` — needed_pdfs lists only fulltext_unavailable, parse_failed, awaiting+pdf_wanted
- `sragent/todos.py:587` — bulk restore_rule: rules.restore + log_answer todo_id 'rule:<rid>' + provenance human_judgment 'restore_rule_exclusion'
- `sragent/actions.py:124` — edit kind 'screen': rule -> rule_restored {'note':'manual decision'} with human_decision

#### I05 · RoB confirm/change has three write paths; the To-do 'Change' path logs twice, the legacy branch never marks dependents, a rationale-only edit counts as confirmation, and stored 'reasons' go stale

*confirmed · medium · human-loop · reasons: R4*

Confirming/changing a risk-of-bias draft has four write paths: todos.apply 'rob:confirm' (sragent/todos.py:519-523; also via /api/bulk rob_confirm from index.html:1121); 'rob:change' with extra.changes (524-528, including an empty list) which delegates to actions.edit and then calls log_answer again and returns before the common save/provenance block, so each To-do Change writes two human_decisions lines ('edit:rob:<rid>' manual_edit and 'rob:<rid>' change) and only the manual_edit provenance; a legacy single-domain branch (529-541) that sets human_confirmed without _mark_dependents and overwrites judgment_agent, which index.html never sends but tests/test_offline.py:226 and 631 still exercise; and actions.edit kind=rob from the study page (actions.py:162-198). actions.edit sets human_confirmed whenever op.confirm is not False (189-190), so a rationale-only edit (174) and a To-do 'Change' with no changed judgment both confirm the draft (the study-page button is labelled 'Save and confirm', index.html:1580; the To-do Change is not). Correction: the stale stored 'reasons' (rob.py:51; read at todos.py:272 and rob.py:87; recomputed at studies.py:279) cannot produce a To-do/study-page disagreement through these paths because each of them sets human_confirmed and the To-do card is then dropped (todos.py:262); only the feedback revision path (feedback.py:170-178) leaves a visible card with stale reasons.

Scenario: Path 1 (To-do Confirm): robConfirm posts /api/bulk {kind:rob_confirm, ids} (index.html:1121) -> todos.bulk -> apply 'rob:<rid>' confirm (todos.py:519-523): sets human_confirmed, upserts, falls through to ctx.save/log_answer/provenance (565-573). Path 2 (To-do Change): robChange builds changes = only domains whose judgment differs (index.html:1127) and posts /api/answer {todo_id:'rob:<rid>', option:'change', extra:{changes}} (1128) -> apply 524-528: extra.get('changes') is not None (an empty list also qualifies) -> actions.edit(kind=rob) which upserts, calls _mark_dependents when something changed (actions.py:197), writes human_decisions {todo_id:'edit:rob:<rid>', option:'manual_edit'} (226) and provenance 'manual_edit:rob' (228); then todos.py:527 calls log_answer(answer,'applied') writing a second human_decisions line {todo_id:'rob:<rid>', option:'change', extra:{changes}} and returns at 528 before the common ctx.save/provenance block, so no 'rob:change' provenance event is written. If the reviewer pressed Change without altering any judgment, changes=[] reaches actions.edit, nothing is changed, op.get('confirm', True) is True (189), human_confirmed is set and the draft is confirmed with the message 'confirmed'. Path 3 (legacy single-domain, todos.py:529-541, extra.domain/judgment): sets judgment_agent = current judgment (overwriting an earlier agent value), human_confirmed, no _mark_dependents, no rationale handling; index.html never sends extra.domain without changes (grep: 'domain:' appears only inside changes arrays at 1127 and 1638), but tests/test_offline.py:226 and 631 exercise it, so it is test-reachable, not dead. Path 4 (study page): editRob seeds changes for every domain with judgment and rationale (index.html:1638) and 'Save and confirm (no LLM)' (1580) posts /api/edit kind rob (1645,1647) -> actions.edit 162-198: a domain whose judgment is unchanged but whose rationale text differs passes the skip test at 174, is appended to changed with judgment unchanged, and human_confirmed is set at 189-190 - a rationale-only edit confirms the draft (on the study page the button label says so; via the To-do Change path only judgments are sent so this case cannot arise there). Stale 'reasons' sub-claim: rob.py:51 stores data['reasons'], todos.py:272 and needs_person (rob.py:87) prefer the stored list, studies.py:279 recomputes. None of the human write paths recompute it, but every one of them sets human_confirmed, after which todos.build drops the card (todos.py:262-263) and needs_person returns False (rob.py:84-85), so the claimed To-do-vs-study-page disagreement cannot be observed through these paths. The only writer that revises domains without confirming is feedback.apply_revised (feedback.py:170-178), which the issue does not cite.

Evidence:
- `sragent/todos.py:524` — opt change with extra.changes is not None (incl. []) -> actions.edit; 527 second log_answer; 528 return before ctx.save/provenance (565-573)
- `sragent/todos.py:529` — legacy extra.domain/judgment branch: judgment_agent overwritten, human_confirmed, no _mark_dependents; not sent by index.html but used by tests/test_offline.py:226,631
- `sragent/actions.py:174` — skip only when judgment equal AND rationale unchanged; a rationale-only edit is appended to changed
- `sragent/actions.py:189` — op.get('confirm', True) -> human_confirmed set, also for changes=[]; 191 'nothing changed' only when confirm is explicitly False
- `sragent/actions.py:226` — actions.edit writes human_decisions todo_id edit:rob:<rid> option manual_edit and provenance manual_edit:rob (228)
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1127` — robChange filters to changed judgments (may be empty) and posts /api/answer option change extra.changes (1128); robConfirm /api/bulk rob_confirm (1121); study page editRob 1638 sends judgment+rationale for every domain, button 'Save and confirm' (1580), /api/edit at 1645-1647
- `sragent/stages/rob.py:51` — data['reasons'] stored at draft time; todos.py:272 and needs_person (rob.py:87) read the stored value; studies.py:279 recomputes
- `sragent/todos.py:262` — card dropped once human_confirmed is set, so stale stored reasons are not visible on the To-do after any of the human write paths

#### I06 · RoB re-assessment has three triggers with three mechanisms and three model sources, and none can re-assess a confirmed draft

*confirmed · medium · human-loop · reasons: R4, R5, R6*

RoB re-assessment has three triggers with three mechanisms and three model sources: the To-do 'Ask the agent to re-assess' starts a feedback job on rob:<rid> (sragent/todos.py:542-544) whose model is the agent last saved in ui_settings.json applied as job_agent (server.py:499, 842-844, 335-337; llm.py:524-525), with FEEDBACK_VERIFY run by roles.verifier only if one is configured, otherwise the same model (feedback.py:58-60, 349), and no cost estimate; the study-page 'Re-run' starts a rerun job with the picker's model and an estimate (index.html:1651-1657) but for a human-confirmed draft rob_one returns the stored data unchanged with only a source_changed flag and no model call (rob.py:22-31), so the change set is 'no_change' (actions.py:250); 'Redraft all' (index.html:759, 1372-1381; native confirm()) runs reassess_all live with stage models, cache_read off and confirmed drafts skipped (rob.py:200-213), and is gated against agent_only only by the client (judging() at 759) since /api/run (server.py:1150) and the CLI reassess-rob (cli.py:255-257) do not call require_judgment_allowed, unlike /api/rerun and /api/propagate (1174-1176). Once a draft is confirmed the To-do card and its 'fix' option disappear (todos.py:262-263) and the source_changed flag that says 'Ask for a re-assessment' has no to-do (todos.py:193). Correction: 'none can re-assess a confirmed draft' is an over-claim - no path produces a fresh draft of a confirmed assessment, but the study-page comment box lists rob:<rid> as a target and 'Send to the agent' starts a feedback job (index.html:1606,1662; server.py:717) that feedback._revise_target runs without any human_confirmed guard (feedback.py:340-370), so a confirmed draft can be revised by the agent that way.

Scenario: Trigger 1 (To-do 'Ask the agent to re-assess'): robAgent posts /api/answer {todo_id:'rob:<rid>', option:'fix'} (index.html:1134) -> apply 542-544 returns job {action:'feedback', target:'rob:<rid>'} -> App.answer submits {**_last_models(), **job} (server.py:499); _last_models is the agent stored in ui_settings.json by the last run/pdfs/sync submit (842-844, 320-322); because 'feedback' is not in STAGE_JOBS (32) that agent becomes job_agent (335-337) and hub.agent_for returns it for every call (llm.py:521-525); the FEEDBACK_VERIFY call runs on verifier(sb) = roles.verifier if configured else that same agent (feedback.py:58-60, 349). No cost estimate is shown. Trigger 2 (study page 'Re-run' risk of bias): doRerun posts /api/rerun with ST.rr.agent (index.html:1657) after studyEst showed an estimate (1651-1654); server refuses in agent_only (1174-1176), else submits a rerun job -> actions.rerun -> rob.rob_one in a sandbox (actions.py:292). For a human-confirmed draft rob_one returns the stored data unchanged with a new source_version and a source_changed flag, with no model call (rob.py:22-31), so feedback._changed is False and the change set ends as 'no_change' (actions.py:250): the picker's model is never used. Trigger 3 ('Redraft all'): button rendered only when judging() (index.html:759, i.e. not agent_only) -> redraftRob (1372-1381) gets an estimate, uses a native confirm(), posts /api/run {action:'reassess_rob'}; /api/run (server.py:1150) has no human_mode check and submit treats reassess_rob as a STAGE_JOB (no job_agent -> stage_models); the CLI reassess-rob (cli.py:255-257, 376-378) calls reassess_all which skips human_confirmed drafts (rob.py:200-201), sets cache_read False (211-213) and upserts live via rob_one without proposals; neither reassess_all nor the CLI calls provenance.require_judgment_allowed, so the agent_only gate is client-side only. Confirmed drafts: the To-do card is dropped at todos.py:262-263 so the 'fix' option goes with it; the source_changed flag's text says 'Ask for a re-assessment' (rob.py:29-30) but todos.build makes flag: to-dos only for synthesis_verify/term_validation/evidence_check/eligibility_doubt (todos.py:193), so it has no action. Over-claim corrected: the study-page comment box lists rob:<rid> as a target (index.html:1606 from studies._study_nodes 86-87) and 'Send to the agent' submits a feedback job on rob:<rid> (server.py:717) which feedback._revise_target (feedback.py:340-370) runs with no human_confirmed guard (no such check anywhere in feedback.py); an accepted revision rewrites domains/overall while keeping human_confirmed (178). So a confirmed draft can still be revised by the agent through a feedback comment - a verifier-gated revision of the existing draft rather than a fresh draft - and that is the only such path.

Evidence:
- `sragent/todos.py:542` — opt fix -> job {action:'feedback', target:'rob:<rid>'}; card itself hidden once human_confirmed (262-263)
- `sragent/ui/server.py:499` — answer submits {**_last_models(), **job}; _last_models (842-844) = agent from ui_settings.json; 32 STAGE_JOBS excludes feedback so 335-337 sets job_agent
- `sragent/llm.py:524` — job_agent used for every call of that job; feedback.verifier (feedback.py:58-60) falls back to it unless roles.verifier is set
- `sragent/stages/rob.py:22` — rob_one on human_confirmed: upsert same data + source_version, flag source_changed (28-30), return without a model call
- `sragent/actions.py:292` — rerun step rob -> rob.rob_one(sb, rid); unchanged -> cs status no_change (250)
- `sragent/stages/rob.py:201` — reassess_all skips human_confirmed; 211-213 cache_read False; no require_judgment_allowed in reassess_all or cmd_reassess_rob (cli.py:255-257)
- `sragent/ui/server.py:1150` — /api/run unchecked for agent_only; only /api/rerun and /api/propagate refused at 1174-1176; UI hides the Redraft button via judging() at index.html:759
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1372` — redraftRob: estimate + native confirm() + /api/run reassess_rob (1376); robAgent (1134) no estimate; doRerun (1657) uses ST.rr.agent after studyEst estimate (1651-1654)

#### I12 · Data items / quality domains can be changed by three paths with three acceptance semantics; the To-do path auto-accepts a locked change with a canned reason and no decision log or mode gate

*confirmed · medium · ui-backend · reasons: R4*

Data items and quality domains can be changed by three paths with three acceptance semantics. The To-do 'fields' card (server.py:550-622) rewrites the topic file and, when config_lock.pending() reports a diff, immediately calls config_lock.accept with the fixed reason 'data items or quality domains edited in the interface' (613-614); there is no provenance.mode/require_judgment_allowed check, no human_decisions.jsonl line, and the fcSave request carries no reason (index.html:892-895), though the card text discloses 'accepted on your behalf' (883) and the change is recorded in meta.config_changes, provenance.jsonl (category config_change) and Appendix D Table D0 with that canned reason (report.py:911-915). The Configs YAML editor path pauses the run on ConfigChanged until a typed reason is accepted (index.html:2001-2010; server.py:823-836; config_lock.py:118-121 raises on an empty reason), and the CLI accept-config requires --reason and a y/N confirmation (cli.py:409-411). The card is hidden in agent_only only because todos.build drops it (todos.py:351-353); the endpoint accepts the call. data_items holds App.lock only for the idle check (557-559); the file write and accept run outside it, so a job submitted in that window is not seen (reload() skips, pending() compares the stale cfg) and later fails with ConfigChanged.

Scenario: Path 1 (To-do card): reviewer clicks 'Edit items and domains' then 'Save (no LLM)' -> fcSave() POSTs /api/data_items {op:'save', fields, domains} with no reason (index.html:892-895) -> App.data_items (server.py:550): takes self.lock only for the idle() check (557-559) and releases it; validates; rewrites the topic file via lib.save (600); sets self.stale and reload(); then Ctx.create(self.cfg, run_dir, check_config=False) (611) and, if config_lock.pending() reports a diff (613), config_lock.accept(ctx, 'data items or quality domains edited in the interface') (614). accept() records actor='user' with that fixed reason in meta.config_changes and provenance.jsonl (category config_change, not human_judgment, so never flagged as a violation) and ctx.log (config_lock.py:122-130); no log_answer/human_decisions.jsonl line and no provenance.mode / require_judgment_allowed check anywhere in data_items. Appendix D Table D0 renders e['reason'] for every change (report.py:911-915), so each such edit shows the canned reason. Path 2 (Configs YAML editor): saving a locked change makes /api/state raise ConfigChanged -> changedPanel() pauses the run; acceptConfig() refuses without a typed reason (index.html:2001-2004) and POSTs /api/config_accept; App.accept_config (823-836) -> config_lock.accept raises ValueError on an empty reason (config_lock.py:118-121). Path 3 (CLI): accept-config requires --reason (cli.py:409) and asks y/N unless --yes (cmd_accept_config). In agent_only mode todos.build drops every card but 'fulltexts' (todos.py:351-353) so the fields card is invisible, but POST /api/data_items is accepted. The UI text at index.html:883 does disclose 'accepted on your behalf and recorded'. Outside-the-lock race (narrow): if a job is submitted between the idle check and the accept, reload() skips (not idle), Ctx.create uses the stale cfg, pending() returns [], nothing is accepted, and the subprocess later fails with ConfigChanged.

Evidence:
- `sragent/ui/server.py:557` — data_items holds self.lock only for the idle() check; file rewrite (600), reload, Ctx.create(check_config=False) (611) and config_lock.accept with the fixed reason (613-614) run outside it; no mode gate, no log_answer
- `sragent/config_lock.py:118` — accept(): reason required (ValueError), records actor='user' + reason in meta.config_changes, provenance 'config_change'/'accepted', ctx.log
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:883` — fieldsCard text: 'written to the topic file, accepted on your behalf and recorded'; fcSave (892-895) sends no reason
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:2001` — acceptConfig(): 'Write a reason first' guard, POST /api/config_accept?config=...
- `sragent/ui/server.py:823` — accept_config: idle check, Ctx.create(check_config=False), config_lock.accept(ctx, reason) -> ValueError on empty reason returned as ok:false
- `sragent/cli.py:409` — accept-config --reason required; --yes to skip the y/N prompt in cmd_accept_config
- `sragent/todos.py:351` — build(): in agent_only every card but 'fulltexts' is dropped, so the fields card is hidden client-side only; /api/data_items has no gate
- `sragent/stages/report.py:911` — Appendix D Table D0 shows e['reason'] for each accepted change -> the canned reason appears for every interface edit

#### I14 · 'Process added PDFs' is reachable from two places with different preconditions (no estimate, no save of the dirty stage table, agent possibly null)

*confirmed · medium · ui-frontend · reasons: R7, R4*

The 'pdfs' job is submitted from two front-ends with different preconditions: processPdfs (index.html:1382-1390; buttons at 1064 in pdfPanel, which is shown on #/pdfs at 1365 and in the To-do 'Full texts' group at 754, and at 1369) posts /api/run {action:'pdfs', agent: runOpts.agent || M.settings.agent, extra_budget} with no estimate and without saving an unsaved Models-per-stage table (ensureModels at 1393 sets M only; runOpts.agent is set only in loadModels at 1190, so from the Full-texts page it is null unless the Run page was visited). The Run page's 'Process added PDFs' card (1231) goes through startRun (1335-1343), which saves a dirty stage table, shows the estimate with the unsaved table, and sends the full runOpts. Both reach App.submit (server.py:315-325), which fills a missing agent from ui_settings.json (_last_models, 842-844) and rewrites ui_settings.json with the agent (and null for until/suite/max_records/judge when absent) although the pdfs job never uses the agent (_write_job_config, 335: job_agent only for non-STAGE_JOBS). A null agent is harmless (skipped by _last_models and loadModels). The practical divergences are: no cost estimate, unsaved stage-model edits silently ignored (they are also discarded on the next Run page visit, 1193), and max_records nulled in ui_settings.json.

Scenario: (1) Reviewer edits the Models-per-stage table on the Run page (smSet, index.html:1294-1300 sets SM.dirty=true) but does not press 'Save to run file'. (2) Reviewer opens #/pdfs (or the To-do 'Full texts' group) and clicks 'Process added PDFs' (button at index.html:1064 inside pdfPanel, rendered on #/pdfs at 1365 and in the To-do group at 754; a second 'Process N added files' button at 1369). (3) processPdfs (1382-1390) runs: `await ensureModels()` (1393: sets M only, never runOpts.agent), then POSTs /api/run {action:'pdfs', agent: runOpts.agent || M.settings.agent, extra_budget: runOpts.extra_budget}. No /api/estimate call, no saveStageModels: the job runs with the stage_models already in the run file; the unsaved edits are ignored (and are discarded when the Run page is next activated, because activate('run') -> loadModels resets SM.edit/SM.dirty at 1193). (4) From the Run page, the same job is the 'Process added PDFs' option card (1231) and goes through startRun (1335-1343): saves a dirty table first (`if (STAGE_JOB(runOpts.action) && SM.dirty && !(await saveStageModels())) return;`), shows the estimate (estimate() at 1307-1324 includes the unsaved stage_models), and sends the full runOpts (until, suite, max_records, etc.). (5) Server App.submit (server.py:315-325): if spec has no agent it fills agent/extra_budget from _last_models() (842-844, read from ui_settings.json), then writes ui_settings.json for run/pdfs/sync/eval with {agent, judge, max_records, until, extra_budget, suite} using spec.get(k) -> keys absent from the processPdfs spec (until, suite, max_records, judge) are written as null. _write_job_config (335) sets job_agent only when action is not in STAGE_JOBS, so the pdfs job never uses the agent it was given. The written agent is later read by loadModels (index.html:1190, prefers st.agent over the config default), defaultAgent (1396) and _last_models for jobs created from to-do answers (server.py:499, 518). Over-claims corrected: 'agent possibly null' is harmless (when runOpts.agent and settings.agent are both absent the server writes agent:null, which _last_models skips via `if s.get(k)` and loadModels falls back to M.current.agent); the agent sent by the two paths converges on settings.agent except when ui_settings.json has no agent yet or the reviewer changed the agent under the Evaluation action (the Agent model select is hidden for run/pdfs/sync at 1247, so only eval can change runOpts.agent). The 'silently set the default model of later agent jobs' effect is shared by both paths, not a difference between them.

Evidence:
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1382` — processPdfs: ensureModels(); /api/run {action:'pdfs', agent: runOpts.agent || st.agent, extra_budget}; no estimate, no saveStageModels
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1393` — ensureModels sets M only; runOpts.agent is set only in loadModels (1190)
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1335` — startRun: saveStageModels if STAGE_JOB && SM.dirty, then /api/run with full runOpts
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1231` — Run page 'Process added PDFs' option card -> same action 'pdfs' via startRun
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1064` — pdfPanel 'Process added PDFs' button -> processPdfs; pdfPanel rendered at 754 (To-do) and 1365 (#/pdfs); second button at 1369
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1193` — loadModels resets SM.edit from M.stages and SM.dirty=false on every Run page activation (550)
- `sragent/ui/server.py:315` — submit: fills agent/extra_budget from _last_models() when agent falsy; writes ui_settings.json for run/pdfs/sync/eval with spec.get(k) (absent keys -> null)
- `sragent/ui/server.py:335` — _write_job_config: job_agent only when action not in STAGE_JOBS; pdfs ignores the agent

#### I15 · 'Update now' from the To-do and 'Update outputs' from the Run page differ in endpoint, logging, estimate, gating and provenance

*confirmed · medium · human-loop · reasons: R4*

'Update now' on the To-do card posts /api/answer {todo_id:'sync', option:'sync'} (index.html:750-751 via answerNow at 1169) -> todos.apply (todos.py:559-561), which writes a human_decisions line (566) but no provenance because _category('sync') returns None (392-397), so require_judgment_allowed is also skipped (409-414); the job then gets _last_models merged (server.py:499). The Run page card posts /api/run {action:'sync', ...runOpts} (index.html:1229-1232, 1340) after showing an estimate, with no human_decisions line and no gate either (server.submit 314-324); the CLI `sync` logs nothing (sync.py:88-98). The difference is thus endpoint, decision log, visible estimate (the To-do question carries the cost but the card renders only title and context; the Home page shows it from stale.est_usd) and model source; neither path is gated by human_mode (in agent_only only the To-do card disappears, todos.py:352-354, while the Run page card stays). state() hides the sync to-do only while a sync/pdfs job runs (server.py:853-854).

Scenario: Path A: the To-do card for kind 'sync' (index.html:746-752) renders sy.title and sy.context (not sy.question, which carries the '(about $x)' estimate built at todos.py:338-342) with buttons answerNow('sync','sync') / answerNow('sync','later') (750-751) -> POST /api/answer (1169-1171) -> server.answer (489-499): if a job runs, the answer is queued and logged 'queued'; else todos.apply: _category('sync', 'sync') -> None (392-397) so require_judgment_allowed is skipped (409-414) and no provenance line is written (568-573); branch at 559-561 returns job {action:'sync'}; log_answer writes a human_decisions line (566); answer() then submit({**_last_models(), **job}) (499). Path B: Run page optcard 'Update outputs after my decisions' (index.html:1229-1232) -> startRun -> POST /api/run {...runOpts} (1340) -> server.submit (314-324): no human_mode check, no human_decisions line, no provenance; the page shows /api/estimate action 'sync' (254-258) first. Both end as `sragent sync --config` (_cmd 357-358) -> sync.sync_dependents (sync.py:88-98), which logs nothing (grep: no provenance/log_answer in sync.py or cmd_sync). Neither path checks human_mode: in agent_only the To-do card is absent because todos.build keeps only 'fulltexts' (352-354) while the Run page card is unconditional (1229-1232). state() (853-854) hides the sync to-do only while a sync/pdfs job runs. Home page staleBox (703) does show stale.est_usd parsed from the sync to-do question (860-863).

Evidence:
- `sragent/todos.py:392` — _category: 'sync' -> None ('starts the agent; no human judgment about content'); gate at 409-414 and provenance at 568-573 skipped
- `sragent/todos.py:559` — tid 'sync' option 'sync' -> job {action:'sync'}; log_answer at 566 writes the human_decisions line
- `sragent/todos.py:338` — sync to-do question carries '(about $x)' estimate; the UI card does not render t.question
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:746` — sync card: title + context + answerNow('sync','sync') / 'later' (750-751); no estimate shown
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1229` — Run page optcard 'sync' shown unconditionally; startRun posts runOpts to /api/run (1340)
- `sragent/ui/server.py:489` — answer(): queue while job runs, else todos.apply then submit({**_last_models(), **job})
- `sragent/ui/server.py:314` — submit(): no human_mode gate, no human_decisions/provenance; _last_models merged when no agent given
- `sragent/ui/server.py:853` — state(): sync to-do dropped only while self.job.action in ('sync','pdfs')

#### I16 · Proposal accept/reject: the CLI differs from the UI in provenance target, decision log and StaleProposal handling; the UI has two payload shapes

*confirmed · medium · cli-eval · reasons: R4*

Proposal accept/reject has two implementations with different audit trails. todos.apply (`cs:` branch, sragent/todos.py:545-556) treats every option except 'accept' (and 'later', snoozed at 416-420) as a reject with the note, catches feedback.StaleProposal and logs it as refused_stale, and on success writes a human_decisions line plus a provenance record with target 'cs:<id>' (570-572). cmd_accept/cmd_reject (sragent/cli.py:208-225) write the provenance record with the bare id as target, write no human_decisions line, and cmd_accept records 'cs:accept' before calling feedback.accept, so a stale proposal leaves a provenance record for an accept that applied nothing and the StaleProposal raised at feedback.py:719-720 surfaces as a traceback (cli.main at 414-420 catches only ConfigChanged). In the UI the To-do card posts text/extra via submitTodo (index.html:1175-1180) while the study page (1600) and Activity (1723) call decideCs (1666-1670), which posts {todo_id, option} with no note. An accept queued behind a running job (server.py:491-495) that turns out stale is refused only as a refused_stale line in human_decisions.jsonl because _drain_answers (502-518) discards the result's ok/message.

Scenario: UI path: reviewer clicks Accept on a proposal card in the To-do list -> submitTodo (index.html:1175-1180) posts {todo_id:'cs:FB..', option:'accept', text, extra} -> /api/answer -> App.answer (server.py:488-500) -> todos.apply. apply reaches the `cs:` branch (todos.py:545-556): accept -> feedback.accept inside try/except StaleProposal; on StaleProposal it calls log_answer(..., 'refused_stale') and returns {ok:False, message} BEFORE any provenance line; on success it falls through to ctx.save(), log_answer('applied') and provenance.record('human_judgment','cs:accept', target='cs:FB..', rid=None) (todos.py:570-572). Any option other than 'accept' (and other than 'later', which todos.py:416-420 snoozes) goes to feedback.reject(ctx, cid, text). The study page (index.html:1600) and the Activity tab (1723) instead call decideCs (1666-1670), which posts bare {todo_id:'cs:'+id, option} so no note reaches feedback.reject. If a job is running, App.answer queues the answer and returns 'Saved. A job is running...' (server.py:491-495); _drain_answers (502-518) later calls todos.apply and discards r['ok'], so a stale accept is refused with only a 'refused_stale' line in human_decisions.jsonl and no message to the person. CLI path: `sragent accept --id FB..` -> cmd_accept (cli.py:208-216) calls provenance.record('human_judgment','cs:accept', target=args.id) (bare id, no 'cs:' prefix) BEFORE feedback.accept, never calls todos.log_answer, and does not catch StaleProposal; feedback.accept (feedback.py:706-725) marks the change set stale and raises StaleProposal at 719-720; cli.main (414-420) catches only ConfigChanged, so the exception prints as a traceback, while the provenance log already holds a 'cs:accept' record for an accept that applied nothing. cmd_reject (219-225) likewise writes target=args.id and no human_decisions line.

Evidence:
- `sragent/todos.py:545` — `cs:` branch: accept -> try feedback.accept except StaleProposal -> log_answer refused_stale, return ok False (546-551); else -> feedback.reject(ctx, cid, text) (553-554)
- `sragent/todos.py:571` — provenance.record('human_judgment', f'cs:{opt}', target=tid) where tid='cs:<id>'; reached only after a successful apply
- `sragent/todos.py:416` — opt=='later' is handled before the cs: branch (snooze), so 'any other option' means any option except accept/later
- `sragent/cli.py:208` — cmd_accept: provenance.record(... 'cs:accept', target=args.id) at 213 precedes feedback.accept at 214; no log_answer; StaleProposal uncaught
- `sragent/cli.py:219` — cmd_reject: provenance target=args.id (223); feedback.reject(ctx, args.id, args.note or '') (224); no log_answer
- `sragent/cli.py:414` — main() catches only ConfigChanged, so StaleProposal from cmd_accept is a traceback
- `sragent/feedback.py:706` — accept(): base_versions compared at 710; cs marked stale, flag resolved, StaleProposal raised at 719-720
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1175` — submitTodo posts {todo_id, option, text, extra}

#### I18 · Comments 'sent to the agent' have three forms with three model sources; the HTML report's form carries no run selector; the job is submitted before the comment exists; no decision log; no UI confirmation

*confirmed · medium · ui-backend · reasons: R4, R8*

Comments 'sent to the agent' have three forms with three model sources: the studies-list form sends ST.agent (index.html:1461), the study-page form sends ST.rr.agent || defaultAgent() (1662), and the HTML report's fb() posts to the relative URL /api/comment with neither agent nor ?config= (report.py:326). Because _app() resolves a missing config to the --config default run, then to the single open App, else raises LookupError -> HTTP 500 (server.py:1037-1042, 1195-1196), a comment from run B's report lands in run A (and its feedback job runs and is charged there) whenever the UI was started with --config A, and fails with a raw error when two runs are open without a default. App.comment submits the feedback job (717-721) before mutate() stores the comment (722); if the worker starts the job first, the comment is queued in pending_answers and written only after the job ends (the job itself is unaffected, it receives --text). actions.add_comment writes meta.comments, a changelog entry and a provenance record but no human_decisions.jsonl line (actions.py:31-44), so the Activity 'Your decisions' tab (index.html:1714) lists a comment only when it happened to be queued (server.py:544). reviewComment shows an inline message with no toast and no refresh (1459-1463).

Scenario: Three forms: (a) studies list reviewComment() sends agent: ST.agent (index.html:1461; picker at 1450); (b) study page studyComment() sends agent: ST.rr.agent || defaultAgent() (1662; picker at 1525; defaultAgent at 1394 = runOpts.agent || settings.agent || current agent at effort 'low'); (c) the HTML report's fb() does fetch('/api/comment', {target, text, send}) with no agent and no query string (report.py:326). Wrong-run scenario: UI started with --config A; reviewer opens run B on the Configs page and clicks Report -> U('/report') = /report?config=B (index.html:484, 607). In that page fb() posts to the relative URL /api/comment (query dropped) -> do_POST -> _app(parse_qs('')) -> ws.get(None) -> _path(None) returns Path(self.default) = A (server.py:1037-1042, Workspace._path) -> the comment (and, with send, the feedback job and its cost) go to run A. Without --config and two runs open, ws.get(None) is None and len(ws.apps)==2 -> LookupError -> HTTP 500 (1195-1196); fb() prints j.error ('no run file is open...') because its catch branch only handles fetch exceptions. Ordering: App.comment (704) with send submits the feedback job first (717-721: submit() appends to self.queue under the lock) and only then calls mutate() (722); the worker loop (437-444, 0.5 s poll) can set self.job before mutate() takes the lock, in which case mutate sees self.job is not None and the comment goes to pending_answers with todo_id 'op:comment' (543-545), to be written after its own job finishes; the job still runs with --text so the agent is not affected, and r['message'] is overwritten with 'Sent to the agent (job ...)' (723-725). Logging: actions.add_comment (actions.py:31-44) writes meta.comments, a changelog entry via st._log and a provenance record (annotation/comment or human_judgment/feedback) but no human_decisions.jsonl line; the Activity 'Your decisions' tab reads human_decisions.jsonl (index.html:1714; studies.py:393), so a comment is listed there only when it was queued and log_answer ran (server.py:544). UI: reviewComment shows msg('revcm', r) inline, no toast, no refresh() (1459-1463); studyComment toasts and re-opens the study (1660-1664).

Evidence:
- `sragent/stages/report.py:326` — fb(): fetch('/api/comment', {target:id, text:t, send}) - relative URL, no agent, no ?config=
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:484` — U(path) appends ?config=CFG; the Report link (607, 662) opens /report?config=X but fetches inside that page drop the query
- `sragent/ui/server.py:1037` — _app: ws.get(None) -> Workspace._path(None) -> the --config default; else the single open App; else LookupError -> 500 at 1195-1196
- `sragent/ui/server.py:717` — comment(): self.submit(feedback spec) at 717-721, then self.mutate({_op:'comment', job_id}) at 722; worker (437-444) may start the job in between -> comment queued behind it
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1461` — reviewComment agent: ST.agent (picker 1450); studyComment 1662 agent: ST.rr.agent || defaultAgent() (picker 1525, defaultAgent 1394)
- `sragent/actions.py:31` — add_comment: meta.comments + st._log + provenance.record; no human_decisions.jsonl line
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1714` — 'Your decisions' tab is H.decisions = human_decisions.jsonl (studies.py:393); a comment appears only if mutate() queued it (server.py:544)

#### I29 · 'ext/rob status follows the eligibility decision' is implemented in seven places; the two manual decision paths do none, while GUIDE says exclusion removes the study from RoB immediately

*not yet verified · medium · graph-engine · reasons: R5, R2*

engine._statuses (sragent/engine.py:352-361), screening.rescreen_rule_only (576-582, actor user), rules.exclude (rules.py:144-146, actor rule), feedback._propagate (386-392), feedback._include_study (579-582), extraction.recheck_eligibility (280-284) and screening.verify_existing (418-421) each set ext/rob status from inclusion with different actors and reasons. actions.edit screen and todos.apply elig change the decision without touching ext/rob status (actions.py:133 'follow via Update outputs'; todos.py:449). GUIDE.md:296 says 'excluding a study removes it from the PMID list and from risk of bias immediately'; studies_list still shows rob_overall from the active rob node (studies.py:123) until 'Update now'.

- `sragent/todos.py:439` — elig exclude: screen upsert only; no set_status
