- `sragent/engine.py:135` — considered = meta or empty drives 'integrate'; scope filter at 137-138
- `sragent/feedback.py:75` — sandbox_meta = every differing meta key; copied by commit at 695-696
- `sragent/sync.py:65` — pending_changes fallback differs from engine.plan

#### I03 · Study-page 'awaiting' writes a human_decision that removes the record from the eligibility to-do and from PDF re-screening with no way back

*confirmed · medium · human-loop · reasons: R4, R2*

actions.edit kind=screen accepts decision 'awaiting' and writes human_decision={decision:'awaiting'} (sragent/actions.py:115,123). With that mark set, todos.build omits the record from the eligibility table (sragent/todos.py:113), sync.process_pdfs does not re-screen it when its PDF arrives (sragent/sync.py:25-26), needs_fulltext_review returns False (screening.py:455-456), rules.apply skips it (rules.py:90-91) and graph.human_pinned pins it (graph.py:49,104-107), while sync_awaiting_flags keeps raising a 'Please decide' flag on it (screening.py:534-537). The To-do path never offers 'awaiting' (todos.py:161-163; index.html:1001), so the two paths disagree on whether a person can mark a study undecided, and the study-page version silently removes the study from the automatic queue. Correction: it is not 'forever' or 'no way back' - the study page can later set include/exclude, and its Re-run screening (actions.py:288, keep_human=False) proposes a replacement. human_decision_pending_fulltext (screening.py:45,474,238-246; rules.py:90; graph.py:49) is a deliberate hold-and-restore state for an include decision whose full text failed to fetch, not a near-dead variant, though it duplicates the human-mark concept.

Scenario: On the study page the reviewer opens the eligibility edit, clicks 'Awaiting' in the segment (index.html:1540) and 'Save (no LLM)' -> /api/edit {kind:screen, decision:'awaiting'} (1642,1647) -> actions.edit accepts it (actions.py:115) and writes d.decision='awaiting' plus human_decision={decision:'awaiting',...} (123), then resolves the open 'Awaiting classification ... Please decide' screening flag with 'human decided: awaiting' (130). Resulting state: todos.build skips the record because d.get('human_decision') is truthy (todos.py:113), so it leaves the eligibility table; when the reviewer later drops <rid>.pdf and runs 'Process added PDFs', sync.process_pdfs sees human truthy (sync.py:25-26) and only converts the file (fetch_one) instead of review_one; needs_fulltext_review returns False (screening.py:455-456); rules.apply skips it (rules.py:90-91); graph.human_pinned returns True via HUMAN_MARKS (graph.py:49,104-107) so the update engine treats it as pinned; sync_awaiting_flags (screening.py:534-537) re-raises an 'Awaiting classification ... Please decide' flag on the next sync because it checks only dec=='awaiting', while the To-do list still does not list it. The To-do path never offers 'awaiting' (todos.py:161-163 and index.html:1001 offer include/exclude/pdf). Over-claim corrected: 'no way back' is too strong. The same study-page edit can later set include/exclude (actions.edit overwrites human_decision), and the study-page 'Ask the agent > Re-run screening' (index.html:1657 -> /api/rerun -> actions.rerun) calls screen_one with keep_human=False (actions.py:288; screening.py:197-199) and proposes a fresh agent decision with the note 'this replaces your earlier manual decision' (actions.py:298). What is lost is only the automatic re-screen on PDF arrival and the To-do listing. human_decision_pending_fulltext is not a near-dead variant: _mark_retrieval parks an include decision there when retrieval fails (screening.py:474) and screen_one restores it when the full text arrives (238-239, 245-246); rules.py:90 and graph.py:49 honour it in the meantime. It is a deliberate hold state with one writer and one restorer, though it does duplicate the human-mark concept.

Evidence:
- `sragent/actions.py:115` — dec in ('include','exclude','awaiting'); 123 human_decision written for awaiting too; 130 resolves the open 'Awaiting classification' screening flag
- `sragent/todos.py:113` — eligibility to-do skipped when human_decision is set, whatever its value
- `sragent/sync.py:25` — human = sc['data'].get('human_decision'); 26: only non-human, non-rule, non-exclude records are re-screened when their PDF arrives
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:455` — needs_fulltext_review False for any human_decision
- `sragent/graph.py:104` — human_pinned: any HUMAN_MARKS key (49) pins the node for the update engine
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:534` — sync_awaiting_flags re-flags dec=='awaiting' 'Please decide' without checking human_decision, so the flag reappears while the To-do list omits the record
- `sragent/actions.py:288` — study-page Re-run screening uses keep_human=False and proposes a replacement (298): a way back exists on the study page
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:474` — human_decision_pending_fulltext written by _mark_retrieval and restored at 238-239 / carried at 245-246: a live round-trip, not dead

#### I09 · meta.protocol_approved is set by one path, reset by one of four edit paths, and gates nothing in the pipeline

*confirmed · medium · pipeline · reasons: R2*

meta.protocol_approved is set only by the To-do approve answer (sragent/todos.py:423) and popped only by planning.apply_protocol (planning.py:109, i.e. the UI revise/edit paths). Two other protocol-changing paths re-version the protocol node without clearing it: `sragent rescreen` (cli.py:69-80) and planning.plan re-adopting a hand-edited protocol.yaml on the next run (planning.py:23-32, 51-55); the To-do 'edited' answer (todos.py:425-433) is a third but is hidden by the UI once approved (todos.py:69, index.html:804), so it only matters via the raw API. Nothing gates on the flag: pipeline.run never reads it, /api/run has no check, the Run page only warns via the positional S.steps[0].status==='needs you' (index.html:1223), and next_action (todos.py:655-656) merely asks for approval first. Dependent screening decisions are still re-derived correctly by pipeline._screen; the defect is that the card can say 'Protocol (approved)' and steps.py:33 can say 'approved' over criteria nobody approved, contradicting the todos.py:55-56 promise that a change asks for approval again.

Scenario: 1. In the To-do list the reviewer clicks 'Yes, approve' on the protocol card. server.answer (sragent/ui/server.py:488-497) calls todos.apply; the branch at sragent/todos.py:421-424 writes st.meta['protocol_approved'] = {ts, note}. 2. The reviewer hand-edits <run_dir>/protocol.yaml (changes a criterion text) and runs `sragent run` (or the Run page 'Continue the review'). pipeline.run (sragent/pipeline.py:135-150) calls _plan -> planning.plan: no topic.protocol_file, so the `elif pfile.exists()` branch (sragent/stages/planning.py:23-32) loads the edited file, records provenance 'protocol_edited', then lines 51-55 upsert the protocol node (reason 'protocol updated') and sync_criteria versions the changed crit: node. Nothing pops meta['protocol_approved'] on this path (the only pop is planning.apply_protocol line 109, reached from revise_protocol/edit_criteria via /api/protocol). 3. pipeline._screen (pipeline.py:23-32) runs engine.plan/execute and re-derives the stale screening decisions correctly, but the approval flag survives. 4. On the next UI refresh todos.build (todos.py:57-73) computes approved=True, renders the card as 'Protocol (approved)' over the edited criteria with no approve option, and steps.py:33 reports the plan step as 'approved'. The same happens with `sragent rescreen` (sragent/cli.py:69-80: upsert + sync_criteria + rescreen_rule_only, no pop). 5. pipeline.run never reads protocol_approved (grep: readers are only todos.py:57 and steps.py:33); /api/run -> App.submit (server.py:1150-1151, 314-325) has no approval check; the Run page only shows a warning built from S.steps[0].status==='needs you' (index.html:1223, positional on todos.STEPS[0]=='plan') and explicitly says 'You can still run'. The To-do 'edited' answer (todos.py:425-433) also does not pop the flag, but the UI offers that option only while unapproved (todos.py:69-72, index.html:804), so it leaves a stale flag only when driven through /api/answer directly.

Evidence:
- `sragent/todos.py:423` — approve answer sets st.meta['protocol_approved']
- `sragent/todos.py:69` — options=[] once approved: the 'edited' re-apply is UI-hidden after approval (also index.html:804)
- `sragent/todos.py:430` — 'edited' path: upsert + sync_criteria + rescreen_rule_only, no pop of protocol_approved
- `sragent/stages/planning.py:109` — the only place protocol_approved is popped (apply_protocol, used by revise_protocol 131 and edit_criteria 166)
- `sragent/stages/planning.py:23` — plan() reloads a hand-edited protocol.yaml and upserts it at 51-54 without touching the approval flag
- `sragent/cli.py:78` — cmd_rescreen upserts the protocol node (actor user) and syncs criteria; no pop
- `sragent/pipeline.py:135` — run(): no reference to protocol_approved anywhere in pipeline.py
- `sragent/ui/server.py:1150` — /api/run -> App.submit (314-325): no approval gate

#### I11 · Search-query confirmation is the only human judgment with no decision log, no provenance and no human_mode gate; it is refused instead of queued, silently cleared, and confirm_search+agent_only blocks a run with no to-do

*confirmed · medium · human-loop · reasons: R4, R2*

/api/search_strategy writes meta.search_strategy directly and saves (sragent/ui/server.py:655-702) with no provenance.record, no todos.log_answer and no require_judgment_allowed in the handler or in search.py; it returns ok:false while a job runs (661-662) instead of queueing as /api/answer, /api/bulk and /api/edit do (489-495, 538-545), and refuses once the search node exists (665-666). search.confirm_query hard-codes by:'user' (search.py:151). In agent_only the card is absent only because todos.build omits it (todos.py:76-77, 352-354) while the endpoint, including the LLM ops suggest/refine, stays open; pipeline._waiting_for_search (pipeline.py:104-111) checks only confirm_search and meta, so a hand-written run file with confirm_search: true and human_mode: agent_only (the New-review form never writes that combination, configs.py:464-465, and config.py:179-181 does not reject it) stops the run before the search with nothing to answer in the UI. 'Change it' (ssClear, index.html:980) is unlogged and its success message is overwritten by the re-render (ssCall toasts only on failure). The only persistent trace of a confirmation is the search node's query_source/confirmed fields written when the search runs (search.py:155-160); configs/README.md:159 nevertheless states that all human input is logged in provenance.jsonl.

Scenario: Reviewer clicks 'Confirm this query' (index.html:935) -> ssConfirm -> ssCall -> POST /api/search_strategy {op:'confirm'} -> server.search_strategy (655-702): inside the lock, if self.job is not None returns ok:false 'A job is running; choose the search strategy when it has finished.' (661-662) instead of appending to pending_answers as answer()/mutate() do (489-495, 538-545); if st.get('search') is not None returns ok:false (665-666); otherwise search.confirm_query (search.py:141-153) writes meta.search_strategy.confirmed with hard-coded by:'user' and only ctx.log; handler ends with ctx.save() (700). Neither the handler nor search.py calls provenance.record, todos.log_answer or require_judgment_allowed (grep: no 'provenance' in search.py; no human_mode check in search_strategy), so the ops suggest/refine (LLM calls) and confirm are all accepted in agent_only. Clicking 'Change it' (index.html:918) -> ssClear (980) -> ssCall({op:'clear'}, '') -> handler sets confirmed=None; ssCall toasts only when !r.ok (959) and writes r.message into #m-search (958) just before refresh+renderTodo re-renders the card, so the clear is effectively silent and unlogged. Agent-only run with pipeline.confirm_search: true (not produced by the New-review form, configs.py:464-465, and not rejected by config.py validation 179-181): todos.build omits the search to-do (todos.py:76-77) and keeps only 'fulltexts' (352-354); next_action says 'run the review' (638-645); pipeline.run -> _waiting_for_search (104-111) checks only confirm_search and meta.confirmed and stops before the search with a log line pointing at a To-do card that does not exist. configs/README.md:159 states all human input is logged in provenance.jsonl. The one trace that does exist: once the search runs, search.search stores query_source 'confirmed by the review team' and confirmed {variant,note,by,ts} in the search node (search.py:155-160).

Evidence:
- `sragent/ui/server.py:655` — search_strategy: ok:false while a job runs (661-662); ok:false once the search node exists (665-666); ctx.save() at 700; no provenance.record, no log_answer, no require_judgment_allowed/human_mode check
- `sragent/ui/server.py:489` — answer(): queues to pending_answers and log_answer(..., 'queued') while a job runs; mutate() (538-545) does the same for /api/bulk and /api/edit
- `sragent/stages/search.py:141` — confirm_query: meta.confirmed = {..., 'by': 'user', 'ts': ...} (151) and ctx.log only; no provenance import anywhere in search.py
- `sragent/todos.py:76` — search to-do built only when mode != agent_only; 352-354 keep only 'fulltexts' in agent_only
- `sragent/pipeline.py:104` — _waiting_for_search: returns True on confirm_search and no confirmed query, regardless of human_mode
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:980` — ssClear -> ssCall({op:'clear'}, ''); ssCall (954-962) toasts only on !r.ok, writes message to #m-search then re-renders
- `sragent/ui/configs.py:464` — New-review form writes confirm_search: true only when mode != agent_only; config.py:179-181 validates only fulltext_unclear for agent_only
- `configs/README.md:159` — 'All human input is logged in provenance.jsonl.'

#### I31 · No 'clear this stage' exists; `--fresh` wipes the store but replays the shared LLM cache and leaves protocol.yaml, proposals, PDFs, usage, settings and job files that are re-read as live state

*confirmed · medium · pipeline · reasons: R2, R5*

No function removes a node, a node type, a meta key or a stage's files (grep: the only unlink/rmdir calls in sragent/ are for temp files). Ctx.create(fresh=True) builds an empty in-memory store (context.py:59) and the first save rewrites store_changelog.jsonl from a _flushed=0 store (store.py:234-240), keeping only store.json.bak. Everything else in run_dir survives and is re-read: protocol.yaml (re-adopted by plan(), so PLAN is not asked), fulltext/auto and .parsed (caches), needed_pdfs.csv, feedback/*.json (a 'proposed' one reappears as a cs: to-do, todos.py:286-321; Accept marks it 'stale' once and it then disappears, feedback.py:713-721), human_decisions.jsonl and provenance.jsonl (append-only logs; provenance does not hide PDFs because new_local_files also checks the ft node, fulltext.py:281), usage.jsonl (the budget is cumulative per run directory by design, llm.py:74-80, so a --fresh run starts with the old spending against budget_usd), ui_settings.json (_last_models source), ui_job_config.yaml (overwritten per job, so recorded cmds are not reproducible, server.py:346-354), ui_jobs/jobs.jsonl, report.md ('review done' on Configs, configs.py:28-35). eval_private/gold.json is a benchmark fixture rather than run state. The LLM disk cache (llm.py:500) is shared by every run and `sragent run` has no cache flag (cli.py:295-300); only rob.reassess_all, eval and the per-study actions.rerun bypass it, so `run --fresh` replays SEARCH/SCREEN/... answers verbatim - the documented intent of GUIDE.md:752, which leaves no way to re-run any stage but rob with fresh model calls short of deleting the sqlite file.

Scenario: 1. The reviewer runs `sragent run --fresh --config C` (cli.py:299 help: 'ignore an existing store.json in run_dir'; GUIDE.md:752 presents it as 'start a review over ... the cache keeps it cheap'). cmd_run (cli.py:62-65) -> Ctx.create(fresh=True) (context.py:48-70): line 59 builds an empty ReviewStore instead of loading store.json; nothing in sragent/ deletes anything in run_dir (grep rmtree/unlink/os.remove: only temp files in store.py:42, configs.py:202/293/536, local.py:125, docparse.py:154). 2. First ctx.save() -> store.save (store.py:226-240): store.json is written atomically with backup=True (store.json.bak kept); the changelog has _flushed=0 so the else branch (239) overwrites store_changelog.jsonl with the fresh store's entries only (no backup for the changelog). 3. Everything else in run_dir stays and is re-read: protocol.yaml is re-adopted by planning.plan (planning.py:23-25) so PLAN is not even asked; fulltext/auto (fulltext.py:67,97) and fulltext/.parsed (docparse.py:44) are reused as caches; needed_pdfs.csv (fulltext.py:247) stays until rewritten; feedback/*.json with status 'proposed' are listed as cs: to-dos by todos.build (todos.py:286-321) - on Accept, feedback.accept (feedback.py:706-721) finds base_versions missing in the new store, sets status 'stale' and raises StaleProposal, so the item is refused once and then disappears (not forever); human_decisions.jsonl is an append-only log (todos.py:385-387, shown via studies.py:393); provenance.jsonl survives (provenance.py:40) but does not hide processed PDFs because new_local_files also compares the ft: node's local_file (fulltext.py:281), so with an empty store every local PDF is listed as new; usage.jsonl is re-read by CostTracker (llm.py:72-80, comment: 'cumulative per run directory') so the fresh run starts with spent = all earlier spending counted against budget_usd (tracker.check at llm.py:83-86 before every uncached call) and server.spent() (server.py:300-311) shows it; ui_settings.json feeds _last_models (server.py:221-223, 842-844); ui_job_config.yaml is rewritten per job (server.py:346-347) while the recorded cmd (354-360) points at that one path; ui_jobs/jobs.jsonl keeps the old history (57); report.md makes configs.run_status say 'review done' (configs.py:28-35) for the fresh store; eval_private/gold.json (gold.py:15) is a benchmark fixture, not run state. 4. LLM calls: LLMHub builds one DiskCache at cache_dir/llm_cache.sqlite shared by every run (llm.py:500), cache_read defaults True (502, 517) and LLM.chat returns a hit before any API call (266-275) with usd 0.0. `sragent run` has no cache flag (cli.py:295-300); cache reads are disabled only by rob.reassess_all (rob.py:211-213), eval run_all.py:84, and actions.rerun (which sets llm.cache=None, actions.py:281-283). So `run --fresh` returns the cached SEARCH/SCREEN/... answers verbatim - the documented, intended behaviour (GUIDE.md:752), which means there is no way to get a genuinely new model answer for any stage but rob without deleting the sqlite file by hand.

Evidence:
- `sragent/context.py:59` — fresh=True only skips ReviewStore.load; run_dir is otherwise untouched
- `sragent/store.py:234` — save(): _flushed=0 on a fresh store -> else branch rewrites store_changelog.jsonl; backup=True only for store.json (231)
- `sragent/stages/planning.py:23` — protocol.yaml re-adopted on --fresh: PLAN is not asked at all
- `sragent/feedback.py:713` — accept of a stale proposal sets status 'stale' once and raises StaleProposal; todos.build then skips it (todos.py:303)
- `sragent/stages/fulltext.py:281` — new_local_files also compares ft node's local_file, so surviving provenance.jsonl does not hide PDFs from a fresh store
- `sragent/llm.py:74` — CostTracker re-reads usage.jsonl: 'the budget is cumulative per run directory'
- `sragent/llm.py:500` — one DiskCache per cache_dir shared by every run; hit returned at 266-275
- `sragent/cli.py:295` — run parser: --until, --plan-only, --fresh; no cache flag

#### I35 · The config_lock stage-model lock is set by output presence (from outside the stage, before any model call), never released, only checks keys already recorded, and records the configured model rather than the one that produced the node

*not yet verified · medium · models-config · reasons: R1, R6*

record_if_new runs on every ctx.save(): the first save after STAGE_STARTED[s] becomes true records stage_models.s and nothing ever removes the key (sragent/config_lock.py:105-113; accept() keeps every started stage, 130-133). STAGE_STARTED is node presence (31-41): fulltext is 'started' when a screen node reaches stage retrieval, which _mark_retrieval sets without a model call (screening.py:466-476), or when sync/feedback fetch an ft node; snowball is 'started' by meta.setdefault before any seed is chased (snowball.py:34); extract/rob when the screen-stage engine pass creates them. diff() only compares LOCKED keys already in the record (92), so adding a key to LOCKED never protects existing reviews although configs/README.md:85 says a started run records its settings on its next save. models_by_stage reports the recorded model as 'the model each stage ran with' (165-175) although the screen-stage engine pass ran extract/rob items under stage_models.screen. set_stage_models then refuses: 'These stages have started, so their model is fixed' (server.py:814-816).

Evidence:
- `sragent/config_lock.py:31` — STAGE_STARTED lambdas: node presence; fulltext also true for a screen node at full_text/retrieval; snowball = meta.snowball
- `sragent/config_lock.py:105` — record_if_new only adds keys; accept keeps started stages (130-133); diff checks recorded keys only (92)
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:466` — _mark_retrieval sets stage='retrieval' without a model call
- `sragent/stages/snowball.py:34` — meta.setdefault('snowball', ...) before any work
- `sragent/config_lock.py:165` — models_by_stage: 'the model each started stage ran with (as recorded when it started)'

waiting_for_pdfs is written only by pipeline._waiting_for_pdfs (sragent/pipeline.py:120-127) yet read by steps.py:55 (synthesis 'ready to run') and next_action (todos.py:644-645, 678-679); the To-do answers that resolve the wait (pdf_unavailable at todos.py:454-458 and bulk 597) never recompute it, so the banner says 'waits for N full texts' until the next run. pdf_unavailable is only ever appended (todos.py:456, 597) and snoozed only appended (417-419; grep: no remover), so 'Later' on the sync or fulltexts card and a mistaken 'Not obtainable' are permanent. rules_applied is rewritten by every rules.apply (rules.py:114) and drives _auto_rules writes on GET. graph.stale never sees meta (graph.py:130-135) and store.diff copies whole changed meta keys from a sandbox on accept (store.py:272; feedback.py:75, 695-696).

#### I62 · Status flips (set_status) are not versions, so re-including a study is invisible to dependents; the way back is engine 'reactivate'/'integrate' items driven by meta.synthesis_considered and scans of statement history

*not yet verified · medium · graph-engine · reasons: R3, R2*

Exclusion propagates only because stale_reasons treats a non-active dependency as stale (graph.py:123-124). Re-activation (engine._statuses 359; feedback.py:580, 603) leaves the dependent's recorded version unchanged, and after a statement dropped the study it no longer has an edge to that study's ext/rob (synthesis.statement_deps lists cited studies only, synthesis.py:14-20). Recovery relies on engine.plan's 'reactivate'/'integrate' items (engine.py:142-151) and _include_study's scan of every statement's history for earlier citations (feedback.py:589-604); after any sandbox run copied synthesis_considered (I53), nothing brings the study back while the dependency view says the synthesis is current.

#### I64 · Config values that shape outputs are neither settings nodes nor (for several) locked, so an accepted change makes nothing stale; cfg:human_mode/cfg:screening_rules are nodes nothing depends on; fulltext_unclear is read at six sites; sync_settings rewrites nodes on every Ctx.create before any accept

*confirmed · medium · models-config · reasons: R2*

pipeline.fulltext_unclear is LOCKED (config_lock.py:27) but has no settings node (graph.py:54-72) and is read only at decision time (screening.py:215, 281, 371, 554; engine.py:340; feedback.py:162; fulltext_screen_chars at screening.py:202, 265, 405 and extraction.py:229); verify_screening, verify_rob, verify_synthesis, repair_synthesis, context_chars, fulltext_screen_chars, max_stored_chars, validate_terms, skip_stages and update_policy are neither locked nor nodes. Because engine.plan derives staleness only from dependency versions (engine.py:69), accepting a switch from include to await after a pilot leaves the 'included by rule' studies included with a screening_unclear flag (screening.py:538-541) that no to-do lists (todos.py:194). cfg:human_mode and cfg:screening_rules are settings nodes no node depends on. graph.sync_settings runs on every Ctx.create with an existing store (context.py:66-67), including the check_config=False paths (server.py:611, 829; cli.py:42), and upserts a new version when data differ (graph.py:82-83); in the server paths accept() follows at once, and in the CLI path a refused acceptance is never saved, so the premature version move is in-memory only.

Scenario: 1) Pilot run with pipeline.fulltext_unclear: include. screen_one decides with decide(..., 'include') (screening.py:215); unclear full-text records get decision 'include' and data.unclear_by_rule='include'; sync_awaiting_flags opens a 'screening_unclear' flag (538-541). 2) The person edits the run file to fulltext_unclear: await. Ctx.create(check_config=True) raises ConfigChanged because the key is LOCKED (config_lock.py:27, 96-102); the person accepts via the UI (server.py:829-832) or 'sragent accept-config' (cli.py:42-58). 3) After acceptance nothing is stale: graph.settings_from_config (graph.py:54-72) creates nodes only for question, seed_eligibility, screening_rules, records, human_mode, rob_tool, field:*, dom:*; engine.plan derives staleness solely from graph.stale(st) dependency versions (engine.py:69), so no screen node is re-derived; the 'included by rule' studies stay included. The screening_unclear flag is not a to-do: todos.py:194 only lists flags with source synthesis_verify/term_validation/evidence_check/eligibility_doubt, and 'screening_unclear' appears nowhere in todos.py, server.py or index.html. 4) The policy is only read again at the next decision: screening.py:215, 281, 371, 554; engine.py:340; feedback.py:162. fulltext_screen_chars is read at screening.py:202, 265, 405 and extraction.py:229. verify_screening (screening._verify_wanted), verify_rob (rob), verify_synthesis/repair_synthesis, context_chars, max_stored_chars, validate_terms, skip_stages, update_policy (engine.py:219) are in neither LOCKED nor settings_from_config. 5) cfg:human_mode is a settings node (graph.py:63) but no node declares it in depends_on (grep: only the docstring at graph.py:41 mentions it); cfg:screening_rules is read via st.get('cfg:screening_rules') in engine.execute (engine.py:240) but likewise is not a dependency edge. 6) Ctx.create calls graph.sync_settings(store, cfg) whenever the store exists (context.py:66-67), including the check_config=False paths (server.py:611, 829; cli.py:36-42); sync_settings upserts a new version whenever data differ (graph.py:82-83). In the server paths accept() follows immediately; in the CLI path a refused acceptance returns without ctx.save() (cli.py:55-57), so the moved versions stay in memory only. Since every settings node corresponds to a LOCKED key, the check_config=True path raises before sync_settings runs.

Evidence:
- `sragent/config_lock.py:24` — LOCKED includes pipeline.fulltext_unclear; verify_*, *_chars, validate_terms, skip_stages, update_policy absent
- `sragent/graph.py:54` — settings_from_config: cfg:question, seed_eligibility, screening_rules, records, human_mode, rob_tool, field:*, dom:* only
- `sragent/engine.py:69` — plan() derives work only from graph.stale(st) (dependency versions); a changed fulltext_unclear has no node and makes nothing stale
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:215` — fulltext_unclear read at decision time; also 281, 371, 554; engine.py:340; feedback.py:162
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:538` — 'included by rule' studies get a screening_unclear flag; todos.py:194 does not list that source and no UI code references it
- `sragent/context.py:66` — graph.sync_settings(store, cfg) runs on every Ctx.create with an existing store, after config_lock.check (only when check_config=True)
- `sragent/ui/server.py:611` — Ctx.create(check_config=False) then config_lock.accept (614); also 829-832
- `sragent/cli.py:42` — accept-config: _ctx(check_config=False) -> sync_settings before the y/N prompt; a refusal returns without save (55-57)

#### I66 · provenance.jsonl is the processed-file registry for PDFs, and sandbox runs write to it (and store fetched files), so a rejected proposal can make a replaced PDF invisible to processing

*not yet verified · medium · fulltext · reasons: R2, R5*

fulltext.new_local_files decides 'new file' by name and by SHA-256 membership in provenance.jsonl (sragent/stages/fulltext.py:276-283). fetch_one records a pdf_provision 'supplied' event with the file's sha whenever it is absent (165-167) and keep_fetched_files writes files (212-213), on whichever store it runs; it is called on sandbox stores by actions.rerun (actions.py:286-287), feedback._include_study (feedback.py:575-576) and engine._extract (engine.py:368-369). The live ft:<rid> node is not updated by a sandbox run, but the registry is, so after a rejected re-run the Full texts panel shows 0 new files and `sragent pdfs` processes nothing.

Evidence:
- `sragent/stages/fulltext.py:165` — provenance 'supplied' recorded if the sha is not yet in provenance.jsonl
- `sragent/stages/fulltext.py:281` — new = ft.local_file != f.name or content_sha(f) not in processed
- `sragent/actions.py:286` — rerun step=screen at full text fetches on the sandbox store
- `sragent/engine.py:368` — _extract fetches on whatever store ctx holds

#### I68 · Verification outcomes (screen_verify, eligibility_review, rob_verify, decided_by) are process state stored in data that gates re-verification and the human queue, with no node or edge to the verifier settings; source_changed flags never reach a to-do

*confirmed · medium · screening · reasons: R2, R9*

Verification outcomes (screen_verify and decided_by written by screen_one, screening.py:226-228; eligibility_review and decided_by 'extraction+verifier' written by recheck_eligibility, extraction.py:261/270; rob_verify written by rob._verify) are process state stored in node data that gates re-verification and the human queue: verify_existing skips any node with screen_verify or eligibility_review (screening.py:390-391), rob.verify_existing skips drafts with rob_verify (rob.py:159), and rob.needs_person routes a draft to a person by rob_verify.outcome (rob.py:82-88). Each record does name the verifier model that ran (the 'by' field holds provider:model), but there is no settings node or dependency edge for roles.verifier / pipeline.verify_screening / pipeline.verify_rob, so changing the verifier or its mode makes nothing stale and the CLI-only verify-* commands treat existing verdicts as final. rob_one's source_changed flag (rob.py:28-30) is neither a To-do kind (todos.py:194) nor a labelled report kind (report.py:398-399); it is only visible under its raw key in open_flags and on the study page.

Scenario: screen_one stores screen_verify (screening.py:226) and decided_by='verifier' (228) in the screen node's data. verify_existing (screening.py:390-391) skips any node with human_decision, rule, screen_verify or eligibility_review, so `sragent verify-screening` (cli.py:243-247) never re-reads a record once a verify record exists, whatever model or mode produced it. rob.needs_person (rob.py:82-88) returns False when rob_verify.outcome is 'agreed' or 'decided_by_verifier', which removes the draft from the required To-do queue (todos.py:264 'optional': not needs_person); rob.verify_existing (rob.py:159) skips drafts that already carry rob_verify, so `sragent verify-rob` (cli.py:250-252) treats old verdicts as final. recheck_eligibility writes eligibility_review and decided_by='extraction+verifier' into the screen node (extraction.py:261, 270). Over-claim refuted: each verify record does record the verifier model: screen_verify.by = checker.name (screening.py:347), eligibility_review.verifier.by = checker.name (extraction.py:239), rob_verify.by = chk.name (rob.py:117), and Agent.name is f'{provider}:{model}' (llm.py:245-246). What is true is that there is no settings node and no edge for roles.verifier, pipeline.verify_screening or pipeline.verify_rob: graph.settings_from_config (graph.py:54-72) emits only cfg:question, cfg:seed_eligibility, cfg:screening_rules, cfg:records, cfg:human_mode, cfg:rob_tool, field:* and dom:*, and screen/rob depends_on never include a verifier setting; so changing the verifier model or mode makes nothing stale and re-runs nothing, and the skip guards above make the CLI verify-* commands a no-op for already-verified nodes. source_changed: rob_one flags a human-confirmed draft whose full text changed with source='source_changed' (rob.py:28-30); todos.build handles flags only for sources synthesis_verify, term_validation, evidence_check, eligibility_doubt (todos.py:194), manual_edit (226) and feedback (243), so no To-do is created; report.FLAG_KIND (report.py:398-399) has no entry for it, so open_flags shows the raw key 'source_changed' (report.py:419 fallback) and the study page lists it via studies._flags_of.

Evidence:
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:390` — verify_existing skips nodes with human_decision, rule, screen_verify or eligibility_review
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:226` — screen_one stores screen_verify; 228 decided_by='verifier'
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:347` — screen_verify record has 'by': checker.name (provider:model) -- the verifier model IS recorded
- `sragent/stages/rob.py:82` — needs_person: False when rob_verify.outcome in (agreed, decided_by_verifier); line 159 verify_existing skips drafts with rob_verify
- `sragent/stages/rob.py:117` — rob_verify record has 'by': chk.name
- `sragent/stages/extraction.py:239` — eligibility_review.verifier.by = checker.name; decided_by 'extraction+verifier' at 261/270
- `sragent/graph.py:54` — settings_from_config has no node for roles.verifier / verify_screening / verify_rob
- `sragent/stages/rob.py:28` — source='source_changed' flag

#### I69 · Direct in-place data mutations (verify_one, revalidate_terms, flag text edits) bypass versioning but count as 'changes' for store.diff, so they ride into proposals and are versioned on accept as the reviewer's dependent changes

*not yet verified · medium · graph-engine · reasons: R3*

synthesis.verify_one writes n['data']['verification'] in place with no upsert, history or changelog (sragent/stages/synthesis.py:154); extraction.revalidate_terms mutates item['terms'] and replaces n['flags'] (extraction.py:396-398); actions._mark_dependents edits a flag's text (actions.py:91). store.diff compares data/flags (store.py:270), so a sandbox verify_one (engine._synthesis runs it on whatever store, engine.py:433-436) makes the statement a 'changed' node; sandbox_delta includes it (feedback.py:73-74) and commit upserts a new live version 'accepted feedback (dependent change)' with actor user (688-690) that was never listed in cs['changes'] because _changed compares the editable view, which excludes verification (194-196, 79-93).

Evidence:
- `sragent/stages/synthesis.py:154` — n['data']['verification'] = res, no upsert
- `sragent/store.py:270` — diff: version/status/data/evidence/flags/depends_on compared
- `sragent/feedback.py:73` — sandbox_nodes = listed ∪ diff.new ∪ diff.changed; commit upserts any differing node (688-690)
- `sragent/stages/extraction.py:396` — revalidate_terms mutates fields and flags directly

### Plain defects found on the way (14)

#### I20 · The answer queue lives only in memory; drained failures (ok:false) are dropped silently; each queued answer logs twice; drained jobs bypass submit()

*confirmed · high · ui-backend · reasons: R8*

pending_answers is an in-memory list initialised at sragent/ui/server.py:44 and never restored by _load(); nothing reads the 'queued' lines back from human_decisions.jsonl, so a UI restart while a job runs silently loses every decision the person was told was 'Saved' (494-495, 545) and the to-dos reappear. _drain_answers (502-518) logs only exceptions as 'failed'; an {ok:false} result from todos.apply (todos.py:413-414 agent_only, 437-438 include without full text, 564 unknown) or actions.edit (actions.py:112-113) is discarded with no log line and no message (only StaleProposal logs 'refused_stale' itself, todos.py:551). Each queued answer yields a 'queued' line (493/544) and an 'applied' line (todos.py:566 or actions.py:225-227). Jobs produced by drained answers are appended at 517-518 with _last_models() read at drain time and bypass submit() (315-326): no label, no created, no 'queued' record, no ui_settings write; the worker records them first at 'running' (401), the Activity list shows only the bare action word and no time (index.html:1683-1684), and after a restart _load_jobs sorts them first (139).

Scenario: Restart loss: a job is running; reviewer answers an eligibility to-do -> App.answer() (server.py:488) sees self.job is not None, appends to self.pending_answers (an in-memory list initialised at 44 and never rebuilt by _load()), writes a 'queued' line to human_decisions.jsonl (493) and returns 'Saved. A job is running, so this is applied as soon as it finishes.' (494-495; same text from mutate() at 545). The UI's next_action also says 'You can keep answering; answers are applied when it finishes' (todos.py:638). The UI process is then restarted (Ctrl+C, crash, or to pick up a change): _load() only reloads jobs.jsonl and marks running/queued jobs 'interrupted' (136-138); no code reads the 'queued' lines back (grep: human_decisions.jsonl is only read by studies.history for display). The answers are gone and the to-dos reappear. Silent drop: after the job, _worker calls _drain_answers under self.lock (444) -> for each answer `r = self._apply_op(ctx, ans)`; only r.get('job') is inspected (509-511); an exception is logged 'failed' (513-514) but an {ok:false} result - e.g. todos.apply's 'cannot be included without a full-text review' (todos.py:437-438), 'unknown to-do' (564), actions.edit's ok:false (actions.py:112-113), or the agent_only refusal (413-414) - is discarded with no human_decisions line (only StaleProposal logs 'refused_stale' itself, todos.py:551) and no message; the to-do simply reappears. Double log: the queued line at 493/544 plus the 'applied' line written by todos.apply at 566 (or actions.edit at actions.py:225-227) for the same answer. Bypassing submit(): jobs collected from drained answers are appended at 517-518 as {**_last_models(), **j, id, status:'queued'} with no label, no created, no _record('queued') and no ui_settings write, unlike submit() (315-326). The worker records them only at 'running' (401) with label None and created None; the Activity list shows j.label||j.action, i.e. the bare action word 'feedback'/'sync' (index.html:625, 1684) and fmtT(undefined) for the time (1683); after a restart _load_jobs sorts them first (139, 'created or 0').

Evidence:
- `sragent/ui/server.py:44` — pending_answers = [] in __init__; _load() (49-60) never repopulates it; no reader of 'queued' lines in human_decisions.jsonl anywhere in sragent/
- `sragent/ui/server.py:493` — answer(): log_answer(..., 'queued') then 'Saved. A job is running, so this is applied as soon as it finishes.'; mutate() does the same at 544-545
- `sragent/ui/server.py:508` — _drain_answers: `r = self._apply_op(ctx, ans)`; only r.get('job') is read; except Exception -> log 'failed' (513-514); r['ok'] False is never checked
- `sragent/todos.py:437` — apply() returns {ok:false} for include without full text (437-438), agent_only (413-414), unknown to-do (564) without any log line; only refused_stale (551) logs
- `sragent/todos.py:566` — apply() writes the 'applied' line after ctx.save() -> second line for a queued answer
- `sragent/ui/server.py:518` — queue.append({**_last_models(), **j, 'id':..., 'status':'queued'}) - no label, created, _record or ui_settings write; submit() (315-326) does all of these
- `sragent/ui/server.py:139` — _load_jobs sorts by created or 0: records without a timestamp sort first
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1684` — Activity row shows j.label||j.action and fmtT(j.created) (1683): drained jobs appear as the bare action word with no time

#### I89 · extract_one's eligibility-doubt exit returns {} without an upsert; callers index the missing node and crash, and process_pdfs drafts a RoB for a study that was just excluded or set pending

*confirmed · high · extraction-rob-synthesis · reasons: R5*

extraction.extract_one returns {} without upserting ext:<rid> when recheck_eligibility yields exclude or pending (sragent/stages/extraction.py:316-319). On a first-time extraction (no ext node yet) every caller that then indexes the node crashes with TypeError on None['data']: engine._extract (engine.py:373; reached by 'Update now' via sync.py:92, by pipeline.py:30 and 68), feedback._include_study (feedback.py:582-585) and actions.rerun step=extract (actions.py:296); the paid extraction and verifier calls and the eligibility change are lost with the aborted job. A re-extraction does not crash (the existing node is set 'excluded' at 281-284 and still readable), and the first pipeline run (extract_all, 362-368) never indexes the node. sync.process_pdfs (sync.py:37-38) still calls rob.rob_one after a doubt-excluded extraction, paying for a RoB draft and upserting an active rob node for a non-included study; line 43 is guarded (40) and the orphan is retired by sync_dependents -> engine._statuses (engine.py:352-361) in the same call, so that part is a wasted call rather than a lasting inconsistency.

Scenario: A person includes study R from the To-do list (todos.apply include) or R is included on a re-screen; no ext:R node exists yet. Reviewer clicks 'Update now' -> sync.sync_dependents (sync.py:88-92) -> engine.execute -> plan() adds ext:R 'extract' ('included, not extracted', engine.py:140-141) -> _extract (364-373): old = None, before = None, extraction.extract_one(ctx, R). The extractor returns an eligibility_doubt (_doubt, extraction.py:197-205), recheck_eligibility (211-291) sets screen:R to exclude or awaiting on the live store and returns 'exclude'/'pending', so extract_one returns {} at 316-319 without any upsert of ext:R. Back in _extract line 373: st.get('ext:R') is None -> None['data'] -> TypeError; engine.execute has no try/except, so 'Update now' aborts after the paid extraction and verifier calls (the sync CLI/job fails without ctx.save). The same happens in pipeline._synthesize's else-branch and pipeline._screen's engine.execute (pipeline.py:30, 68), in feedback._include_study (feedback.py:582-585: fn(sb, rid) then st.get(nid)['data'] for a missing ext node — reached by feedback that includes a study or the engine's integrate item), and in actions.rerun step='extract' for an included study with no ext yet (allowed at 263-266; 296 sb.store.get(nid)['data']). Not a crash: the normal first run — extract_all (362-368) uses ctx.map and never indexes the node. When ext:R already existed, recheck sets it 'excluded' (281-284) and st.get returns the node, so only first-time extraction crashes. process_pdfs (sync.py:34-38) calls rob.rob_one unconditionally after extract_one: with ext:R missing, _ext_brief returns '{}' (rob.py:99-101) and rob_one upserts rob:R active for a study that is now excluded/awaiting (a paid call); line 43's st.get('ext:R')['data'] is guarded by 'old is not None' (40), so no crash there, and sync_dependents at line 52 -> engine._statuses (engine.py:352-361) retires the orphan rob node in the same call, so the active RoB for a non-included study is transient (the wasted call is not).

Evidence:
- `sragent/stages/extraction.py:316` — outcome in ('exclude','pending') -> return {} before the ext upsert (330)
- `sragent/engine.py:373` — _record(..., st.get(f'ext:{rid}')['data'], ...) -> TypeError when no ext node existed (old None at 370-371)
- `sragent/engine.py:140` — plan adds 'extract' for included studies without extraction; reached from sync.sync_dependents (sync.py:92), pipeline.py:30 and 68
- `sragent/feedback.py:582` — _include_study: fn(sb, rid) then st.get(nid)['data'] with no None guard
- `sragent/actions.py:296` — rerun: after = sb.store.get(nid)['data']; step 'extract' allowed without an existing ext node (263-266)
- `sragent/sync.py:37` — process_pdfs: extract_one then rob_one unconditionally; line 40 guards the ext read; sync_dependents (52) -> engine._statuses (352-361) retires the orphan rob node
- `sragent/stages/extraction.py:362` — extract_all uses ctx.map and never indexes the node: the first pipeline run does not crash

#### I02 · Bulk eligibility save ignores per-item refusals and reports 'N saved'

*confirmed · medium · human-loop · reasons: R4*

todos.bulk kind=eligibility calls apply() per item and increments n regardless of the returned ok (sragent/todos.py:582-586). apply refuses an include without a full text at 437-438, returning before log_answer, so the refused decision is neither stored nor logged. App._apply_op returns {ok:True, message:'N saved'} (sragent/ui/server.py:526-527) and saveElig shows it as success and toasts it (index.html:1033-1034); the To-do table offers Include on rows that have no full text (index.html:1001). The reviewer is told N decisions were saved when some were silently not applied, and the refusal message ('supply <rid>.pdf first') is lost. Correction: the agent_only refusal (apply 409-414) is not reachable through bulk because bulk itself raises NotAllowed at 580 before any item is processed, which surfaces as an HTTP 500 error in the UI rather than a false 'saved'.

Scenario: A To-do eligibility row whose suggestion is 'Need full text' (no PDF, suggest.option 'pdf' from todos.py:151) still offers an Include button (OPT at index.html:1001). The reviewer sets it to Include, sets two other rows to Exclude with a criterion, and presses 'Save 3 decisions'. saveElig posts /api/bulk {kind:eligibility, items:[...]} (1032) -> server.mutate -> _apply_op (server.py:526) -> todos.bulk (todos.py:577-586). For each item bulk calls apply(...) and does n += 1 without reading the returned dict (584-586). For the Include row, apply hits line 437-438 ((ft:<rid>).data.kind != 'fulltext') and returns {ok:False, message:'<rid> cannot be included without a full-text review; supply <rid>.pdf first.'} BEFORE log_answer (566), so nothing is written to the store or to human_decisions.jsonl. bulk returns 3; _apply_op returns {ok:True, message:'3 saved'} (527); saveElig renders it as a green '3 saved' and toasts it (1033-1034), deletes elig[r] for all three rids and refreshes. After refresh the undecided row reappears with its selection reset to the suggestion and no explanation; the refusal text is never shown. Correction on the agent_only sub-claim: todos.bulk calls provenance.require_judgment_allowed(ctx, kind) at 580 before the loop, which raises NotAllowed; the POST handler's except at server.py:1195-1196 returns HTTP 500 {error:...} and saveElig shows r.error in red, so agent_only is not a silent-success case. The same discarded-return pattern also applies when a job is running: mutate queues the op and _drain_answers later calls _apply_op with the same '{n} saved' result that nobody reads.

Evidence:
- `sragent/todos.py:584` — bulk kind=eligibility: apply(ctx, {...}) return value discarded; n += 1 at 586
- `sragent/todos.py:438` — include without full text returns {ok: False, message} before ctx.save/log_answer (566): no store write, no human_decisions line
- `sragent/todos.py:580` — agent_only: require_judgment_allowed raises NotAllowed before the loop, propagating to server.py:1195-1196 (HTTP 500 with error) rather than being counted
- `sragent/ui/server.py:526` — _apply_op bulk -> {'ok': True, 'message': f'{n} saved'}
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1001` — OPT offers Include on every eligibility row, including rows with no full text (suggest 'pdf' from todos.py:151)
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1033` — saveElig renders r.message as success and toasts it when r.ok

#### I103 · The HTTP cache has no TTL and caches 404 as None forever across all runs, and pmcid_for ignores isOpenAccess (dead branch), so a later-OA article is never retrieved and 'live' PubMed counts are frozen

*not yet verified · medium · tools · reasons: R2*

http.get stores {'body': None} on 404 (sragent/sources/http.py:49-50, 58-59) in a DiskCache with no expiry (llm.py:120-135) living in cfg.cache_dir shared by all runs; affected calls include Europe PMC fullTextXML and pmcid_for, Unpaywall, OpenAlex and esearch, so an article that becomes open access later is never retrieved and a query without maxdate returns the same hit list forever, including the search-strategy card's 'live hit counts'. europepmc.pmcid_for returns the PMCID on both branches (europepmc.py:16-18), so a non-OA PMCID yields a fullTextXML 404 that is then cached forever.

Evidence:
- `sragent/sources/http.py:49` — 404 -> body None; cached at 58-59
- `sragent/sources/europepmc.py:16` — if isOpenAccess == 'Y': return pmcid; return pmcid anyway
- `sragent/llm.py:500` — cache_dir shared by all runs

#### I104 · Network errors in term validation silently become status 'not_found': synonyms vanish from the search query and screening prompts during planning, and false 'Term not found' flags are raised at extraction

*not yet verified · medium · tools · reasons: R9*

terms._safe returns {'error': ...} on any exception (sragent/terms.py:41-45); validate counts only v.get('found') (144-145) so an outage yields status 'not_found' (157) or leaves disease status not_found (160-166); planning keeps only status/preferred/ids/synonyms (planning.py:45) so the error text is dropped from protocol.yaml; revalidate_terms and extract_one flag every not_found as 'Term not found in RxNorm/PubChem/ChEMBL/MONDO' (extraction.py:336-339, 401-403).

Evidence:
- `sragent/terms.py:41` — _safe returns {'error': ...}
- `sragent/terms.py:157` — status 'not_found' when nothing found (errors indistinguishable)
- `sragent/stages/extraction.py:336` — not_found -> term_validation flag to the person

#### I105 · Full-text retrieval misreports: an unreadable open-access PDF is 'no open-access full text found', an unreadable user file is silently replaced by an online copy with origin 'Europe PMC', and the 'no open-access full text found' reason is emitted even when the lookups were disabled

*not yet verified · medium · fulltext · reasons: R5, R2*

parse_failed is set only on the user-file branch (sragent/stages/fulltext.py:171-172); the OA branch leaves kind='abstract' when raw <= 1500 (192-195) and review_one then marks 'fulltext_unavailable' with reason 'no open-access full text found; PDF requested' (screening.py:496) although a PDF was downloaded to fulltext/auto and auto_pdf is recorded only when kind==fulltext (212-213). After a short user-file parse, fetch_one continues to Europe PMC/OA (174-195) and on success resets parse_failed (207: `parse_failed and kind != 'fulltext'`) with origin 'Europe PMC ...' while local_file is still recorded (211), so the reviewer is never told the supplied PDF was unreadable. screening.py:496 always uses the 'no open-access' reason even when pipeline.fulltext or open_access_lookup is False (fulltext.py:174, 185). The study-page file contract also differs: local_file accepts .pdf/.txt only (47) while supplements and parse_file accept .md/.csv (docparse.py:196, 173-176).

Evidence:
- `sragent/stages/fulltext.py:171` — parse_failed only on the user-file branch; reset at 207
- `sragent/stages/fulltext.py:192` — OA branch: short parse leaves kind 'abstract'
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:496` — reason 'no open-access full text found; PDF requested' always
- `sragent/stages/fulltext.py:47` — local_file tries .pdf/.PDF/.txt only; docparse.supplement_files accepts .md/.csv (196)

#### I106 · 'Decide without it' is shown where it has no effect (priority-1 not-retrieved rows) and hidden where it would (priority-2 awaiting rows); not-retrieved records are not in the eligibility table either

*confirmed · medium · ui-frontend · reasons: R7, R2*

pdfPanel renders 'Decide without it' only for rows with x.priority===1 (index.html:1048), i.e. records whose screen decision is fulltext_unavailable or parse_failed (fulltext.py:227-230). The handler it calls, bulk(kind='pdf_unwant') (todos.py:604-608), only pops meta.pdf_wanted[rid] and writes a human_judgment/decide_without_fulltext provenance event that nothing reads; pdf_wanted is only ever set for awaiting records (todos.py:450-451), so on a priority-1 row the click is a no-op: the record stays fulltext_unavailable, is re-listed by needed_pdfs with the same button after refresh, is still skipped by the eligibility table (todos.py:113 requires decision=='awaiting'), and the next full-text run only retries retrieval (screening.py:458). The reviewer nevertheless gets the toast 'The agent will decide without this full text'. The rows where popping pdf_wanted would actually act - priority-2 rows (awaiting + pdf_wanted, fulltext.py:231-232), which todos.py:114-115 hides from the eligibility table precisely because pdf_wanted is set - never get the button. For those rows the only visible control is 'Not obtainable', which does return the record to the eligibility table (it pops pdf_wanted, todos.py:599) but also marks it pdf_unavailable with pdf_provision/not_obtainable provenance and a 'marked not obtainable' badge. For a priority-1 record, neither available route yields an agent decision: the study-page Edit can only exclude it by hand (include is refused without full text, actions.py:117; 'awaiting' sets human_decision and hides it from both the table and the agent), and 'Not obtainable' only removes the row from the list and counts the study as not retrieved while its decision stays fulltext_unavailable. All cited line numbers in the original statement are accurate.

Scenario: 1) Full-text review runs; record R has no open-access copy, so review_one() falls through to _mark_retrieval(ctx, R, "fulltext_unavailable", ...) (sragent/stages/screening.py:496); screen:R now has decision=fulltext_unavailable, stage=retrieval.
2) needed_pdfs() lists R with priority 1 (fulltext.py:227-228; it does not look at meta.pdf_wanted for this branch). todos.build() puts R in the 'fulltexts' todo's files (todos.py:167-169) and skips R in the eligibility loop because decision != 'awaiting' (todos.py:113).
3) pdfPanel renders R's row with 'Decide without it' because x.priority===1 && !x.present (index.html:1048) and 'Not obtainable' (index.html:1049).
4) Reviewer clicks 'Decide without it' -> pdfUnwant(R) (index.html:1072) -> POST /api/bulk {kind:'pdf_unwant'} -> server.py:525-526 -> todos.bulk(); the only matching branch (todos.py:604-608) pops meta.pdf_wanted[R] (a no-op: pdf_wanted is only ever set by apply(elig:<rid>, option='pdf') at todos.py:450-451, which requires the record to be in the eligibility table, i.e. awaiting), records provenance human_judgment/decide_without_fulltext, saves, returns n=1. The UI toasts 'The agent will decide without this full text'.
5) refresh(true): needed_pdfs() still lists R (decision unchanged), the same row and button come back; the eligibility table still excludes R; needs_fulltext_review() (screening.py:455-459, dec in RETRIEVAL_FAILURES) still returns True, so a later full-text run only retries retrieval. grep shows no reader of the 'decide_without_fulltext' provenance event anywhere in sragent/. Nothing decides R.
6) Conversely, record Q sits in the eligibility table and the reviewer picks 'Need full text': apply(elig:Q, 'pdf') sets meta.pdf_wanted[Q]=True (todos.py:450-451). Now todos.py:113-114 hides Q from the eligibility table ('listed under Full texts') and needed_pdfs() lists Q with priority 2 (fulltext.py:231-232). In pdfPanel Q's row has x.priority===2, so 'Decide without it' is not rendered - yet pdf_unwant is exactly the operation that would pop pdf_wanted[Q] and return Q to the eligibility table. The only visible control for Q is 'Not obtainable' (todos.py:595-602), which does pop pdf_wanted (so Q reappears in the eligibility table) but also adds Q to meta.pdf_unavailable, records pdf_provision/not_obtainable instead of human_judgment/decide_without_fulltext, and shows a 'marked not obtainable' badge on the study page (index.html:1504).
7) Other routes for R checked: study-page Edit (index.html:1552/1540 -> actions.edit, actions.py:111-134): 'include' is refused unless ft kind == fulltext (actions.py:117-118); 'awaiting' sets human_decision, after which todos.py:113 skips it from the eligibility table and screening.py:455 stops the agent re-screening it; only a manual 'exclude' works. 'Not obtainable' for R keeps decision=fulltext_unavailable, removes the row from the Full texts todo (todos.py:168 filter) and counts it as not retrieved (studies.py:76-78, report.py:41/519); it does not produce a decision.

Evidence:
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1048` — `${x.priority===1 && !x.present ? <button onclick="pdfUnwant(...)">Decide without it</button> : ""}` - rendered only for priority-1 rows; line 1049 renders 'Not obtainable' for any missing row
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1072` — pdfUnwant posts /api/bulk kind=pdf_unwant and toasts 'The agent will decide without this full text' on r.ok, then refresh(true)
- `sragent/todos.py:604` — bulk(): kind=='pdf_unwant' only does meta.pdf_wanted.pop(rid) + provenance.record('human_judgment','decide_without_fulltext') + save; the screen node is untouched (lines 604-608)
- `sragent/todos.py:113` — eligibility loop: `if d["decision"] != "awaiting" or d.get("human_decision"): continue`; lines 114-115 skip awaiting rows with pdf_wanted set and no full text ('listed under Full texts')
- `sragent/todos.py:450` — only setter of meta.pdf_wanted: apply(elig:<rid>, option='pdf'), reachable only for records in the eligibility table (awaiting)
- `sragent/todos.py:595` — kind=='pdf_unavailable' adds to meta.pdf_unavailable and pops pdf_wanted (lines 595-602) - for priority-2 rows this is the de-facto 'decide without it' under the wrong label and provenance
- `sragent/todos.py:168` — fulltexts todo: `wanted = [x for x in needed_pdfs(ctx) if x["rid"] not in unavailable]` - 'Not obtainable' removes the row but does not change the decision
- `sragent/stages/fulltext.py:227` — priority 1 = decision fulltext_unavailable (227-228) or parse_failed (229-230), independent of pdf_wanted; priority 2 = awaiting and meta.pdf_wanted[rid] (231-232)

#### I22 · job_agent is merged with deep_merge so the agent role's long_context pricing, extra_body and json_mode leak into the job model, unlike stage overrides

*not yet verified · medium · models-config · reasons: R6*

agent_for builds the job client with `deep_merge(role_cfg(cfg,'agent'), cfg['job_agent'])` (sragent/llm.py:525). Stage overrides go through _with_model/_over_agent, which replace pricing wholesale and reset extra_body/json_mode on a provider change (config.py:250-261, 327-332) precisely so a stage's price never merges into the agent's. role_override writes pricing without long_context (server.py:239-241), so a Haiku agent with long_context pricing plus a Gemini job model yields Gemini base prices with Haiku's long_context tier, and a local agent's chat_template_kwargs in extra_body are sent to an API provider.

Evidence:
- `sragent/llm.py:525` — deep_merge(role_cfg(agent), job_agent)
- `sragent/config.py:327` — _over_agent: a stage's price replaces the agent's; _with_model resets extra_body/json_mode on provider change (257-261)
- `sragent/ui/server.py:239` — role_override pricing {input_per_m, output_per_m} only

#### I49 · Three cost figures for 'Update now' and structured data recovered by parsing display text (the Overview regex-parses the dollar figure from the to-do question; to-do context, 'insist' resend and evidence pointers parse flag text)

*confirmed · medium · ui-backend · reasons: R2, R7*

The sync cost is computed numerically by engine._cost (engine.py:180-200, plan items, no history), returned as stale.est_usd by sync.pending_changes (sync.py:77), formatted into the to-do question (todos.py:338-341) and then regex-parsed back out of that string by App.state (server.py:864-866) for the Overview, while the Run tab's action=sync estimate uses a different formula (server.py:254-259: pending_changes counts with history), so the Overview and the Run tab can show two different dollar figures and the to-do card (index.html:746-750) shows neither, only a hard-coded 'a few cents with Gemini Flash' hint. Likewise manual-edit context is split from flag text (todos.py:234) although dependents/from_version are stored on the flag (actions.py:95-96); the disagreement to-do and the 'insist' resend split on '-> agent:' (246, 510), re-sending the feedback truncated to 200 chars by feedback.py:263 although cs['feedback'] holds it in full; and _confirm_evidence (feedback.py:275-291) regex-parses field names from a '(context: ...)' string (todos.py:475) whose marker phrases no current code produces.

Scenario: Cost: engine.plan computes p['cost'] with _cost (engine.py:180-200: calls from plan items, estimate_stages without history); sync.pending_changes returns it numerically as stale.est_usd (sync.py:77); todos.build formats it into the sync to-do question string '(about $X.XX)' (todos.py:338-341); App.state then regex-parses `\$([0-9.]+)` back out of sync_t['question'] (server.py:864-866) for stale.est_usd, which the Overview shows as 'Updating costs about $X' (index.html:703). The Run tab's 'Update outputs after my decisions' (1231) calls /api/estimate action=sync, where server.estimate (254-259) builds calls from pending_changes counts (need_extract, need_rob, new_for_synthesis + 2*excluded_but_cited, synthesis_verify 3) with history, so e.g. a stale screen (reassess) or three stale statements (3 revise + 3 verify) are priced by the engine but not by this formula, and the two numbers differ. The to-do card itself (744-750) renders title and context, not the question, plus a hard-coded 'a few cents with Gemini Flash' hint. Text parsing: manual-edit context is f['text'].split(' dependent output')[0].split('. ')[0] (todos.py:234) over the text built at actions.py:93-94, although f['dependents'] and f['from_version'] are stored structurally (95-96); the disagreement to-do splits the flag text on '-> agent:' (246) and the 'insist' answer re-sends flag['text'].split('-> agent:')[0].split(':',1)[-1] (510), which is the feedback truncated to 200 chars when the flag was written (feedback.py:263), while cs['feedback'] in feedback/<id>.json holds the full text (feedback.py:211); _confirm_evidence (feedback.py:275-291) finds field names by regex in the '(context: ...)' tail that todos.apply appends from flag['text'][:300] (todos.py:475). grep finds no code producing the EVIDENCE_FLAG_MARKS strings, so that parser only ever matches text a person typed or forwarded.

Evidence:
- `sragent/ui/server.py:864` — regex `\$([0-9.]+)` over sync_t['question'] to recover est_usd
- `sragent/sync.py:77` — pending_changes returns stale.est_usd = p['cost']['total_usd'] numerically
- `sragent/todos.py:338` — cost formatted into the question string
- `sragent/ui/server.py:254` — estimate('sync'): calls from pending_changes counts with history; engine._cost (engine.py:180-200) from plan items without history
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:746` — sync to-do card renders title+context and a static 'a few cents with Gemini Flash' hint, not the question; Overview shows stale.est_usd at 703
- `sragent/todos.py:234` — manual_edit context from f['text'].split(' dependent output'); f['dependents'] stored structurally at actions.py:95-96
- `sragent/todos.py:510` — 'insist' re-sends the text parsed from the flag; feedback.py:263 wrote it truncated to 200 chars; cs['feedback'] (feedback.py:211) has the full text
- `sragent/feedback.py:275` — _confirm_evidence parses field names from the '(context: ...)' tail built at todos.py:475

engine.propose calls execute(sb, cs, roots=None, ...) (sragent/engine.py:277) and filters cs['changes'] to the deferred nodes or syn:* but falls back to all changes (278); sandbox_delta includes every changed node and commit applies them all (feedback.py:73-74, 681-690), so a 'propose synthesis only' policy carries unrelated work into the proposal. propose flags nodes[:1] (289-291) but cs.target is nodes[0] only when len(nodes)==1 (275), and accept/reject resolve flags on cs.target only (feedback.py:697-701, 733-737), so accepted or rejected multi-node update proposals leave a permanent open flag. engine.propose and actions.propagate_from both mint 'UP' ids (engine.py:273; actions.py:324), distinguished only by cs['kind'] (todos.py:309-314).

#### I96 · Server-side job spec mismatches: /api/run accepts any action and a typo fails only in the worker, ui_job_config.yaml is overwritten per job so recorded cmds are not reproducible, and the locked pipeline.max_records is exposed as a per-job option

*confirmed · medium · ui-backend · reasons: R8*

The Run page offers 'Max records to screen' for every benchmark run file (index.html:1239) and _write_job_config puts the value into the per-job overlay as pipeline.max_records (server.py:342-343), although pipeline.max_records is LOCKED (config_lock.py:27) and nothing in the form, runWarn, estimate or submit checks the lock. On a fresh benchmark the job subprocess records the overlay value as the lock (context.py:82), after which the UI's own App.ctx() (server.py:92-100, run file without overlay) raises ConfigChanged and the review shows as 'Paused: settings changed'; accepting it flips the lock to null and the next Run-page job (which restores the number from ui_settings.json, index.html:1193) exits with ConfigChanged rc=2 and is marked failed. On a benchmark started from the CLI, entering or clearing a number fails the job with ConfigChanged instead of being refused in the form. Minor: POST /api/run forwards any action to submit (1150-1151); an unknown action is queued and then fails in the worker via _cmd's ValueError (381, 414-415) — reachable only by a hand-crafted request. run_dir/ui_job_config.yaml is overwritten by each job (346-347) while the recorded cmd (400; shown at index.html:1356 and in the log) points at it, so the displayed command is not a faithful record of the budget/job_agent/max_records that job ran with; there is no re-run-from-history control, and jobs.jsonl keeps the model per job.

Scenario: Member 3 (max_records vs the lock) holds and is worse than stated. (1) Open a benchmark run file in the UI (e.g. configs/benchmark/emicizumab_pk/gemini_agentonly.yaml, which has `max_records: null` at line 23). /api/models returns benchmark=True (server.py:208, is_benchmark), so the Run page renders 'Max records to screen' (index.html:1239) for the 'run' action; neither runWarn (index.html:1220-1224) nor App.estimate (server.py:245-298) nor submit (314-325) consults config_lock. (2) The reviewer types 50 and presses Run: startRun posts max_records: 50 (index.html:1337-1340); submit records the spec and writes ui_settings.json (320-322); the worker calls _write_job_config, which writes run_dir/ui_job_config.yaml with include:[run file] and pipeline: {max_records: 50} (342-347), then runs `sragent.cli run --config <overlay>` (353-354, 398-410). (3) In the subprocess load_config deep-merges the overlay (config.py:167-176), so pipeline.max_records = 50; on the first ctx.save() record_if_new (context.py:81-82 -> config_lock.py:105-113) stores pipeline.max_records: 50 in store.json meta.config_lock because pipeline.max_records is in LOCKED (config_lock.py:27). (4) Every UI poll calls App.ctx() (server.py:92-100), which runs Ctx.create(self.cfg, ...) with self.cfg = load_config(run file) — no overlay — so config_lock.check (context.py:61-62 -> config_lock.py:96-102) diffs recorded 50 vs now None and raises ConfigChanged; /api/state catches it (server.py:1074-1076) and the UI shows 'Paused: settings changed' (index.html:599-601, 1984-2000) for a review nobody edited. (5) If the reviewer clicks Accept, accept() re-records locked_values(run-file cfg) -> max_records None (config_lock.py:116-135); the next Run-page job restores 50 from ui_settings.json (index.html:1193), writes the overlay again, and the subprocess's Ctx.create raises ConfigChanged -> cli main returns 2 (cli.py:414-420) -> job status 'failed' (server.py:412). Conversely, on a benchmark started from the CLI with max_records: null, any number typed in the form fails the job the same way, exactly as the issue says. Members 1 and 2 are real but minor: /api/run passes the body straight to submit (1150-1151) and the UI only ever sends run/pdfs/sync/eval, so a bad action needs a hand-crafted request; it is queued with label=action (label_for fallback, 163-165), the worker writes the overlay, _cmd raises ValueError(a) (381), the except at 414-415 records status 'failed' with error = the action name. ui_job_config.yaml is overwritten per job (346-347) and spec['cmd'] (400) references it, shown in the job box (index.html:1356) and as the log's first line (405); there is no re-run-from-history control (jobs table offers only toggleLog/cancelJob, index.html:1697-1710), and the model actually used is kept per job in jobs.jsonl (_record, 141-149), so this is a provenance-readability concern, not a wrong re-run.

Evidence:
- `sragent/ui/server.py:342` — _write_job_config: over['pipeline'] = {'max_records': int(spec['max_records'])} when the form sent a number; overlay written to run_dir/ui_job_config.yaml at 346-347
- `sragent/config_lock.py:27` — LOCKED contains 'pipeline.max_records'; record_if_new (105-113) records it on the first save, check (96-102) raises ConfigChanged on any later difference
- `sragent/context.py:61` — Ctx.create runs config_lock.check whenever check_config=True — both in the job subprocess and in App.ctx()
- `sragent/ui/server.py:92` — App.ctx() -> Ctx.create(self.cfg, self.run_dir); self.cfg is load_config(run file) without the job overlay, so a lock recorded from the overlay value trips the UI itself
- `sragent/ui/server.py:1074` — /api/state turns ConfigChanged into config_changed; index.html:599 shows 'Paused: settings changed'
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1239` — 'Max records to screen' shown whenever M.benchmark, regardless of whether the review has started; runWarn (1220) and estimate do not check the lock
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1193` — last max_records restored from ui_settings.json, so the overlay value is re-sent on the next job
- `sragent/cli.py:414` — main catches ConfigChanged and returns 2 -> worker marks the job 'failed' (server.py:412)

*not yet verified · medium · models-config · reasons: R6*

local.py matches `role in e['roles']` (sragent/local.py:284-285) against the client key passed as role: 'agent@<stage>' for stage clients (llm.py:529), 'agent@job' for a chosen job model (525), while `sragent local-endpoints --roles` offers agent/judge/reviewer/judge2 (cli.py:400) and local_endpoints.yaml lists the same four (no verifier). A stage with stage_models.extract on a role-restricted endpoint logs 'Waiting for an available endpoint for agent@extract' indefinitely.

- `sragent/llm.py:529` — client key f'agent@{stage}'; 'agent@job' at 525
