#### I111 · Layering inversions and eval coupling: feedback imports eval.claim2, cli imports ui.configs, claim2 calls private feedback internals and handle_feedback carries eval-only kwargs, eval mutates store internals without versioning, build_C reindexes the wrong store, and the same prompt text serves as in-pipeline check and as the evaluation judge

*confirmed · low · cli-eval · reasons: R5*

Layering and eval coupling as stated: feedback._global_audit imports compact_table from sragent.eval.claim2 (sragent/feedback.py:501); cmd_runs imports run_status from .ui.configs (sragent/cli.py:116); claim2._propagate_into_synthesis calls the private feedback._revise_dependent with tp='corrupt' (sragent/eval/claim2.py:80) and handle_feedback carries the eval-only kwargs case_id/tag_prefix (feedback.py:200-203, used only from claim2.py:486). stagewise.py (42-43, 52, 75-76, 92) and claim2.py (155-157, 194) delete and edit store nodes directly, bypassing upsert's history/version/log and leaving ReviewStore._children stale; build_C then calls st.reindex() on the unmodified source store instead of the edited clone (claim2.py:158). These are eval-only sandbox stores that every consumer re-clones (claim2.py:481) or reloads (402), so the stale index and the wrong-store reindex have no observable effect; they are a maintainability hazard, not a reproducible bug. The same prompt texts serve the pipeline and its evaluation: SYNTH_VERIFY is both the self-check (synthesis.py:151-152, verifier or agent role) and the judge metric whose agreement is reported as 'self_check_vs_judge_agreement' (synthesis_eval.py:24, 41); SOURCE_CHECK and CONSISTENCY run in the agent-side audit (feedback.py:461-463 with pipeline.context_chars; 510-511) and in the claim2 judge (claim2.py:205-206 with a fixed 40000-char budget; 359-361); INJECT_* calls carry tag 'inject', which stage_of_tag maps to the feedback stage model (inject.py:130; config.py:276-283).

Scenario: All cited facts hold in the source. Layering: sragent/feedback.py:501 `from .eval.claim2 import compact_table` inside _global_audit (core imports the evaluation package); sragent/cli.py:116 `from .ui.configs import run_status` inside cmd_runs. Eval coupling: sragent/eval/claim2.py:80 calls the private feedback._revise_dependent(c, cs, sid, src_id, before, after, tp='corrupt'); handle_feedback (feedback.py:200-203) carries case_id/tag_prefix whose only caller is claim2.py:486 (case_id -> cs id at 211, tag_prefix -> tag at 220). Store mutation without upsert: eval/stagewise.py:42-43 and 75-76 `del st.nodes[nid]`, 52 `n['data'] = d`, 92 `item['value'] = NOT_VERIFIED`; claim2.py:155 and 194 `c.store.nodes.pop(...)` and 156-157 rewrite syn depends_on directly. None of these refresh ReviewStore._children (store.py:124-135), add history or bump version; but all are eval sandbox stores produced by clone() (store.py:255-260 rebuilds the index) and every later consumer clones again (_sub_ctx at claim2.py:57-62 and 481) or loads from disk (claim2.py:402, ReviewStore.load), so the stale index is never consulted. build_C (claim2.py:148-161) edits c.store (a clone) but calls st.reindex() on ctx.store, the unmodified source: a no-op on the source and no repair of the clone; because case['R1'] is only read with .get()/.nodes in score_case (241-311) and cloned before any feedback run (481), there is no observable effect. Prompt sharing: synthesis.verify_one (stages/synthesis.py:148-152) runs prompts.SYNTH_VERIFY on checker(ctx) (verifier role if configured else the agent role, 131-134) and stores the result as data['verification']; eval/synthesis_eval.py:24 runs the identical SYNTH_VERIFY text on the judge and line 41 reports 'self_check_vs_judge_agreement' from the two. prompts.SOURCE_CHECK is used in the agent-side audit at feedback.py:461-463 (verifier, packed(text, context_budget(sb)) = pipeline.context_chars) and by the claim2 judge at claim2.py:205-206 (packed(text, 40000)); prompts.CONSISTENCY at feedback.py:510-511 (sb.agent, tag f'{tp}_audit') and claim2.py:359-361 (judge). inject_synthesis (eval/inject.py:127-130) calls ctx.agent.chat(..., tag='inject'); stage_of_tag('inject') falls through to 'feedback' (config.py:276-283), so the planted errors are produced by the feedback stage model.

Evidence:
- `sragent/feedback.py:501` — _global_audit imports compact_table from .eval.claim2
- `sragent/cli.py:116` — cmd_runs imports run_status from .ui.configs
- `sragent/eval/claim2.py:80` — feedback._revise_dependent(..., tp='corrupt'); handle_feedback(case_id=sub, tag_prefix=...) only at claim2.py:486
- `sragent/feedback.py:200` — handle_feedback signature has case_id (used at 211) and tag_prefix (used at 220); no other caller passes them
- `sragent/eval/stagewise.py:42` — del st.nodes[nid] (42-43, 75-76); n['data']=d (52); item['value']=NOT_VERIFIED (92); no reindex/upsert
- `sragent/eval/claim2.py:154` — c.store.nodes.pop (155), syn depends_on rewritten (156-157), then st.reindex() on the source ctx.store (158); build_E pops at 194
- `sragent/eval/claim2.py:481` — every condition runs on _sub_ctx(ctx, case['R1'], sub) which clones (store.clone() reindexes, store.py:255-260), so R1's stale index is never used
- `sragent/store.py:124` — _index/reindex maintain _children; upsert adds history and version (76-107); direct dict edits bypass all of it

#### I97 · Config library inconsistencies: Library.save stores run files that fail semantic checks, set_stage_models rewrites the whole roles block and writes explicit nulls, roles.* keys and efforts are never validated, and the New-review form cannot create benchmark/synergy topics

*confirmed · low · ui-backend · reasons: R8*

Real, low: keys and reasoning_effort values under roles.* (and llm_defaults) are never validated — _resolve_stage_models (config.py:286-317) covers stage_models only, role_cfg (212-215) merges roles[role] blindly, and _check_run's unknown-key warning covers only pipeline/feedback/eval/local (configs.py:24, 268-272) — so a typo such as roles.verifier.reasoning_efort is silently ignored and an invalid effort value fails only at the provider call (llm.py:387-398, 431-432). Cosmetic: every save from the Run page's Models-per-stage table rewrites the whole roles: block through set_block (server.py:796-810, configs.py:388-408), dropping comments, anchors and null role entries, though all dict values are preserved. Not a defect: an explicit `reasoning_effort: null` in stage_models is the intended encoding of the table's 'model default' choice, which overrides the agent's effort via deep_merge (config.py:131-138) and round-trips through stage_rows (server.py:741). Design, with visible recovery: Library.save writes a file that fails semantic checks ('saved with N problem(s) to fix', configs.py:352-357); the open App's next reload then fails, which /api/state reports as config_error and the UI shows as 'Run file does not load' with a Fix-it button (server.py:1077-1079, index.html:1838-1839). Design limitation: the New-review form and new_topic create review/ topics with pubmed or file records only (configs.py:494, 520; index.html:2113); benchmark topics are hand-written (README:119) and benchmark run files come from new_run (configs.py:471).

Scenario: Mixed: one member holds as a real (silent) gap, one is cosmetic, one is designed behaviour with visible recovery, and one claim is wrong. (A) Unvalidated roles.* — confirmed. _resolve_stage_models (config.py:286-317) checks unknown keys and EFFORTS only for stage_models; role_cfg (212-215) is `_with_model(cfg, llm_defaults, roles.get(role, {}))` with no key check; _check_run (configs.py:268-275) checks unknown keys only for SECTIONS = ('pipeline','feedback','eval','local') (line 24), so roles and llm_defaults are never key-checked. Trace: write `roles: {verifier: {model: claude-haiku-5-5, reasoning_efort: high}}` in a run file; the editor's check reports 'Loads with its includes' (index.html:1956); the verifier runs with llm_defaults' effort. Write `reasoning_effort: hgih` instead and the value is handed to the provider at call time (llm.py:387-398 openai/gemini, 431-432 anthropic) and fails there. (B) roles block rewrite — real but cosmetic. The Run page's SM.edit always contains a 'verifier' key (built from M.stages which includes the verifier row, index.html:1194, server.py:750-757), so every 'Save to run file' takes the `ver != '__absent__'` branch (server.py:796-810): roles is rebuilt from safe_load'ed dict entries and re-emitted by set_block (configs.py:388-408) one line per role in flow style; the loop at 404-407 consumes every indented line, so comments inside the block and YAML anchors are lost and null/non-dict role entries are dropped. Values of dict entries (model, pricing, base_url, …) are re-dumped intact, so semantics are preserved. (C) 'writes reasoning_effort: null' — refuted as a defect. smSet sends null for 'model default' (index.html:1294); set_stage_models writes it (789-790); deep_merge (config.py:131-138) copies None over the agent's effort so that stage runs at the model default instead of the agent's low — the documented meaning ('null for the model default', config.py:315); stage_rows (741) round-trips it and the table shows 'model default' (index.html:1280). An explicit null is the only way to express that override. (D) Library.save with problems — designed, with recovery. save blocks only YAML-syntax / 'the file must be' errors and otherwise writes with 'saved with N problem(s) to fix' (configs.py:352-357); the editor shows 'Saved, with problems to fix' and the error list (index.html:2022-2028, 1953); ws.changed() marks apps stale (server.py:943-947); the next ws.get -> app.reload -> _load -> load_config raises (86-89, 940), /api/state catches it as config_error (1077-1079) and the UI shows 'Run file does not load' with a 'Fix it' button (index.html:599-601, 1838-1839). reload() is skipped while a job runs (88-89), so a running job is unaffected. (E) new_topic scope — design limitation. new_topic writes under review/ and accepts pubmed/file only (configs.py:494, 520), matching the 'New review' form which offers only those two sources (index.html:2113); benchmark run files for an existing benchmark topic are created by new_run/run_text (configs.py:471, 480-487); configs/README.md:119 points to the hand-written benchmark/emicizumab_pk/topic.yaml as the benchmark template; the warning at 307-308 only fires for a hand-edited review topic.

Evidence:
- `sragent/config.py:286` — _resolve_stage_models validates unknown keys (304) and reasoning_effort against EFFORTS (311-315) for stage_models only
- `sragent/config.py:212` — role_cfg merges roles[role] over llm_defaults with no key or effort check
- `sragent/ui/configs.py:24` — SECTIONS = pipeline, feedback, eval, local — _check_run's unknown-key warning (268-272) never covers roles or llm_defaults
- `sragent/llm.py:431` — reasoning_effort is passed through to the provider unchecked (also 387-398), so a bad value fails at call time
- `sragent/ui/server.py:796` — roles rebuilt from dict entries and re-emitted wholesale via set_block whenever the table sends a verifier key (always: index.html:1194)
- `sragent/ui/configs.py:404` — set_block drops every indented line of the old block (comments, anchors) and writes one flow-style line per entry; values are kept
- `sragent/ui/server.py:789` — reasoning_effort: null written for a stage = the UI's 'model default' (index.html:1294); deep_merge (config.py:131-138) applies None over the agent's effort — intended, documented at config.py:315
- `sragent/ui/configs.py:352` — save blocks only syntax errors and otherwise writes with 'saved with N problem(s) to fix'

### Controls that exist for some stages only (7)

#### I30 · 'Run only this stage' exists for exactly one stage (rob); every other stage has an implicit, different 'done' test; the only per-stage on/off is skip_stages in YAML, split across two keys for snowball and ignored by the estimate

*confirmed · high · pipeline · reasons: R1, R5, R7*

The only whole-stage redo is 'Redraft all risk-of-bias assessments' (index.html:759-760, To-do page, gated by judging()) -> /api/run reassess_rob -> rob.reassess_all (rob.py:193-226), which is also the only stage code that turns cache reads off. pipeline.run (pipeline.py:135-155) always walks REGISTRY from plan: _plan re-validates key concepts (through the HTTP disk cache) and rewrites protocol.yaml every run (planning.py:41-48); _search runs only when the node is absent (17); screen_all skips records that have a screen node (screening.py:438-439); fulltext_review only needs_fulltext_review records (505); extract_all/rob_all skip existing nodes unless source_version moved (extraction.py:364; rob.py:185-186); _synthesize writes only when no active synthesis exists, else runs the update engine (pipeline.py:56-73). The Run page offers run/pdfs/sync (+eval) only (index.html:1229-1232). skip_stages (config.py:113; 186-188 refuses plan/screen/report) has no dedicated UI control (stageCard 1272-1290 is model+effort; it can only be typed into the raw YAML editor on Configs), snowball has a second switch pipeline.citation_chasing (config.py:107, snowball.py:30), estimate.remaining_calls never reads skip_stages (estimate.py:67-140, while honouring citation_chasing at 108), and steps.py has no skipped-stage status.

Scenario: 1. Whole-stage redo: the only one is the To-do page button 'Redraft all risk-of-bias assessments' (index.html:759-760, shown only when k('rob').length && judging()) -> redraftRob (1372-1380) -> POST /api/run {action:'reassess_rob'} -> server._cmd 'reassess-rob' (server.py:359-360) -> rob.reassess_all (rob.py:193-226), which is also the only stage code that sets cache_read=False (211-213). No equivalent exists for plan/search/screen/fulltext/snowball/extract/synthesize (grep: cache_read only in rob.py and eval/run_all.py; actions.rerun 281-283 is per study). 2. pipeline.run (pipeline.py:135-155) iterates REGISTRY from 'plan' every time; each wrapper decides 'done' differently: _plan always runs planning.plan, which re-validates key concepts through terms.validate (planning.py:41-46; HTTP goes through sources/http.py's disk cache, so network only on a miss) and rewrites protocol.yaml unconditionally (47-48); _search runs only if store['search'] is None (17); screen_all screens records with no screen: node (screening.py:438-439); fulltext_review only needs_fulltext_review records (505); extract_all / rob_all only missing nodes or source_changed (extraction.py:364; rob.py:185-186); _synthesize writes only when no active synthesis node exists, else runs engine.execute as an update (pipeline.py:56-73). 3. The Run page's 'What to run' list is run/pdfs/sync (+eval on benchmarks) (index.html:1229-1232); stageCard (1272-1290) edits model/effort per LLM_STAGES row only; grep 'skip_stages' in index.html: 0 hits, so the only way to set it from the UI is the raw YAML textarea on the Configs page (index.html:1895, configs.py:343). 4. config.py:113 declares skip_stages; load_config refuses plan/screen/report (186-188) but allows search, fulltext, snowball, extract, rob, synthesize. Snowball has a second, independent switch pipeline.citation_chasing (config.py:107; snowball.py:30-31 returns {'skipped': True}). 5. With skip_stages: [rob] in the run file, estimate.remaining_calls (estimate.py:67-140) still adds calls['rob'] for every included study (128-131) because it never reads skip_stages (grep 'skip' in estimate.py: 0 hits), while it does honour citation_chasing at 108; the Run page estimate therefore over-counts. steps.py (full file, 63 lines) has no notion of a skipped stage, so a skipped rob stage shows as 'to do' forever.

Evidence:
- `sragent/pipeline.py:139` — run(): for stage in REGISTRY, every wrapper up to `until` is invoked unless in skip_stages
- `sragent/pipeline.py:17` — _search only when store['search'] is None
- `sragent/pipeline.py:62` — _synthesize: synthesize only when no active synthesis; else engine.execute update
- `sragent/stages/planning.py:41` — terms re-validated every run (via sources/http.py cache) and protocol.yaml rewritten at 47-48
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:438` — screen_all: only records with no screen: node
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:505` — fulltext_review: only needs_fulltext_review records
- `sragent/stages/extraction.py:364` — extract_all: missing ext: node or source_changed
- `sragent/stages/rob.py:185` — rob_all: missing rob: node or source_changed; reassess_all at 193-226 is the single whole-stage redo and the only stage that disables cache reads (211-213)

#### I07 · Synthesis statement edits create no 'Your edits' to-do or propagate path, and the pipeline freezes an edited synthesis while 'Update now' revises around it

*confirmed · medium · extraction-rob-synthesis · reasons: R4, R2*

actions.edit kind=syn (sragent/actions.py:199-219) never calls _mark_dependents and _dependents (74-82) handles only ext:/rob: ids, so an edited statement gets no manual_edit flag, no 'Your edits' to-do (todos.py:225-239) and no 'edited:' answer — but this is not by itself a defect: a statement has no agent-made dependents (only the report, re-rendered at 223-224). The agent can still be asked to revise the same statement from three places — the flag card (todos.py:198-200, 468-474), the HTML report comment box (report.py:386-391 -> /api/comment) and the study page comment (index.html:1607-1612, 1662) — where only the study page passes a chosen model; the other two fall back to server.submit's _last_models() (server.py:317-318). The real asymmetry: pipeline._synthesize (pipeline.py:59-61) runs nothing when any statement is edited and something changed, while sync.sync_dependents (sync.py:92) runs engine.execute, which keeps the pinned statement for stale 'revise' items (engine.py:109-110) but not for 'integrate': engine._synthesis (425-429) -> feedback._include_study (611-613) -> synthesis.write_statements (56-80) upserts any statement the integrate model reworded with a data dict that drops 'edited' (71, 75-77), so a human edit can be silently overwritten on 'Update now'.

Scenario: (1) Reviewer edits statement syn:S3 on the study page (index.html:1636 editSyn -> 1646-1647 POST /api/edit kind=syn). actions.edit kind=syn (actions.py:199-219) upserts the node with data['edited']={...}, resolves open synthesis_verify flags (216-218) and re-renders the report (223-224); it never calls _mark_dependents, and _dependents (74-82) only knows ext:/rob: ids, so no manual_edit flag and no 'Your edits' to-do (todos.py:225-239 builds those only from manual_edit flags). This part is correct behaviour rather than a defect: a statement has no agent-made dependents (graph.py:46: report depends on syn:*), and the report is re-rendered immediately. (2) The reviewer can still ask the agent to revise S3 from three entry points: the flag card (todos.py:198-200 'fix' -> apply 468-474 -> job {'action':'feedback','target':'syn:S3'}), the HTML report comment box (report.py:386-391 _fbbox -> fb() at 324-326 -> /api/comment send=true -> server.comment 704-721 -> feedback job), and the study page comment (index.html:1607-1612 targets include every syn:* node; 1662 POST /api/comment with agent: ST.rr.agent||defaultAgent()). Model source: only the study page passes a chosen model; the other two carry no agent and server.submit (server.py:317-318) fills in _last_models(), so it is 'one place picks a model, two use the last-used default', not three different sources. (3) Asymmetry: after an edit, `sragent run` reaches pipeline._synthesize; with human_edited true and state['changed']>0 or considered != included (59-60) it only logs 'not re-written (use Update outputs)' (61) and runs no engine at all, whereas 'Update now' -> sync.sync_dependents (sync.py:92) -> engine.execute, where plan() marks the edited (pinned, graph.human_pinned checks 'edited') statement 'keep' (engine.py:109-110) and revises the rest. (4) In that same 'Update now' run, a newly included study yields an 'integrate' item (engine.py:148-150) -> _synthesis 425-429 -> feedback._include_study -> SYNTH_INTEGRATE sees statements_json (id/text/cites/scope only, synthesis.py:220-222) and returns all statements; write_statements (synthesis.py:56-80) upserts any whose text/cites differ with a fresh data dict {id,text,cites,scope} (71) — no check for old['data']['edited'] — so a reworded S3 silently replaces the human's text and drops the 'edited' mark (the pin). The 'keep' rule in plan() does not cover this path.

Evidence:
- `sragent/actions.py:199` — edit kind=syn: upsert with d['edited'] (211, 214-215), resolve synthesis_verify flags (216-218); no _mark_dependents call, unlike ext (160) and rob (196-197)
- `sragent/actions.py:74` — _dependents handles only ext:/rob: ids; a synthesis statement has no agent-made dependents (graph.py:46 report only), report re-rendered at 223-224
- `sragent/pipeline.py:59` — human_edited and (changed or considered != included) -> log only, no engine.execute
- `sragent/sync.py:92` — sync_dependents always runs engine.execute(tag='sync')
- `sragent/engine.py:109` — plan: pinned synthesis (data['edited']) -> 'keep' for stale revise items only
- `sragent/synthesis.py:56` — write_statements: upsert when text/cites/scope differ (75-77), data rebuilt without 'edited' (71); no edited guard
- `sragent/feedback.py:611` — _include_study -> write_statements on the integrate model's full statement list; called by engine._synthesis 425-429
- `sragent/todos.py:198` — synthesis_verify flag card option 'fix' -> feedback job on the statement (468-474)

#### I26 · Per-study re-run semantics differ by step: screen replaces the human decision and never shows the earlier note, extract keeps edited fields by code, rob returns a confirmed draft unchanged

*confirmed · medium · human-loop · reasons: R5, R3*

Per-study re-run semantics differ by step. screen: actions.rerun calls screen_one(..., keep_human=False) (actions.py:288), so the proposal carries the model's decision with only cs['explanation'] = 'Note: this replaces your earlier manual decision (<decision>)' (297-298); the human_decision.note stored by todos.apply (todos.py:443) is never put in the prompt (screen_one's prompt at screening.py:203-208 plus ctx.note at context.py:115-120 carries only the new note) nor shown in the proposal card. extract: extract_one re-asks the model but keep_human_edits (extraction.py:321, 342-351) re-attaches every edited field with the fresh value only as agent_value; feedback._changed (194-197) ignores agent_value, so an edited field never counts as changed and, when nothing else changed, the whole result is dropped as 'no_change'. rob: rob_one returns a human-confirmed draft unchanged with a sandbox-only source_changed flag and no model call (rob.py:22-31), so the re-run job completes as a 'no_change' proposal at near-zero cost (not 'paid for'); the sandbox flag is lost. The study page offers the same 'Re-run this step' control for all three without checking for a confirmed RoB draft (index.html:1519-1527) and /api/rerun and actions.rerun have no guard either. GUIDE.md:306 describes the screen behaviour ('only a re-run you ask for replaces it, as a proposal') and the extract/rob behaviour ('values you edited and ratings you confirmed survive a re-extraction or re-assessment') but not that the re-run control does nothing for a confirmed RoB draft.

Scenario: Setup: the reviewer decided screen:R1 = include via the To-do eligibility table with note 'Table 2 shows adults only' (todos.apply, todos.py:441-446: human_decision = {decision, note, reason, ts}). (a) Step 'screen': on the study page the reviewer picks 'Screening (eligibility)' under 'Re-run this step' (index.html:1519-1527), types a new note, clicks Re-run -> /api/rerun -> job 'sragent rerun' -> actions.rerun: sb = ctx with store clone and note=<new note> (actions.py:280); screening.screen_one(sb, protocol, rid, stage, tag_suffix='_rerun', keep_human=False) (288). screen_one builds prompts.SCREEN from criteria, synonyms, record text and stage only (screening.py:203-208); ctx.msgs appends only ctx.note, the new note (context.py:115-120). With keep_human=False the branch at screening.py:235-246 that re-attaches human_decision is skipped, so the sandbox node has the model's decision and no human_decision. rerun then sets cs['explanation'] = 'Note: this replaces your earlier manual decision (include).' (actions.py:297-298) - the earlier note is neither in the prompt nor in the proposal card (todos.py:306-320 shows 'You asked: ...' / 'Model: ... explanation'). The change set is a proposal the reviewer accepts or rejects, as GUIDE.md:306 says. (b) Step 'extract': the reviewer edited ext:R1.sample_size to '64' (actions.edit sets item['edited'], 146-147), then re-runs extraction. extraction.extract_one calls the model, then keep_human_edits(st.get('ext:R1'), fields) (321, def 342-351) replaces the fresh item with the person's item plus agent_value/agent_evidence. feedback._changed compares only value and evidence per field (feedback.py:194-197, editable 79-82), so the edited field never counts as changed; if no other field changed, cs['changes'] is empty, _finish sets status 'no_change' (actions.py:249-250) and the sandbox, with the fresh agent_value, is discarded. If other fields changed, accepting the proposal stores the person's value with agent_value beside it; the fresh value never becomes the value. (c) Step 'rob': the reviewer confirmed rob:R1 in the To-do (todos.py:514-518 sets human_confirmed) and later clicks 'Re-run' with 'Risk of bias' (offered whenever d.extraction and group included, index.html:1519). actions.rerun -> rob.rob_one(sb, rid): old['data'].get('human_confirmed') is truthy, so it upserts the same data with a refreshed source_version, adds a 'source_changed' flag in the sandbox and returns without any model call (rob.py:22-31). _changed on rob compares domains/judgments/rationale/overall (feedback.py:87-90) -> unchanged -> cs status 'no_change' (actions.py:249-250), the sandbox flag is dropped with the sandbox. The job runs, costs nothing in model calls (cost_usd ~0), and the reviewer receives a 'no_change' proposal explaining '0 output(s) would change.' There is no guard on the study page or in /api/rerun (server.py:1177-1185) or actions.rerun (263-266) for a confirmed draft; the only route to a fresh assessment of a confirmed draft is the 'Ask the agent to re-assess' feedback option.

Evidence:
- `sragent/actions.py:288` — screening.screen_one(sb, ..., tag_suffix='_rerun', keep_human=False); explanation 'this replaces your earlier manual decision' at 297-298 (not 297 alone)
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:203` — SCREEN prompt has criteria/synonyms/record/stage only; the keep_human branch 235-246 is skipped when keep_human=False
- `sragent/context.py:115` — msgs() appends only self.note (the new re-run note) as REVIEWER NOTE
- `sragent/todos.py:443` — human_decision = {decision, note, reason, ts}; the note is never read by actions.rerun or screen_one
- `sragent/stages/extraction.py:321` — kept = keep_human_edits(st.get(f'ext:{rid}'), fields); def at 342-351 keeps the person's item and adds agent_value
- `sragent/feedback.py:194` — _changed compares editable() = value+evidence per field (79-82), so a kept edited field never registers as changed
- `sragent/stages/rob.py:22` — human_confirmed -> upsert same data with new source_version, flag source_changed, return; no model call
- `sragent/actions.py:249` — _finish: no changes -> cs['status'] = 'no_change'; sandbox (and its flag) discarded

#### I27 · Per-study re-run covers screen/extract/rob only; 'screen' silently means full-text when a full text exists, is priced as full-text, forces effort 'low', and the UI cannot pass --cached

*confirmed · medium · ui-backend · reasons: R5, R7*

Per-study re-run covers only screen/extract/rob (actions.py:25; server.py:1178; index.html:1521). A 'Screening (eligibility)' re-run silently runs at full text whenever the stored screen is at full_text or an ft node of kind fulltext exists (actions.py:271-273), and App.estimate prices every screen re-run as screen_full_text (server.py:271). The study-page picker sets effort 'low' on every change with no effort control (index.html:1403; defaultAgent 1394-1397 may inherit a Run-page effort initially), and since job_agent overrides stage_models for the whole job (llm.py:521-525), the stage table's efforts (1279-1284) and the Run page's low|default (1249) do not apply. _cmd (server.py:366-370) never passes --cached, which only the CLI offers (cli.py:338). There is no per-study re-fetch (review_one(refetch=True) only in sync.py:27; rerun fetches only when no ft node exists) and no per-statement re-synthesis step; plan and search have their own revise/refine endpoints (/api/protocol, /api/search_strategy) but no re-run step.

Scenario: On a study page, the 'Ask the agent' card offers only screen/extract/rob (index.html:1521, from RERUN_STEPS in actions.py:25; /api/rerun validates the same three at server.py:1178). The reviewer picks 'Screening (eligibility)' for a study that has an ft node of kind 'fulltext': actions.rerun (271-273) sets stage='full_text' and pstage='fulltext' and runs screen_one at full text; nothing on the card says so. The study page estimate (studyEst 1651-1654 -> /api/estimate action=rerun) is priced by server.estimate as {'screen': 'screen_full_text'} unconditionally (271), so a title/abstract re-run is priced as a full-text call. modelPicker (1401-1405) initialises from defaultAgent() (1394-1397, which may inherit a Run-page effort) but every change sets effort:'low' and there is no effort control on the card; the job config writes job_agent from that selection (_write_job_config 334-335) and LLMHub.agent_for (llm.py:521-525) uses job_agent for every call of the job, so the stage table's per-stage effort (1279-1284) and the Run page's low|default choice (1249) do not apply. _cmd (366-370) emits `rerun --rid --step [--note] [--no-propagate]` and never `--cached`, which only the CLI has (cli.py:338). screening.review_one(refetch=True) is called only from sync.process_pdfs (sync.py:27; grep shows no other refetch caller), and actions.rerun fetches only when no ft node exists (278-279), so there is no per-study re-fetch; no per-statement re-synthesis step exists in RERUN_STEPS.

Evidence:
- `sragent/actions.py:25` — RERUN_STEPS = screen/extract/rob
- `sragent/actions.py:271` — stage = full_text if stored screen stage is full_text or ft kind == fulltext; pstage at 273
- `sragent/ui/server.py:271` — estimate action=rerun maps 'screen' -> 'screen_full_text' unconditionally
- `sragent/ui/server.py:1178` — /api/rerun accepts only screen/extract/rob
- `sragent/ui/server.py:366` — _cmd rerun branch: no --cached
- `sragent/cli.py:338` — rerun --cached exists only on the CLI
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1403` — modelPicker onchange sets effort:'low'; defaultAgent 1394-1397; no effort control on the study card (1521-1527)
- `sragent/llm.py:521` — agent_for: job_agent overrides stage_models for every call of the job

#### I28 · CLI-only and UI-only operations: every human decision exists only in the UI, verifier passes and flags exist only on the CLI, and /api/propagate and /api/job are dead endpoints

*confirmed · medium · cli-eval · reasons: R4, R7*

Several human-triggered operations exist on exactly one side. CLI-only (sragent/cli.py:295-411): run --plan-only/--fresh, revalidate-terms, rescreen, feedback --pmid/--local-edit/--accept, rerun --cached, eval --allow-cache/--from-run/--label/--resume, eval-audit, eval-summary, fetch-files, reassess-rob --rids, and the standalone verifier passes verify-screening and verify-rob (the UI's reassess_rob job and the pipeline's verify_rob setting still run the verifier, but the standalone passes have no UI entry); App._cmd (sragent/ui/server.py:350-381) passes only --text/--target/--no-verify for feedback and --suite/--n-cases for eval. UI-only: every todos.apply answer (/api/answer), actions.edit (/api/edit), todos.bulk, comments, /api/protocol, /api/search_strategy, /api/data_items, /api/stage_models, /api/stop and /api/cancel (server.py:1135-1192) have no CLI subcommand. tests/test_golden.py exercises todos.apply/actions.edit directly and tests/test_offline.py:253-277 exercises App.answer once at object level; the HTTP request handler itself is untested. POST /api/propagate (server.py:1186) and GET /api/job (1083) are never called from index.html. Propagation after a manual edit is reachable in the UI only through the `edited:` answer (todos.py:498-500); the live answer path, the dead endpoint and the CLI all converge on `sragent propagate` -> actions.propagate_from -> llm.agent_for, and differ only in the model: the UI paths inject a job_agent override from the last UI-picked agent (server.py:318, 337; the dead endpoint also accepts an explicit agent), while the bare CLI uses stage_models.feedback (config.py:276-283).

Scenario: CLI-only: the subparsers at sragent/cli.py:295-411 define run --plan-only/--fresh (298-300), revalidate-terms (316), rescreen (318), feedback --pmid/--local-edit/--no-verify/--accept (323-328), rerun --cached (336), eval --allow-cache/--from-run/--label/--resume (351-360), eval-audit (361-364), verify-screening (366-370), verify-rob (372-374), reassess-rob --rids (376-378), fetch-files (380-381) and eval-summary (383-387). The UI can only start what App._cmd builds (sragent/ui/server.py:350-381): run (--until), pdfs, sync, reassess-rob (no --rids), feedback (--text/--target/--no-verify), rerun (--rid/--step/--note/--no-propagate), propagate (--node/--from-version/--note) and eval (--suite/--n-cases); nothing in index.html references verify-screening, verify-rob, fetch-files, revalidate or eval-audit (grep: 0 hits apart from a hint text at 760). UI-only: POST /api/answer, /api/bulk, /api/edit, /api/comment, /api/comment_resolve, /api/protocol, /api/search_strategy, /api/data_items, /api/stage_models, /api/stop, /api/cancel (server.py:1135-1192) have no subcommand in cli.py:295-411. Dead endpoints: POST /api/propagate (server.py:1186-1189) and GET /api/job (1083-1084) are never requested by index.html (grep for '/api/propagate' and exact '/api/job' returns 0; '/api/jobs' is a different route). Propagation reaches the agent only via the `edited:<node>:<flag>` answer with option 'update' (todos.py:498-500 -> job {'action':'propagate', target, from_version, text, rid}) which App.answer submits with {**_last_models(), **job} (server.py:499); submit() (315-325) also fills _last_models() into any spec without 'agent' (318), and _write_job_config (337) turns spec['agent'] into cfg['job_agent'] because 'propagate' is not in STAGE_JOBS (32). The subprocess runs `sragent propagate` -> cmd_propagate -> actions.propagate_from (actions.py:310-335) -> ctx.agent (StageAgent, context.py:15-27) -> hub.agent_for (llm.py:521-528): job_agent if present, else stage_cfg(stage_of_tag('propagate_...')) which is the feedback stage (config.py:276-283). So all three entry points converge on the same CLI command; they differ only in whether a job_agent override (the UI's last-picked agent, or the dead endpoint's explicit agent) replaces stage_models.feedback. Tests: tests/test_golden.py calls actions.edit/todos.apply directly (266, 280, 295, 304, 341); tests/test_offline.py:253-277 instantiates App and calls app.state/estimate/models and app.answer once (protocol approve); no test touches the HTTP request handler (grep for make_handler/do_POST/urlopen in tests: none).

Evidence:
- `sragent/cli.py:298` — run --plan-only/--fresh; feedback --pmid/--local-edit/--no-verify/--accept at 323-328; rerun --cached at 336; eval --allow-cache/--from-run/--label/--resume at 351-360
- `sragent/cli.py:366` — verify-screening (366-370), verify-rob (372-374), reassess-rob --rids (376-378), fetch-files (380-381), eval-summary (383-387); no answer/edit/bulk/comment/protocol/stop subcommand anywhere in 295-411
- `sragent/ui/server.py:350` — App._cmd builds only run/pdfs/sync/reassess_rob/feedback(--text,--target,--no-verify)/rerun/propagate/eval(--suite,--n-cases)
- `sragent/ui/server.py:1186` — POST /api/propagate handler; GET /api/job at 1083; index.html has no request to either (grep 0)
- `sragent/ui/server.py:318` — submit(): _last_models() merged into any spec without 'agent'; _write_job_config sets cfg['job_agent'] from spec['agent'] for non-STAGE_JOBS (337)
- `sragent/todos.py:498` — edited:<node>:<flag> option 'update' -> job {'action':'propagate', target=node_id, from_version, text, rid}
- `sragent/llm.py:521` — agent_for: job_agent overrides every call; otherwise stage_cfg(stage) with stage_of_tag('propagate_*') == 'feedback' (config.py:276-283)
- `tests/test_offline.py:277` — app.answer({'todo_id':'protocol','option':'approve'}) is tested at App level; no test of the HTTP handler class

#### I92 · The rule 'flag with a reason, verifier, person only if unsettled' is implemented for two of nine check points; reassess after a protocol change skips the second reading and shows only the changed criteria; synthesis repair exists only in synthesize() and the feedback path uses a different repair prompt; defaults are off while the new-run form writes flagged/pending

*confirmed · medium · extraction-rob-synthesis · reasons: R9*

The rule 'flag with a reason -> verifier -> person only if unsettled' is implemented in full only for risk of bias (sragent/stages/rob.py:51-57, 82-92, when verify_rob is 'flagged') and for the extraction eligibility doubt (extraction.py:211-291). Screening verifies by verify_screening mode and decision class (screening.py:308-318) — 'pending' is reason-like for undecided records, but TA exclusions only under 'all'. Extraction values have no verifier: 'check none'/'uncovered' are markers and term_validation flags go straight to the person (333-339; todos.py:205-208). screening.reassess (254-305; engine.py:334-335) never calls verify_screen and prompts only the changed criteria, so any_of siblings are absent from the prompt and from settle's group rule (decide() still uses the merged set). synthesize() verifies every statement, repairs once with SYNTH_REPAIR (which may retire) and re-verifies (synthesis.py:116-119, 174, 194); the update engine only verify_one's touched statements with no repair (engine.py:433-436), and the feedback path repairs with prompts.REPAIR (feedback.py:489), which returns the same schema and cannot retire explicitly, run whenever changes exist and post_check is on regardless of verify (238). 'reassess' (screening.py:301-304), 'source_changed' (rob.py:28-30) and 'manual_edit' (actions.py:93-94) flags go straight to the person. config defaults verify_rob and verify_screening to 'off' (config.py:97, 100) while the New-review form writes 'pending'/'flagged' for human_in_the_loop runs (ui/configs.py:465-467).

Scenario: RoB: rob_one computes reasons_of (rob.py:51, 64-79), calls _verify only when verify_rob == 'flagged' (52-53, 91-92), and needs_person (82-88) sends the draft to a person only when the verifier did not settle it ('agreed'/'decided_by_verifier') — the rule as stated. Eligibility doubt: extract_one -> recheck_eligibility -> verifier -> 'awaiting' for a person only on disagreement (extraction.py:259-275). Screening: _verify_wanted (screening.py:308-318) keys on pipeline.verify_screening and the decision class ('awaiting' under pending/full_text/all, any full-text decision under full_text/all, title/abstract only exclusions under all), not on a reason; the 'pending' mode is reason-like (undecided -> verifier -> person) but no other screening decision carries a reason. Extraction: no verifier for values; 'check none' and 'uncovered' are markers (333-335) and not_found terms become term_validation flags straight to the person (336-339; todos.py:205-208 card). reassess (screening.py:254-305): sub-protocol with only crit_ids (261), criteria_text shows the 'any of group X' label (planning.py:187) but the siblings are not in the prompt, settle's any_of rule runs on the sub members only (70-98), decide() then uses the merged full set (281-282); verify_screen is never called; engine._screen passes only i['criteria'] (engine.py:334-335). Synthesis: synthesize() verifies all, repairs once and re-verifies (synthesis.py:116-119, 194) with SYNTH_REPAIR, which may retire (174, 181-183); engine._synthesis only verify_one's touched statements, no repair (engine.py:433-436); the feedback path's _check_and_repair uses prompts.REPAIR (feedback.py:489; prompts.py:597-609, 'revised' in the same schema, no retire key — a retire is possible only indirectly by returning empty cites, apply_revised -> retire_if_uncited 191) and runs whenever changes exist and post_check is on, independent of the verify flag (238). Flags 'reassess' (screening.py:301-304), 'source_changed' (rob.py:28-30) and 'manual_edit' (actions.py:93-94) are raised with no verifier step. Defaults: config.py:97 verify_rob 'off', 100 verify_screening 'off'; the New-review form writes verify_screening: pending and verify_rob: flagged (configs.py:466-467, not 464-466) only when human_mode != agent_only (465).

Evidence:
- `sragent/stages/rob.py:51` — reasons_of -> _verify when verify_rob=='flagged' (52-53, 91-92); needs_person 82-88
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:308` — _verify_wanted by verify_screening mode and decision class (308-318)
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:254` — reassess: sub-protocol with changed ids only (261), settle on the sub (268), decide on the merged set (281-282), no verify_screen; engine.py:334-335 passes i['criteria']
- `sragent/stages/extraction.py:333` — check none / uncovered are markers; term_validation flags straight to the person (336-339; todos.py:205-208)
- `sragent/engine.py:433` — update engine: verify_one on touched statements only, no repair; synthesize() verifies, repairs, re-verifies (synthesis.py:116-119, 194)
- `sragent/feedback.py:489` — _check_and_repair uses prompts.REPAIR ('revised' same schema, no retire key) vs SYNTH_REPAIR (synthesis.py:174, may retire); post-check gated on post_check only (238)
- `sragent/config.py:97` — verify_rob 'off' (97), verify_screening 'off' (100)
- `sragent/ui/configs.py:466` — run_text writes verify_screening: pending (466) and verify_rob: flagged (467) when human_mode != agent_only (465)

#### I93 · 'Insist' skips the feedback verifier but not the post-check, so the agent can rewrite what the person insisted on; the feedback verifier both judges and rewrites, then the same role re-checks its own revision

*not yet verified · medium · graph-engine · reasons: R9*

The To-do 'insist' answer resolves the flag with 're-sent without the evidence check' and submits a feedback job with no_verify (sragent/todos.py:509-512; cli.py:174 verify=not args.no_verify); handle_feedback(verify=False) uses FEEDBACK_APPLY (feedback.py:363-368), then, because feedback.post_check defaults True and the condition is cs['changes'] only (238; config.py:124), _check_and_repair runs SOURCE_CHECK by the verifier and REPAIR by the agent (485-495), which can revert the insisted value with no explanation on the card. With verify on, FEEDBACK_VERIFY returns `revised` (prompts.py:388-389) which is applied (349-370), and _check_one uses verifier(sb) again on that revision (461-470), so the post-check is not independent of the reviser; when roles.verifier is unset, writer, verifier and checker are all the feedback stage model.

Evidence:
- `sragent/todos.py:509` — insist -> resolve_flag; job no_verify True
- `sragent/feedback.py:238` — if cs['changes'] and post_check: _check_and_repair — independent of verify
- `sragent/feedback.py:489` — REPAIR by sb.agent on issues; apply_revised writes it
- `sragent/feedback.py:349` — FEEDBACK_VERIFY by verifier returns revised; _check_one uses verifier again (461-470)

### Model and role resolution (7)

#### I21 · The last Run-page agent silently becomes the model (job_agent) of every later feedback/propagate/rerun/comment/To-do job that names none; job_agent is an undocumented key; docs say a picked model applies to that job only

*not yet verified · high · models-config · reasons: R6*

submit() fills `agent` from ui_settings.json for any job without one (sragent/ui/server.py:317-318); ui_settings.json is written by every run/pdfs/sync/eval submission (320-322) from runOpts.agent, which index.html always seeds with effort 'low' (1184-1190) even for stage jobs where the select is hidden (1247). To-do answers and drained answers merge _last_models() explicitly (499, 518). _write_job_config turns it into `job_agent` for every non-STAGE_JOBS action (335-337), and LLMHub.agent_for then returns the job client for every agent call of the subprocess (llm.py:521-525), overriding stage_models.feedback and the stage models of screening/extraction done inside a feedback job (feedback.py:577-585, 642-644). job_agent is absent from DEFAULTS and the docs (config.py; configs.py:24 RUN_KEYS would flag it). configs/README.md:151 says a model picked in the interface applies to that job only; GUIDE 7.1 says sync/pdfs use the last Run-tab model although they are STAGE_JOBS and ignore it.

Evidence:
- `sragent/ui/server.py:317` — if not spec.get('agent'): spec.update(_last_models()); 320-322 ui_settings.json written
- `sragent/ui/server.py:335` — job_agent written when spec.agent and action not in STAGE_JOBS; 499, 518 to-do jobs merge _last_models
- `sragent/llm.py:521` — agent_for: job_agent replaces every stage client
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1184` — runOpts.agent seeded (effort 'low' at 1190); select hidden for STAGE_JOB (1247) yet sent (1337)
- `configs/README.md:151` — 'A model picked ... applies to that job only'

#### I44 · The model a call runs on depends on the entry point, not the stage: pipeline._screen runs the whole update engine under the screen model, snowball screens with its own model, eval builds stages outside ctx.at, and Ctx.map outside a stage takes the feedback stage's concurrency

*confirmed · high · pipeline · reasons: R6*

StageAgent.client returns agent_for(ctx.stage or stage_of_tag(tag)) (sragent/context.py:22-24), so a set ctx.stage overrides every tag. pipeline._screen runs engine.execute(max_rounds=1) with no roots inside ctx.at('screen') (pipeline.py:24-30) when any screen item is stale after a protocol change, so in that round full-text rescreens, extractions of stale or newly included studies, risk-of-bias drafts and statement revisions/integrations/verifications all use stage_models.screen while their tags (screen_full_text_pipeline, extract, rob, pipeline_revise_dependent, synthesis_verify) attribute them to fulltext/extract/rob/feedback/synthesize. _synthesize runs the engine with stage None (68), where the tag fallback routes each call to its own stage model (the intended behaviour, not a defect). snowball calls screen_one and screening.fulltext_review, which re-reviews every record that needs a full-text review, not only the new ones (screening.py:505), under ctx.at('snowball') (snowball.py:130-133; pipeline.py:42), bypassing stage_models.screen and stage_models.fulltext. eval/stagewise.py:60-62 calls extract_all/rob_all/synthesize without ctx.at, but with stage None the tags route to the extract/rob/synthesize models, so the model is only wrong there when job_agent is set: because 'eval' is not in STAGE_JOBS and the Run page always sends an agent pick for it, the UI writes job_agent (server.py:317-318, 335-337) and every agent call of the evaluation, including the stage-wise rebuild, runs on it, while manifest() records models.agent = role_cfg('agent') (run_all.py:95-97; the sanitized config dump does retain job_agent). StageAgent.map calls client() with an empty tag, so reassess_all and process_pdfs (run from the CLI without ctx.at) take the thread-pool size of the feedback stage's client (context.py:29-30; config.py:283) while their chats still go to the right model by tag. screening.verify_existing wraps ctx.map in ctx.at('fulltext') (screening.py:423), which verify_screen ignores because it picks its checker by the record's stage (329-330), so that wrapper only sets the concurrency. The config lock and the report (config_lock.models_by_stage, report.py:73-74, 638-640) attribute each stage to its configured model regardless of which client actually ran.

Scenario: StageAgent.client(tag) returns hub.agent_for(ctx.stage or stage_of_tag(tag)) (context.py:22-24), so whenever ctx.stage is set, every tag is ignored. (1) Reviewer edits a protocol criterion after a complete run and starts `sragent run` again. pipeline._screen (pipeline.py:23-32) enters ctx.at('screen'); screen_all finds nothing new; engine.plan reports screen items with action reassess/rederive, so engine.execute(..., max_rounds=1) runs with no roots. In that single round (engine.py:221-257) the engine runs rules.apply, then _screen items: 'reassess' -> screening.reassess (tag screen_full_text_pipeline for full-text nodes, screening.py:269) and 'rescreen' -> review_one -> screen_one(full_text) (tag screen_full_text); then _statuses; then _extract for every stale or missing extraction (extraction.extract_one, tag 'extract'); _rob (rob.rob_one, tag 'rob'); and _synthesis -> feedback._revise_dependent / _include_study / synthesis.verify_one (tags pipeline_revise_dependent, pipeline_integrate, synthesis_verify). Every one of those calls goes through ctx.agent.chat -> client(tag) -> agent_for('screen'), i.e. stage_models.screen, while usage.jsonl tags attribute them to fulltext/extract/rob/feedback/synthesize and config_lock.models_by_stage (used by report.py:73-74 and 638-640) says each stage ran on its configured model. (2) pipeline._snowball (pipeline.py:42) wraps snowball.snowball in ctx.at('snowball'); lines 130-133 call screen_one(title_abstract) for the new records and screening.fulltext_review, whose todo (screening.py:505) is every record for which needs_fulltext_review is true (forwarded, fulltext_unavailable, parse_failed records from the earlier stage too), so title/abstract and full-text screening of those records run on stage_models.snowball, bypassing stage_models.screen and stage_models.fulltext. (3) pipeline._synthesize runs the engine at line 68 with ctx.stage None; here the tag fallback routes each call to the stage its tag names, which is the intended behaviour, so this one is a fact, not a defect. (4) eval/stagewise.py:60-62 calls extract_all/rob_all/synthesize on an eval Ctx whose stage is None (run_all.eval_context, run_all.py:85); the tags 'extract', 'extract_merge', 'rob', 'rob_verify', 'synthesis*' route to their own stage models via stage_of_tag, so the model is right there unless job_agent is set. From the web interface, 'eval' is not in STAGE_JOBS (server.py:32), the Run page shows an Agent model select for eval (index.html:1246-1248, runOpts.agent is always populated) and submit() also copies the last agent from ui_settings.json when none is sent (server.py:317-318), so _write_job_config writes over['job_agent'] (server.py:335-337); load_config keeps the key, eval_context deep-copies the cfg, and LLMHub.agent_for returns the 'agent@job' client for every agent call (llm.py:524-525) including the stage-wise rebuild, while manifest() records models.agent = role_cfg(ectx.cfg,'agent') (run_all.py:95-97), which does not see job_agent (the manifest's sanitized config dump does keep the job_agent key). (5) StageAgent.map calls self.client() with an empty tag (context.py:29-30); stage_of_tag('') returns 'feedback' (config.py:276-283), so Ctx.map in rob.reassess_all (cli reassess-rob) and sync.process_pdfs -> fulltext_review (cli pdfs), both run without ctx.at, take the thread-pool size of the feedback stage's client (the agent role's unless stage_models.feedback sets concurrency); the chats inside still go to the rob/extract/fulltext models by tag. (6) screening.verify_existing wraps ctx.map in ctx.at('fulltext') (screening.py:423), but verify_screen picks its checker explicitly (roles.verifier, else agent_for('fulltext' or 'screen') by the record's stage, screening.py:329-330), so the wrapper only sets the map concurrency.

Evidence:
- `sragent/context.py:22` — client(tag): hub.agent_for(self._ctx.stage or stage_of_tag(tag)); map() at 29-30 calls client() with no tag
- `sragent/pipeline.py:24` — with ctx.at('screen'): screen_all, then engine.execute(ctx, ..., tag='pipeline', render=False, max_rounds=1) when any screen item is reassess/rederive; no roots filter
- `sragent/engine.py:244` — one round runs _screen (reassess/rescreen), _statuses, _extract (252-253), _rob (254-255) and _synthesis (257) before returning
- `sragent/pipeline.py:68` — _synthesize runs engine.execute outside ctx.at (stage None): the tag fallback then routes each call to its own stage model
- `sragent/stages/snowball.py:130` — rules.apply, ctx.map(screen_one title_abstract) and screening.fulltext_review under ctx.at('snowball') (pipeline.py:42)
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:505` — fulltext_review's todo is every screen node where needs_fulltext_review is true, not only the records snowball added
- `sragent/config.py:276` — stage_of_tag: screen_title_abstract->screen, screen_full_text->fulltext, extract, rob, synthesis->synthesize, plan, search; anything else (including '') -> 'feedback'
- `sragent/eval/stagewise.py:60` — extract_all/rob_all/synthesize without ctx.at on an eval Ctx with stage None (run_all.py:85)

#### I86 · The verifier is one role resolved by five code paths with four different fallbacks, its base is llm_defaults rather than the agent role, and the docs describe one verifier with one default

*not yet verified · high · models-config · reasons: R6, R9*

With roles.verifier empty: feedback checks use ctx.agent (= stage_models.feedback by tag, or the job agent; sragent/feedback.py:58-60); synthesis and RoB checks use hub['agent'] (the plain agent role, ignoring stage_models and job_agent; synthesis.py:131-134; rob.py:109); the screening second reading uses hub.agent_for('fulltext'|'screen') and the eligibility recheck hub.agent_for('fulltext') (screening.py:329-330; extraction.py:230), which during a UI re-run is the job model (llm.py:521); the estimate and the Run-page row use role_cfg('agent') (estimate.py:180-181; server.py:750). When set, role_cfg layers roles.verifier over llm_defaults, not over roles.agent (config.py:212). configs/README.md:54 and config.py:66 describe it as checking feedback/revisions/synthesis statements only; GUIDE.md:456 says default 'the agent role', 461 'the agent model', screening.py:323 'else the stage's model' — four descriptions, and none mentions screening disputes, eligibility rechecks or RoB second looks.

Evidence:

- `sragent/stages/synthesis.py:131` — checker: hub['verifier'] else hub['agent']; rob._verify imports it (rob.py:109)
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:329` — hub['verifier'] else agent_for('fulltext'|'screen'); extraction.py:230 agent_for('fulltext')

- `configs/README.md:54` — 'verifier (optional) checks feedback ..., re-checks revised outputs and checks every synthesis statement, and is the agent role unless set'

#### I36 · stage_models.feedback writes every post-synthesis revision but is never locked, never in the report's model list, and excluded from the job's recorded model

*not yet verified · medium · models-config · reasons: R1, R6*

pipeline._synthesize (existing synthesis), sync_dependents and process_pdfs run REVISE_DEPENDENT/SYNTH_INTEGRATE/IMPACT with tags pipeline_*/sync_*/pdf_* (sragent/pipeline.py:68; sync.py:92; feedback.py:554, 606), which stage_of_tag maps to 'feedback' (config.py:276-283). STAGE_STARTED has no feedback entry (config_lock.py:31-41), so stage_models.feedback is never recorded or locked; models_by_stage iterates STAGE_STARTED (171); stage_summary (the recorded model of run/pdfs/sync jobs) uses LLM_STAGES[:-1] (server.py:759-765).
