#### I47 · Cost is attributed by raw tag: the tag-to-stage map lives in three places and misroutes verifier, vision, eligibility and sync/pdf tags to 'feedback'; usage 'role' is a client key; tags follow four naming patterns; Activity's 'LLM calls by stage' lists tags

*confirmed · medium · models-config · reasons: R6*

Spend is attributed by raw tag everywhere it is shown: CostTracker.by_tag and usage.jsonl (llm.py:97-110, with 'role' being the hub client key such as 'agent', 'verifier', 'agent@fulltext' or 'agent@job'), studies.history llm_by_tag (studies.py:384-392) and the Activity table headed 'Stage (tag)' (index.html:1740). Three unrelated tag/row-to-model maps exist: config.stage_of_tag (config.py:276-283, seven prefixes, default 'feedback') is used only by context.StageAgent (context.py:24) to choose the MODEL for ctx.agent calls outside a pipeline stage, so vision_page, sync_*, pdf_*, pipeline_*, rerun_*, *_revise_dependent and *_integrate run on stage_models.feedback; estimate.ROW_STAGE (estimate.py:32-34) plus the verifier special case (180-181) choose the price per estimate row; and the checker resolvers in screening.py:329-330, extraction.py:230, synthesis.py:134 pick the verifier explicitly, so screen_verify_* and eligibility_verify never pass through stage_of_tag. estimate.history only learns from tags equal to or prefixed by a DEFAULTS row (57-58), so sync/pdf/rerun/pipeline/eligibility_verify/vision_page lines never feed an estimate. One revision call appears as feedback_/rerun_/propagate_/pdf_/sync_/pipeline_ + _revise_dependent (no 'corrupt_' prefix exists). No per-stage spend view and no snowball estimate row exist.

Scenario: 1) A pipeline run in the web UI: screening.verify_screen (sragent/stages/screening.py:336) calls checker.chat(..., tag='screen_verify_full_text'); LLM.chat -> CostTracker.add(self.role, self.model, tag, ...) (llm.py:273/306) where self.role is the hub client key ('verifier', 'agent', or 'agent@fulltext' from LLMHub.agent_for, llm.py:529; 'agent@job' when job_agent is set, llm.py:525). add() keys by_tag on the raw tag (llm.py:97) and writes {role, model, tag} to usage.jsonl (llm.py:106-110). 2) studies.history builds llm_by_tag from the raw tag only (studies.py:384-392); index.html:1740 renders it under the heading 'Stage (tag)' with esc(u.tag). 3) server.estimate -> estimate.history(self.run_dir.parent) (server.py:292) maps each usage line to a DEFAULTS row only if the tag equals or is prefixed by a row name (estimate.py:57-58): 'screen_verify_full_text' -> 'screen_verify', 'rob_verify' -> 'rob_verify', but 'sync', 'pipeline', 'sync_revise_dependent', 'pdf_revise_dependent', 'rerun_revise_dependent', 'eligibility_verify', 'vision_page' match nothing and never inform any estimate; estimate_stages then prices screen_verify at roles.verifier else role_cfg('agent') (estimate.py:180-181) and other rows via ROW_STAGE (32-34). 4) config.stage_of_tag (config.py:276-283) is a third tag->stage map, but its only caller is context.StageAgent.client (context.py:24): it selects the MODEL for ctx.agent calls made outside a pipeline stage (sync/pdfs/rerun/feedback jobs), where every unrecognised tag ('vision_page' from docparse.py:159 via ctx.agent in a pdfs job, 'sync_*', 'pdf_*', 'pipeline_*', '{tp}_revise_dependent', '{tp}_integrate', 'feedback_locate') is served by stage_models.feedback. It is not used for cost attribution. screen_verify_* and eligibility_verify do NOT pass through stage_of_tag: verify_screen (screening.py:329-330) and the extraction eligibility check (extraction.py:230) pick the checker explicitly (hub['verifier'] else agent_for('fulltext'|'screen')). 5) The '{tp}_revise_dependent' tag (feedback.py:558) is produced with tp = 'feedback' (handle_feedback default tag_prefix, feedback.py:203), 'rerun' (actions.py:305), 'propagate' (actions.py:331), 'pdf' (sync.py:47), 'sync' (sync.py:92 -> engine tag), 'pipeline' (pipeline.py:30,68) and 'claim2_<cond>' in eval; no 'corrupt_' prefix exists. 6) No 'snowball' row exists anywhere: remaining_calls folds citation-chasing candidates into screen_title_abstract/screen_full_text (estimate.py:108-112) and DEFAULTS/ROW_STAGE have no snowball row.

Evidence:
- `sragent/config.py:276` — stage_of_tag: seven prefixes (screen_title_abstract, screen_full_text, extract, rob, synthesis, plan, search), everything else -> 'feedback'
- `sragent/context.py:24` — the only caller of stage_of_tag: StageAgent.client picks hub.agent_for(ctx.stage or stage_of_tag(tag)) - model routing, not cost attribution
- `sragent/estimate.py:32` — ROW_STAGE: screen_verify->fulltext, rob_verify->rob, synthesis_verify->synthesize; history() matches tags to DEFAULTS rows by equality/prefix at 57-58; estimate_stages prices verify rows with roles.verifier else agent at 180-181
- `sragent/llm.py:97` — by_tag keyed on raw tag; usage.jsonl line has role (client key), model, tag at 106-110; role names 'agent@job' (525) and 'agent@{stage}' (529)
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:329` — verify_screen picks the checker explicitly: hub['verifier'] else agent_for('fulltext'/'screen'); tag 'screen_verify_{stage}' at 336
- `sragent/stages/extraction.py:230` — eligibility check: hub['verifier'] else agent_for('fulltext'); tag 'eligibility_verify' at 235 (no estimate row, no DEFAULTS match)
- `sragent/docparse.py:159` — tag 'vision_page' sent through llm=ctx.agent (fulltext.py:141): in a pdfs job (no ctx.stage) stage_of_tag routes it to stage_models.feedback
- `sragent/studies.py:384` — llm_by_tag aggregated per raw tag, no role/model

#### I65 · Which model produced a node version is not in the graph; the config lock records one configured model per started stage; Activity records the wrong model for stage jobs and shows a different one in-session than after restart

*confirmed · medium · models-config · reasons: R6, R2*

A node carries data, evidence, status, version, history, flags (sragent/store.py:83-85) and no model/role/effort; only verifier sub-records keep a 'by': client name (screening.py:349, rob.py:117, extraction.py:239). meta.config_lock records stage_models.<stage> once, at the first save after the stage started (config_lock.py:108-113), and the report prints that as the review's models (report.py:73-74, 638-640), while later work on the same nodes may run under job_agent ('agent@job', llm.py:524-525) or the tag-routed stage model. _record writes model = stage_summary() for STAGE_JOBS (server.py:146), which iterates LLM_STAGES[:-1] and so excludes the feedback stage that sync/pdfs revisions use (761); jobs() prefers the in-memory spec's agent dict (455-456), which run/sync/pdfs specs carry whenever the client sent an agent or ui_settings.json holds one (317-318, 842-844; processPdfs always sends one, index.html:1385), so such a job shows the Run-page agent while the UI process lives and the per-stage summary after a restart.

Scenario: 1) store.upsert creates a node with id, type, data, depends_on, dep_versions, evidence, status, version, history, flags, updated (store.py:83-85); no model/role/effort field, and no stage writes a model into node data (grep for '"model"' in stages/*.py finds nothing; only the verifier records 'by': checker.name inside screen_verify/rob_verify/eligibility review sub-records at screening.py:349, rob.py:117, extraction.py:239; change sets record model=llm.name but those are proposals, not nodes). 2) Ctx.save -> config_lock.record_if_new (context.py:81-82) writes stage_models.<stage> only for stages newly started and never overwrites (config_lock.py:108-113); models_by_stage reports that as the model each started stage ran with (165-175) and report.py:73-74, 638-640 print it. Later calls on the same nodes can run on other models: a UI rerun/feedback job sets job_agent (server.py:335-337) so LLMHub.agent_for returns 'agent@job' for every call (llm.py:524-525); sync/pdfs jobs route by tag through stage_of_tag (context.py:24); none of this is recorded in the lock. 3) server.submit: if the spec has no 'agent', it merges _last_models() from ui_settings.json (server.py:317-318, 842-844), which is written on every run/pdfs/sync/eval submission (320-322); processPdfs always sends an agent (index.html:1385). _record writes model = stage_summary() for STAGE_JOBS (146), and stage_summary iterates LLM_STAGES[:-1], excluding 'feedback' (761) although sync/pdfs work outside a stage runs on stage_models.feedback. jobs() view prefers (j.get('agent') or {}).get('model') (455-456), so while the UI process lives a run/sync/pdfs job that carries an agent dict shows the Run-page agent model, and after a restart the same job (loaded from jobs file by _load) shows the stage_summary string. Over-claim: a spec carries 'agent' only when the client sent one or ui_settings.json holds one, not unconditionally.

Evidence:
- `sragent/store.py:83` — node record fields: id, type, data, depends_on, dep_versions, evidence, status, version, history, flags, updated - no model
- `sragent/config_lock.py:108` — record_if_new: stage_models.<stage> recorded once for newly started stages; never overwritten; models_by_stage at 165-175 reports it
- `sragent/stages/report.py:73` — report prints models_by_stage as the models of the review (also 638-640)
- `sragent/llm.py:524` — agent_for: job_agent overrides every call ('agent@job'); server.py:335-337 sets job_agent for non-STAGE_JOBS specs with an agent
- `sragent/ui/server.py:146` — _record: model = stage_summary() for STAGE_JOBS, else agent.model
- `sragent/ui/server.py:761` — stage_summary iterates LLM_STAGES[:-1] (feedback excluded)
- `sragent/ui/server.py:455` — jobs() view: agent.model if the in-memory spec has an agent dict, else j['model'] (the recorded stage summary after a restart)
- `sragent/ui/server.py:317` — submit merges _last_models() (ui_settings.json agent) into specs that lack an agent; ui_settings.json written at 320-322

#### I95 · Vision-page transcription has no role or model of its own: it runs on the fulltext stage model inside `run`, the snowball/job model elsewhere, and the feedback stage model from `pdfs`/`sync`; failures are swallowed while the parser label still says '+ page images'

*not yet verified · medium · fulltext · reasons: R6*

fulltext._parse passes llm=ctx.agent when vision_pages is on (sragent/stages/fulltext.py:141); vision_pages calls llm.chat with tag 'vision_page' (docparse.py:155-159), so the model is stage_models.fulltext during `run`, the snowball model during citation chasing, the job agent in a re-run, and stage_models.feedback from sync.process_pdfs (no ctx.at) because stage_of_tag maps the tag to 'feedback' (config.py:283). Nothing checks that the resolved model accepts images; a failure is logged and the label still reads 'pdftotext + page images' (docparse.py:187-192). config.py:105, configs/README.md:171 and GUIDE.md:339 say 'the (multimodal) agent model'. Two studies parsed by the same parser name can have different texts and the usage row names no model.

Evidence:
- `sragent/stages/fulltext.py:141` — parse_file(..., llm=ctx.agent if vision_pages else None)
- `sragent/docparse.py:187` — try vision_pages except: log; label '+ page images' regardless (192)
- `sragent/config.py:283` — stage_of_tag('vision_page') -> feedback; comment at 105 says agent model

### What a model or a person cannot see (10)

#### I45 · 'See what depends on it' exists only for study-page edits (engine.preview via /api/impact); the To-do eligibility save, RoB confirm/change, protocol edit/ask, search confirm, fields save, restore and 'Decide without it' change earlier stages with no dependency view

*confirmed · high · ui-frontend · reasons: R7, R2*

'See what depends on it' (GET /api/impact -> engine.preview, server.py:1097-1099) is called only by impactLine() for study-page edits (index.html:1625-1634, via editBar at 1531). The To-do eligibility save (saveElig 1024-1032), RoB confirm/change (1118-1131), protocol 'Edit criteria'/'Ask' (pcEditSave/pcAsk 850-860; apply_protocol re-derives decisions immediately, planning.py:104), search Confirm (ssConfirm 973-978), fields Save (fcSave 889-893; server data_items auto-accepts the config lock and computes the plan only after the write, server.py:613-620), Restore (restoreRule 1153) and 'Decide without it' (pdfUnwant 1077) change earlier stages with no dependency view; the only signals are after the fact (Overview stale callout 702-704, sync to-do 746-752, the data_items reply message). No endpoint lists a node's inputs: study_detail (studies.py:235) exposes neither depends_on nor stale reasons.

Scenario: The only dependency preview in the UI is impactLine() (index.html:1625-1634), which calls GET /api/impact?node=... (1628) -> server.py:1097-1099 -> engine.preview (engine.py:295); impactLine is injected only into editBar (1531) for study-page edits (impactNode at 1624: kinds syn/ext/rob/screen). grep over sragent/ shows no other caller of engine.preview or /api/impact. Earlier-stage changes with no preview: (a) saveElig (1024-1032) -> /api/bulk kind 'eligibility' -> app.mutate (server.py:1166-1167); (b) robConfirm (1118-1125) -> /api/bulk rob_confirm, robChange (1126-1131) -> /api/answer; (c) protocol 'Edit criteria' pcEditSave (850-854) and 'Ask' pcAsk (855-860) -> pcCall op edit/revise -> planning.apply_protocol, which writes protocol.yaml and immediately calls screening.rescreen_rule_only (planning.py:104) with no preview; the protocol card (770-805) contains only prose and option hints; (d) search ssConfirm (973-978); (e) fields fcSave (889-893) -> /api/data_items -> server.py:605-620 saves the topic file, auto-accepts the config lock (613-614), and only then computes pending_changes (617-620) returned as an after-the-fact message; (f) restoreRule (1153) -> /api/bulk restore_rule; (g) pdfUnwant (1077) -> /api/bulk pdf_unwant. After-the-fact signals are the Overview stale callout (702-704) and the sync to-do group (746-752). No endpoint lists a node's inputs: studies.study_detail (studies.py:235) contains no depends_on or stale field (grep over studies.py: none), although the store keeps depends_on on every node (store.py:76-83).

Evidence:
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1628` — the only /api/impact call, inside impactLine, used only by editBar (1531) on the study page
- `sragent/ui/server.py:1097` — /api/impact -> engine.preview; engine.preview (engine.py:295) has no other caller
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1024` — saveElig -> /api/bulk eligibility, no impact
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1118` — robConfirm (1118-1125) /api/bulk rob_confirm; robChange (1126-1131) /api/answer; no impact
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:850` — pcEditSave / pcAsk (850-860) -> pcCall; protocol card 770-805 shows prose only
- `sragent/stages/planning.py:104` — apply_protocol: rescreen_rule_only runs immediately on save
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:889` — fcSave -> /api/data_items
- `sragent/ui/server.py:613` — data_items: config_lock.accept then pending_changes computed after the write (617-620)

#### I23 · agent_only gating is enforced in three layers with different outcomes per endpoint; several human-triggered operations are ungated; planning.plan raises an uncaught NotAllowed

*confirmed · medium · ui-backend · reasons: R4*

agent_only gating is enforced in three layers with different outcomes: /api/rerun and /api/propagate are refused at the handler (sragent/ui/server.py:1174-1176); todos.apply and actions.edit return ok:false (todos.py:409-414; actions.py:104-107); apply_protocol raises inside protocol_change and is returned as ok:false (server.py:649-650), though for op 'revise' the model call (planning.py:117-118) is made and paid before the gate at planning.py:97; todos.bulk raises NotAllowed which /api/bulk -> mutate() -> _apply_op does not catch, so the handler returns HTTP 500 (todos.py:579-580; server.py:1195-1196). actions.add_comment(send) also raises NotAllowed, but App.comment gates first (server.py:712-714), so that raise is unreachable from /api/comment. /api/search_strategy (including 'confirm', which records a user-chosen query with no provenance), /api/data_items, /api/config_accept, the 'sync' answer (todos._category None, 396-397), /api/run for every action (1150-1151, including reassess_rob) and /api/stop (1152-1154) have no gate; they are hidden client-side only (todos.build keeps just the 'fulltexts' card, todos.py:351-353; judging() at index.html:1486 used at 759/1450). planning.plan's require_judgment_allowed for a hand-edited protocol.yaml (planning.py:28-30) is caught nowhere: pipeline.run, cmd_run (cli.py:62-66) and main() (414-421, ConfigChanged only) let NotAllowed escape as a traceback, so the run job fails with a stack trace instead of a message.

Scenario: Layer 1, handler: POST /api/rerun or /api/propagate in an agent_only run -> refused with ok:false at server.py:1174-1176. Layer 2, ok:false from the function: POST /api/answer with a human_judgment to-do -> todos.apply -> _category != None -> require_judgment_allowed raises NotAllowed, caught and returned as {ok:false} (todos.py:409-414); POST /api/edit -> actions.edit same (actions.py:104-107); POST /api/protocol -> protocol_change -> planning.apply_protocol raises NotAllowed (planning.py:97), swallowed by `except Exception` -> ok:false (server.py:649-650) - but for op 'revise' the model call at planning.py:117-118 has already been made and paid before the gate. Layer 3, uncaught -> 500: POST /api/bulk {kind:'eligibility'|'rob_confirm'|'restore_rule'|'pdf_unwant'} -> mutate() -> _apply_op -> todos.bulk raises NotAllowed (todos.py:579-580); mutate has no try/except, so do_POST's `except Exception` returns HTTP 500 (1195-1196) (if a job is running the op is queued and later logged 'failed' by _drain_answers instead). REFUTED SUB-CLAIM: actions.add_comment(send=True) does raise NotAllowed (actions.py:33-34), but App.comment checks human_mode() == 'agent_only' first and returns ok:false (server.py:712-714), so the /api/comment path never reaches the raise. Ungated endpoints: /api/search_strategy (655-702; 'confirm' writes meta.search_strategy.confirmed with by:'user', no mode check, no provenance), /api/data_items (550-622), /api/config_accept (823-836), the 'sync' answer (todos._category returns None for tid 'sync', 396-397), /api/run for any action including reassess_rob (1150-1151 -> app.submit(b); the reassess-rob CLI has no gate either per grep of require_judgment_allowed), and /api/stop (1152-1154). The UI hides these controls client-side: todos.build keeps only the 'fulltexts' card in agent_only (todos.py:351-353) and judging() (index.html:1486, used at 759/1450) hides buttons, so the gaps are API-level. Uncaught in the run subprocess: agent_only review, a person edits run_dir/protocol.yaml by hand, then runs `sragent run` (from the UI or CLI) -> planning.plan detects the diff and calls require_judgment_allowed (planning.py:28-30) -> NotAllowed propagates through pipeline.run (no handler; grep shows NotAllowed only in todos.py, actions.py, provenance.py) and cmd_run (cli.py:62-66) to main(), whose try/except catches only ConfigChanged (414-421) -> Python traceback, exit 1, UI job 'failed' with the traceback in its log.

Evidence:
- `sragent/ui/server.py:1174` — handler gate for /api/rerun and /api/propagate only; /api/run (1150), /api/stop (1152), /api/search_strategy (1157), /api/data_items (1161), /api/config_accept (1140) have none
- `sragent/todos.py:409` — apply(): require_judgment_allowed caught -> ok:false; _category('sync') is None (396-397) so a sync answer is never gated
- `sragent/actions.py:104` — edit(): NotAllowed caught -> ok:false (104-107)
- `sragent/todos.py:579` — bulk(): raises NotAllowed for every kind but pdf_unavailable; mutate()/_apply_op have no handler -> do_POST except -> HTTP 500 (server.py:1195-1196)
- `sragent/ui/server.py:712` — comment(): `if b.get('send') and self.human_mode() == 'agent_only': return ok:false` runs before actions.add_comment, so its NotAllowed (actions.py:33-34) is unreachable from /api/comment
- `sragent/stages/planning.py:97` — apply_protocol gate; for 'revise' the model call at 117-118 precedes it; protocol_change's except Exception (server.py:649-650) returns ok:false
- `sragent/stages/planning.py:30` — plan(): require_judgment_allowed for an edited protocol.yaml; cli.py main() 414-421 catches only ConfigChanged; cmd_run (62-66) and pipeline.run have no handler -> traceback
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1486` — judging() defined here (used at 759, 1450) hides controls client-side; todos.build (todos.py:351-353) drops every card but 'fulltexts'

#### I40 · Citation chasing (snowball) has a model row and a Run option but no page, status row, to-do, outputs view, cost row or separate spend; its records depend on 'search', found_via is wrong for file/synergy sources, the date limit is applied three times, and a role branch is dead

*confirmed · medium · pipeline · reasons: R1, R2, R7*

Citation chasing has a model row (ui/server.py stage_rows iterates LLM_STAGES) and a 'Run up to' option, but no Overview row (todos.STEPS, todos.py:26-30), no to-do kind with step 'snowball', no outputs view (studies_list exposes ft.kind as 'source', studies.py:120, never found_via), no cost row (estimate.py:108-111 folds its calls into screen_title_abstract/screen_full_text and ROW_STAGE has no snowball key, so they are priced with the screen/fulltext models while running on stage_models.snowball) and no separate spend (its calls are tagged screen_title_abstract/screen_full_text at screening.py:207 and Activity groups by tag, studies.py:383-390). Its switches are YAML only (pipeline.citation_chasing*), and its model is locked as soon as snowball() sets meta.snowball at line 34, before any seed is processed. Records it adds are upserted with depends_on=['search'] (snowball.py:121), as are user-supplied papers (feedback.py:635), so the graph cannot tell searched from chased or supplied records; the record's own found_via field and meta.found_via are the only provenance. meta.found_via['search'] is derived from search.retrieved_pmids (39-42), which is [] for source=file and the agent's PubMed hits for synergy; however the only consumer, eval/screening_eval.py:77-79, reads found_via just for 'citation' and derives 'search' from retrieved_pmids itself (the benchmark question is whether the agent's query retrieved the record), so the 'wrong provenance on benchmarks' part is not reproduced; for file imports the 'search' tag is simply absent and unused. The date limit is checked three times (year string at 91-92, pubmed.within_dates on candidates at 100-108, and retroactively on meta.added via rules.exclude at 45-52); these are redundant rather than contradictory. _terms tests kc.get('role') (snowball.py:21), which PLAN never emits (prompts.py:37) and no config writes, so that branch is dead.

Scenario: Reviewer runs `sragent run` (or the Run page) past stage 4 on a review with pipeline.citation_chasing left at its default True. pipeline._snowball (pipeline.py:40-43) enters ctx.at('snowball') and calls snowball.snowball(). Line 34 does st.meta.setdefault('snowball', ...) before any seed is looked up, so config_lock.STAGE_STARTED['snowball'] (config_lock.py:37) is true at the next ctx.save() and stage_models.snowball is locked even when the loop breaks at line 56-57 with no seeds. For every new candidate it upserts rec:<rid> with depends_on=['search'] (line 121) and sets meta.found_via[rid]=['citation'] (122); feedback._add_paper does the same depends_on=['search'] for a user-supplied PMID (feedback.py:635), so a record's edges cannot distinguish searched, chased or supplied records. Lines 39-42 tag 'search' from search.data.retrieved_pmids, which search.py:167-168 sets to [] for records.source=file and search.py:170-173 sets to the agent's PubMed hits while the records themselves come from synergy.load (178) for a benchmark. The new records are screened with screening.screen_one (tag 'screen_title_abstract', screening.py:207) and screening.fulltext_review (tag 'screen_full_text') while ctx.stage=='snowball'; usage.jsonl rows carry those tags, and studies.history groups LLM calls by tag only (studies.py:383-390; index.html:1739 'Stage (tag)'), so the Activity view shows them under screening. estimate.remaining_calls adds the expected chasing calls to screen_title_abstract/screen_full_text (estimate.py:108-111) and ROW_STAGE (estimate.py:32-34) has no snowball key, so the estimate prices them with the screen/fulltext models although they run on stage_models.snowball. The Overview is built from todos.STEPS (todos.py:26-30) via ui/steps.py:57, which has no snowball entry; no to-do kind uses step 'snowball' (the only step values in todos.py are plan, search, screen, fulltext, extract, rob, synthesis, feedback); studies_list exposes 'source' = ft.kind (studies.py:120) and never found_via; only report.py:510-514 reads meta.snowball.added for the PRISMA 'citation' count. The date limit is checked at line 91-92 (year string vs until[:4]), lines 100-108 (pubmed.within_dates on the candidates with a PMID) and lines 45-52 (within_dates again on every record in meta.added, excluding via rules.exclude). _terms (line 21) tests kc.get('role'), but prompts.PLAN emits key_concepts as {term, kind} only (prompts.py:37) and no code or config under sragent/ or configs/ writes a 'role' key, so that disjunct is dead.

Evidence:
- `sragent/todos.py:26` — STEPS = plan, search, screen, fulltext, extract, rob, synthesis, feedback; ui/steps.py:57 iterates it, so the Overview has no snowball row; grep of '"step": "' in todos.py finds no snowball to-do
- `sragent/estimate.py:108` — snowball calls added to calls['screen_title_abstract'] / ['screen_full_text']; ROW_STAGE (lines 32-34) has no snowball key, so estimate_stages prices them with the screen/fulltext stage models
- `sragent/stages/snowball.py:121` — st.upsert(rec, depends_on=['search']); fv[rid]=['citation'] at 122; feedback.py:635 upserts user-supplied records with the same depends_on=['search']
- `sragent/stages/snowball.py:39` — retrieved = search.data.retrieved_pmids; search.py:167-168 sets it to [] for source=file, search.py:170-173 to the agent's PubMed hits for synergy while records come from synergy.load (178)
- `sragent/stages/snowball.py:45` — retro within_dates + rules.exclude on meta.added (45-52); year-string check at 91-92; within_dates on candidates at 100-108
- `sragent/stages/snowball.py:21` — kc.get('kind') == 'drug' or kc.get('role') in (...): PLAN schema at prompts.py:37 is {term, kind}; no producer of 'role' found in sragent/ or configs/
- `sragent/stages/snowball.py:34` — meta.setdefault('snowball', ...) before any work; config_lock.py:37 STAGE_STARTED['snowball'] = bool(meta.snowball) so the model is locked at the next save even with zero seeds
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:207` — screen_one tags calls screen_<stage>; under ctx.at('snowball') (pipeline.py:42) these run on the snowball model but are logged under the screening tags

#### I46 · engine.preview only bumps a version, so it misses consequences that depend on the new value (retire/cites-excluded/cost) and the model-decided IMPACT reach, which is a second dependency mechanism outside the graph

#### I70 · Four readings of 'where is the review' are assembled per poll (graph.stale_summary, the sync to-do from engine.plan, steps.py predicates, next_action's ladder) and disagree; stale protocol/search/criterion are 'kept manual' forever; /api/state recomputes staleness twice

*confirmed · medium · ui-backend · reasons: R2, R7*

App.state assembles four readings per poll: graph.stale_summary over every active node type (server.py:861), the sync to-do that exists only when an extraction exists and engine.plan has non-keep/render items (todos.py:327; sync.py:73, so touch items count and manual keeps do not), steps.py predicates (33-55) and next_action's ladder (todos.py:631-691). Stale search and protocol nodes are 'keep manual' (engine.py:115-116) and nothing touches them (criteria are touched at planning.py:75, search and protocol never), so after a protocol revision with the search already run the Overview shows '1 search out of date' with a link to a sync group that is not rendered; before any extraction, stale screens likewise show a count with no to-do. A touch-only to-do is priced $0.00 by _cost, not as a paid update. Per 3 s poll (index.html:2166), state() runs graph.stale twice (engine.plan via pending_changes and stale_summary), re-reads provenance.jsonl, usage.jsonl plus eval usage files and the job-log tail, stats (not hashes: content_sha is lru-cached, fulltext.py:257-265) every local PDF, and Workspace.get re-parses the run file YAML and its includes (server.py:62-81).

Scenario: Every 3 s (index.html:2166) /api/state runs App.state: Workspace.get re-walks and yaml.safe_loads the run file and its includes (server.py:62-81), todos.build -> sync.pending_changes -> engine.plan -> graph.stale (plan at engine.py:58-68), then graph.stale_summary runs graph.stale again (server.py:861), plus steps() (steps.py:33-55, node presence + meta flags), todos.next_action (todos.py:631-691, its own ladder), new_local_files (provenance.jsonl re-read at fulltext.py:273; PDFs are stat'ed, content_sha is lru-cached by path/size/mtime at 257-265), spent() (usage.jsonl and every eval usage.jsonl, 290-300) and job_view (log tail, 470-485). Disagreement case: the search has run and the reviewer revises the protocol (/api/protocol op=revise). apply_protocol upserts the protocol node (planning.py:102) and touches unchanged criteria (75), but nothing ever touches 'search', which depends_on protocol (search.py:220; only touch calls: store.py:92, engine.py:247/332/423, planning.py:75). engine.plan maps search to _item(..., 'keep', manual=True) (engine.py:115-116) and execute skips keep items, so after 'Update now' re-assesses the screens the search node stays stale forever; sync.pending_changes counts only non-keep/render items (sync.py:73) so no sync to-do exists, while graph.stale_summary still counts it, and the Overview (index.html:702-704) says '1 output is out of date (1 search)' with 'See the plan and update' -> goTodoGroup('sync') (664) scrolling to a group that is not rendered (746). Same for protocol after cfg:question/seed_eligibility change (graph.py:36). Before any extraction, stale screens (e.g. after a criterion edit) produce no sync to-do either (todos.py:327 requires st.by_type('extraction')) although the Overview shows them as out of date. For touch-only work the to-do does appear (touch is not keep) but _cost ignores touch, so the parsed cost is $0.00, not a paid update.

Evidence:
- `sragent/ui/server.py:852` — todos.build -> pending_changes -> engine.plan -> graph.stale; stale_summary at 861 runs graph.stale again; spent() at 875, new_local_files at 858, job_view at 877
- `sragent/todos.py:327` — sync to-do only if extraction nodes exist and stale.n > 0
- `sragent/sync.py:73` — work excludes keep/render, so touch counts and manual keeps do not
- `sragent/engine.py:115` — protocol/search/criterion stale -> keep manual=True; execute skips keep (222-227)
- `sragent/stages/planning.py:75` — criteria are touched on protocol edits (do not stay stale); search (search.py:220 depends_on protocol) is never touched
- `sragent/ui/steps.py:33` — step status from node presence + meta flags
- `sragent/todos.py:631` — next_action: separate priority ladder
- `sragent/stages/fulltext.py:257` — content_sha lru-cached by (path, size, mtime_ns): a stat per PDF per poll, not a hash

#### I88 · The eligibility recheck at extraction reads a quarter of what the extractor read, judges one criterion, and defaults to 'include' on any unexpected verifier output

*confirmed · medium · extraction-rob-synthesis · reasons: R9*

The extractor reads the paper packed to context_chars (default 60000) or in consecutive chunks (sragent/stages/extraction.py:298-312), while recheck_eligibility gives the verifier packed(text, fulltext_screen_chars) (229, 235; default 15000, config.py:90-91), the single doubted criterion, the screener's one-line assessment and the extractor's 'why' (prompts.py:224-240) and none of the extracted values. The verdict is 'exclude' only when the reply's verdict starts with 'exclude', else 'include' (236): a well-formed reply with a missing or non-'exclude' verdict (e.g. 'uncertain') keeps the study included and is recorded as the verifier saying include. An empty or malformed (non-JSON-object) reply does not default to include: LLMClient.chat retries it and then raises RuntimeError (llm.py:307-324). A doubt whose evidence lies beyond the 15k packing yields 'two agents disagree' and the study becomes 'awaiting' for a person (267-275).

Scenario: extract_one reads the paper with budget=context_chars (extraction.py:298, 303-305; default 60000 at config.py:91) or in consecutive chunks (308-312). When it returns an eligibility_doubt, recheck_eligibility (211-291) builds the verifier prompt with limit=fulltext_screen_chars (229; default 15000 at config.py:90), packed(text, limit) (235) — evidence.packed (134-166) drops non-data sections first, then keeps whole sections by priority until the budget is spent, marking the rest omitted — and prompts.ELIGIBILITY_VERIFY (prompts.py:224-240) names one criterion, the screener's one-line assessment/reason and the extractor's 'why'; no extracted values are passed. Line 236: verdict = 'exclude' iff str(out.get('verdict','')).lower().startswith('exclude'), else 'include'. Refuted part: an empty or malformed reply cannot reach 236 — checker.chat (llm.py:256-324) parses with json_object(), retries with a correction message on non-object output and finally raises RuntimeError, so the call aborts rather than defaulting. What does default to include: a well-formed JSON object whose 'verdict' is missing or anything other than a string starting with 'exclude' (e.g. 'uncertain', 'unclear'), which is then recorded at 239 as the verifier saying include. With verdict include and human_in_the_loop, lines 267-275 set the decision to 'awaiting' with 'two agents disagree ... a person decides', so a doubt whose decisive sentence was in an omitted section ends in a person re-reading the paper.

Evidence:
- `sragent/stages/extraction.py:229` — limit = fulltext_screen_chars (default 15000, config.py:90); extractor uses context_chars (298, 303-305; default 60000, config.py:91) or chunks (308-312)
- `sragent/stages/extraction.py:236` — verdict = 'exclude' only if the reply's verdict starts with 'exclude', else 'include'
- `sragent/prompts.py:224` — ELIGIBILITY_VERIFY: one criterion, screener assessment/reason, extractor's why, packed text; no extracted values
- `sragent/llm.py:307` — json_object() on the reply; a non-object/malformed reply is retried with a correction message and finally raises RuntimeError (324): it never reaches line 236
- `sragent/evidence.py:134` — packed(): non-data sections dropped, then sections kept by priority until max_chars; the remainder marked omitted
- `sragent/stages/extraction.py:267` — verdict include -> decision 'awaiting' (human_in_the_loop) -> outcome 'pending', reason 'two agents disagree'

table_text emits '[rid] {field: value}' (or value+quote) only, never note, uncovered, check.status, terms, auto_evidence or source_kind (sragent/stages/extraction.py:371-385); SYNTH gets question, table and rob_text = overall + domain judgments without rationale (synthesis.py:105-106; rob.py:236-245); the prompt has no criteria/PICO. ### Vocabulary (13)

#### I37 · Six stage lists disagree on membership and keys (REGISTRY, LLM_STAGES, todos.STEPS, STAGE_STARTED, UI STAGES, STAGE_JOBS), so Overview, Run, Models table, lock and job handling each see a different pipeline

*confirmed · medium · pipeline · reasons: R1*

Six stage lists disagree: pipeline.REGISTRY/STAGES and the hand-copied UI STAGES have 9 keys plan..report incl. snowball (pipeline.py:81-92; index.html:452-453); config.LLM_STAGES has 9 with 'feedback' instead of 'report' (config.py:267) and drives the Models table; todos.STEPS has 8 (no snowball, no report, 'synthesis' instead of 'synthesize', plus 'feedback' hard-coded done=True/run_until=None in steps.py:51); config_lock.STAGE_STARTED has 8 (no report, no feedback), so the feedback row can never lock; STEP_OF_TYPE (todos.py:31) is a node-type-to-STEPS-key map rather than a separate key set; engine.ORDER/STAGE_FOR (engine.py:29-30) are defined and never used. STAGE_JOBS differs between server ('run','pdfs','sync','reassess_rob', server.py:32) and browser (['run','pdfs','sync'], index.html:1265); since reassess_rob is never a Run-page action the agent-selector difference is moot, but redraftRob (index.html:1372-1376) skips the unsaved stage-table save that startRun does (1336). The Overview says 'n of 8 steps done' (index.html:708 from STEPS) while the Run select offers 9 stages (1238 from STAGES). Mostly a naming/consistency defect; no data is corrupted.

Scenario: 1. pipeline.REGISTRY/STAGES (pipeline.py:81-92) = plan, search, screen, fulltext, snowball, extract, rob, synthesize, report (9); the browser's hand-copied const STAGES (index.html:452-453) has the same 9 and feeds only the Run page 'Up to stage' select (1238). 2. config.LLM_STAGES (config.py:267) = the same minus report plus 'feedback' (9); server.stage_rows (server.py:729-746) iterates it to build the Models-per-stage table, marking 'started' from config_lock.started_stages, whose STAGE_STARTED (config_lock.py:31-41) has 8 keys plan..synthesize (no report, no feedback), so the feedback row can never lock. 3. todos.STEPS (todos.py:26-30) has 8 rows: plan, search, screen, fulltext, extract, rob, 'synthesis' (not 'synthesize'), 'feedback'; no snowball, no report. steps.py:32-52 computes done/summary/run_until per row with ad hoc predicates (e.g. screen 'done' = no non-excluded title_abstract decisions, 40; rob 'done' = len(robs)==len(inc), 49) and hard-codes feedback as (True, ..., None) at 51, so the Overview card shows '<nDone> of ${S.steps.length}' = 'n of 8 steps done' (index.html:708-709) while the Run select lists 9 stages. STEP_OF_TYPE (todos.py:31) maps node types screen/extraction/rob/synthesis onto STEPS keys (used at 196 and 231); it is a type-to-step map rather than a distinct key set. 4. engine.ORDER and STAGE_FOR (engine.py:29-30) are defined and referenced nowhere else (grep across sragent/: only their definitions). 5. STAGE_JOBS on the server (server.py:32) = run, pdfs, sync, reassess_rob and governs job recording (146), estimate (282) and job_agent override (335-337); the browser's STAGE_JOB (index.html:1265) = run, pdfs, sync. Because reassess_rob is never offered on the Run page (WHAT, 1229-1232), the 'hide agent selector' difference is moot, but redraftRob (1372-1376) posts /api/run directly and skips the `SM.dirty -> saveStageModels()` step that startRun performs (1336), so an unsaved edit in the stage-model table is neither saved nor used for that job (the server reads the saved run file, 736).

Evidence:
- `sragent/pipeline.py:81` — REGISTRY: 9 keys plan..report incl. snowball
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:452` — const STAGES duplicates REGISTRY by hand; used only by the Run page select at 1238
- `sragent/config.py:267` — LLM_STAGES ends with 'feedback', no report; drives the Models table (server.py:738)
- `sragent/todos.py:26` — STEPS: 8 rows, 'synthesis', 'feedback', no snowball/report; STEP_OF_TYPE at 31 maps node types onto STEPS keys (used 196, 231)
- `sragent/ui/steps.py:51` — feedback = (True, ..., None); ad hoc done predicates 33-50
- `sragent/config_lock.py:31` — STAGE_STARTED: 8 keys plan..synthesize (no report, no feedback)
- `sragent/engine.py:29` — ORDER and STAGE_FOR defined; grep finds no use anywhere in sragent/
- `sragent/ui/server.py:32` — STAGE_JOBS includes reassess_rob (used at 146, 282, 335)

#### I78 · Flag sources: twelve code keys, three partial label maps, raw keys printed on the Overview and study page; five sources never become to-dos; two to-do option labels do not match the action they trigger

*confirmed · medium · human-loop · reasons: R1, R2*

Twelve flag-source keys exist in code (feedback default, manual_edit, rerun, update, reassess, screening, screening_unclear, eligibility_doubt, term_validation, synthesis_verify, source_changed; evidence_check is consumed at todos.py:194/215 and report.py:399 but produced nowhere). report.FLAG_KIND (report.py:398-399) labels seven with short descriptive words and open_flags falls back to the raw key for the other five, so the Overview callout (index.html:706, agent-only mode only) prints a mix of labels and raw keys; the study page prints raw keys for all (1604-1605); todos.py:213-216 has a third set of four titles; GUIDE.md:320 names five sources differently. todos.build turns only synthesis_verify/term_validation/evidence_check/eligibility_doubt (194), manual_edit (224-239) and feedback-with-'-> agent:' (241-256) into to-dos. Of the remaining sources, 'reassess' (screening.py:301-304), 'source_changed' (rob.py:28-30) and 'screening_unclear' (screening.py:539-541) explicitly ask the person to check something yet never become a to-do and are visible only on the study page and in the report's open-flags section, while next_action reports 'Everything is up to date'; 'rerun' (actions.py:248) and 'update' (engine.py:291) are markers for a pending change set that is itself a 'cs:' to-do, so they are answered through it. The flag options 'Ask the agent to screen it again' (eligibility_doubt, todos.py:204) and 'Correct it (describe below)' (term_validation, 208) both resolve the flag and return a generic feedback job on the flagged node (471-478): for screen:<rid> this is a FEEDBACK_VERIFY/APPLY over the node JSON (feedback.py:340-377), not a re-screen; for ext:<rid> it is a verified proposal, not a direct edit.

Scenario: Producers found by grep over the package: store.flag default source 'feedback' (store.py:166, used by feedback.py:255, 263-264 and commit 694), 'manual_edit' (actions.py:94), 'rerun' (actions.py:248), 'update' (engine.py:291), 'term_validation' (extraction.py:339, 403), 'synthesis_verify' (synthesis.py:157), 'reassess' (screening.py:301-304), 'source_changed' (rob.py:28-30), 'screening'/'screening_unclear' (screening.py:530-546 via want[0]), 'eligibility_doubt' (extraction.py:208 DOUBT_SOURCE, raised at 244-247 only when a person had decided the inclusion). 'evidence_check' appears only as a consumer (todos.py:194, 215; report.py:399) - no producer. Labels: report.FLAG_KIND maps seven keys to short descriptive words (report.py:398-399); open_flags substitutes the label when present and the raw key otherwise (411-412), so by_source holds mixed labels and keys. todos.py:213-216 has its own four titles. Overview: flagBox prints Object.entries(of.by_source) (index.html:706) - i.e. the seven mapped labels plus raw 'feedback', 'manual_edit', 'rerun', 'update', 'source_changed' - and only when human_mode === 'agent_only'; the study page prints f.source raw for every flag (1604-1605). GUIDE.md:320 lists 'evidence check, term validation, self-check, feedback, your edit'. To-dos: todos.build makes a 'flag' to-do only for synthesis_verify/term_validation/evidence_check/eligibility_doubt (194), a 'manual_edit' to-do (224-239) and a 'disagreement' to-do for feedback flags containing '-> agent:' (241-256). Traced the rest: 'reassess' is raised after a protocol change when the agent would now decide differently from the person's kept decision, text 'Please check.' (screening.py:301-304) - no to-do, next_action ends at 'Everything is up to date' (todos.py:691); 'source_changed' ('Ask for a re-assessment if the new text should change it', rob.py:28-30) - no to-do; 'screening_unclear' ('Check this inclusion', screening.py:539-541) - no to-do (the 'screening' source is covered indirectly because its node has decision 'awaiting', which is the eligibility to-do). 'rerun' and 'update' flags are 'pending review' markers for a change set that does have a to-do ('cs:<id>', todos.py:316-321) and are resolved by accept/reject (feedback.py:699-701, 736-737), so they are not unanswered. Option labels: flag:fix for any source resolves the flag 'sent to the agent as feedback' and returns {action:'feedback', target:node_id, text} (todos.py:471-478). For eligibility_doubt the node is screen:<rid>; handle_feedback -> _revise_target runs FEEDBACK_VERIFY/FEEDBACK_APPLY on the screen node JSON (feedback.py:340-377) - screen_one is not called, so 'Ask the agent to screen it again' is a feedback verification, not a re-screen. For term_validation the node is ext:<rid>; 'Correct it (describe below)' sends the text as feedback that the verifier checks against the source and may propose; a direct edit of the value is only available on the study page (actions.edit).

Evidence:
- `sragent/stages/report.py:398` — FLAG_KIND maps 7 of 12 keys; open_flags 411-412 uses FLAG_KIND.get(source, source) so by_source mixes labels and raw keys

#### I73 · The undecided eligibility state has six spellings (awaiting, uncertain, unclear, pending, undecided, 'awaiting classification'); estimate tests a value that is never stored; the 'include at title/abstract' branch is unreachable

*confirmed · low · screening · reasons: R1*

The undecided eligibility state is spelled six ways: the stored decision is 'awaiting' (finalize, screening.py:37-41); the model returns 'uncertain' (prompts.py:142) and rule_decision uses the same label internally; per-criterion assessments are 'unclear'; pipeline.fulltext_unclear takes 'await' (config.py:89); studies.GROUPS uses 'pending' (studies.py:21), a word also used for the extraction recheck outcome, screen_verify 'pending_disagreement', verify-screening --mode pending and a queued answer; the UI prints 'Undecided:' (index.html:1014), 'undecided' (steps.py:42, studies.py:72-75), 'Awaiting' (study-page button, index.html:1540) and 'Pending / undecided or waiting for a PDF' (index.html:687); the report and GUIDE say 'awaiting classification' (report.py:398). estimate.py:95-96 tests stored nodes for 'uncertain', which no code path stores. OPEN_AT_TA includes 'include' (screening.py:34) but finalize never returns it at title/abstract and both human include paths require a full text, so the include-at-title/abstract branches in estimate.py:95-96 and studies.py:82 handle a state reachable only through a crash between fetch and full-text screening (studies.py:82's clause is also shadowed by the 'include' return at line 64).

Scenario: finalize (screening.py:37-41) stores 'awaiting' at full text for an 'uncertain' rule_decision under policy 'await' and 'forward' at title/abstract. The prompt asks the model for 'uncertain' (prompts.py:142), kept only as llm_decision; rule_decision's internal label is also 'uncertain' (screening.py:174); per-criterion assessments use 'unclear'; config.fulltext_unclear takes 'await' (config.py:89). estimate.py:95-96 filters stored title/abstract nodes with decision in ('include','uncertain','forward'): a repo grep shows no code path stores 'uncertain' as a node decision (finalize maps it to awaiting/forward/include/exclude; verify_screen returns include/exclude/forward/awaiting; rules.restore returns before or 'forward'; human paths write include/exclude/awaiting), so that branch is dead. studies.GROUPS uses 'pending' (studies.py:21) and status_of prints 'undecided: ...' / 'undecided after full-text review' (studies.py:72-75); 'pending' is also the extraction recheck outcome (extraction.py:275), screen_verify outcome 'pending_disagreement', verify-screening --mode pending (screening.py:313) and t.pending for a queued answer (server.py:854). The UI prints 'Undecided:' in the eligibility table (index.html:1014), 'undecided' in the step summary (steps.py:42) and studies status, 'Awaiting' as the study-page decision button (index.html:1540 via human('awaiting')), and 'Pending / undecided or waiting for a PDF' as the KPI (index.html:687); report.FLAG_KIND and the GUIDE say 'awaiting classification' (report.py:398). OPEN_AT_TA = ('forward','include') (screening.py:34), but finalize returns only exclude|forward at title/abstract, and both human include paths refuse without a fulltext ft node (todos.py:437-438, actions.py:116-117); an 'include' with stage 'title_abstract' could arise only if an ft node of kind fulltext existed while the screen node was still at the title/abstract stage (e.g. a crash between fetch_one and screen_one in review_one), so estimate.py:95-96 and the second disjunct of studies.py:82 handle a practically dead state; studies.py:82's include-at-TA clause is additionally unreachable because status_of already returned at line 64 for any 'include'.

Evidence:
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:37` — finalize: TA -> exclude|forward; FT 'uncertain' -> awaiting|include|exclude by policy; OPEN_AT_TA at 34
- `sragent/prompts.py:142` — model schema decision include|exclude|uncertain
- `sragent/estimate.py:95` — decision in ('include','uncertain','forward') on stored TA nodes; 'uncertain' never stored
- `sragent/studies.py:21` — GROUPS included/pending/excluded; 'undecided ...' strings at 72-75; forward / include-at-TA branch at 82 (dead second disjunct, line 64 returns first)
- `sragent/config.py:89` — fulltext_unclear: 'await'
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:687` — KPI 'Pending' / 'undecided or waiting for a PDF'; 'Undecided:' at 1014; include/exclude/awaiting buttons at 1540
- `sragent/ui/steps.py:42` — 'undecided' and 'waiting for full-text review' in the step summary
- `sragent/stages/report.py:398` — FLAG_KIND 'screening' -> 'awaiting classification'

#### I76 · 'feedback' is a model bucket (the default for every unknown tag), a pipeline step that is always done, a to-do group, a CLI command, a flag source default and a config section

*confirmed · low · models-config · reasons: R1, R6*

'feedback' names the model bucket for the last LLM_STAGES entry ('Feedback, revisions and updates', config.py:267-270) and the default of stage_of_tag (276-283), which context.StageAgent (context.py:24) uses to pick the model for every ctx.agent call outside a pipeline stage (sync, pipeline, pdf_/rerun_/propagate_ revisions, vision_page); verifier tags (screen_verify_*, eligibility_verify) and ui_test_model do not pass through it. It is also the always-done Overview step '8. Feedback & revisions' (todos.py:29; ui/steps.py:51), the to-do group 'Agent answers' / 'The agent's answers to your feedback' (index.html:731, 745), the CLI command (cli.py:323), the job action (server.py:161, 268), the FB change-set id prefix (feedback.py:211), store.flag's default source (store.py:166, read by todos.py:244), the config section (config.py:120) and engine.STAGE_FOR's revise/integrate stage (engine.py:31). The Models table shows the 'Feedback, revisions and updates' row between Synthesis and 'Checks (verifier)' (server.py:738-756). The usage tab lists raw tags and never bills screen_verify_full_text under 'feedback'.

Scenario: 'feedback' is: (a) the last LLM_STAGES entry with label 'Feedback, revisions and updates' (config.py:267-270) and the default of stage_of_tag for every unrecognised tag (276-283), which context.StageAgent uses to pick the model for ctx.agent calls outside a stage (context.py:24) - so sync, pipeline, pdf_*, rerun_*, *_revise_dependent, *_integrate, feedback_locate and vision_page run on stage_models.feedback; screen_verify_*, eligibility_verify and ui_test_model do NOT go through it (explicit checkers at screening.py:329-330, extraction.py:230; a dedicated LLM(cfg,'agent',...) at server.py:890-892); (b) todos.STEPS ('feedback', '8. Feedback & revisions') (todos.py:29) whose Overview row is always done: ui/steps.py:51 sets ('feedback': (True, ...)); (c) the to-do group ['feedback','Agent answers'] in the jump bar (index.html:731) with heading 'The agent's answers to your feedback' (745) holding clarify/changeset/disagreement; (d) the CLI command (cli.py:323), the job action (server.py:161, 268-269), the FB change-set id prefix (feedback.py:211), store.flag's default source (store.py:166) which todos.py:244 reads for disagreement flags, the config section (config.py:120) and engine.STAGE_FOR's revise/integrate stage (engine.py:31). The Models table is built by stage_rows iterating LLM_STAGES (feedback last) and then appending the verifier role row (server.py:738-756), so 'Feedback, revisions and updates' sits between Synthesis and 'Checks (verifier)'. Refuted element: the usage tab never bills under 'feedback' - it lists raw tags (studies.py:384-392, index.html:1740), and screen_verify_full_text runs on the fulltext stage model (or the verifier), not the feedback model.

#### I79 · Status vocabularies shown raw or overloaded: ten snake_case proposal statuses (plus 'confirmed', 'applied', 'refused_stale') printed verbatim; seven job statuses vs five in the GUIDE, 'cancelled' uncoloured; 'done' is a job status, a step status and a run-file status; 'PAUSED' only in the CLI

*confirmed · low · ui-frontend · reasons: R1*

Change-set statuses in code are proposed, needs_clarification, rejected_by_agent, no_change, confirmed, accepted, stale, rejected_by_user (feedback.py:213,257,261,309,702,713,731) plus clarified/withdrawn (todos.py:483); studies.py _changesets() serialises them unmapped and the Activity proposals tab (index.html:1721) prints them raw through badge()/tone(), which colours only proposed, accepted and confirmed; the decisions tab (1731) likewise prints log_answer statuses 'applied', 'refused_stale' and 'queued' raw. Job statuses are queued, running, done, stopped, failed, cancelled, interrupted (server.py:412, 435/444, 138) while GUIDE.md:277 lists five and tone() leaves 'cancelled' uncoloured; 'done' is also the step status (ui/steps.py:60) and part of 'review done' (ui/configs.py:28-37). The over-claim 'PAUSED which the Configs page never shows' is wrong in substance: the Configs list renders the same pending() state as a 'settings changed' badge (index.html:1821, from configs.py:122), the header reads 'Paused: settings changed' (580) and the accept panel says 'is paused' (1988); only the wording differs (PAUSED / settings changed / paused). A naming-only issue; nothing functional depends on it.

Scenario: Proposals: feedback.py writes status 'proposed' (213), 'needs_clarification' (257), 'rejected_by_agent'/'no_change' (261), 'confirmed' (309), 'accepted' (702), 'stale' (713), 'rejected_by_user' (731); todos.py:483 writes 'clarified'/'withdrawn'. studies.py _changesets() passes cs.get('status') through unmapped to /api/history (server.py:1102-1104). renderActivity's proposals tab prints badge(p.status, tone(p.status)) at index.html:1721; tone() (491-497) only matches proposed (warn), accepted/confirmed (ok) and done/queued/running/failed/stopped/interrupted, so rejected_by_user, rejected_by_agent, no_change, stale, needs_clarification, clarified and withdrawn appear as raw snake_case with no colour. The decisions tab (1731) prints x.status raw: log_answer() statuses 'applied' (todos.py:527,566,591,600), 'refused_stale' (551) and 'queued' (server.py mutate). Jobs: server.py:412 sets done|stopped|failed, 435/444 'cancelled', 138 'interrupted', plus queued/running = seven; GUIDE.md:277 lists five (queued, running, done, failed, cancelled); tone() has no case for 'cancelled' so jobRow (1687) shows it uncoloured. 'done' is also the step status (ui/steps.py:60: needs you | done | ready to run | to do) and 'review done' is in run_status (ui/configs.py:28-37). PAUSED: cli.py:130 appends ', PAUSED: fixed settings changed' for `sragent runs` only; the UI uses other words for the same pending() state.

Evidence:
- `sragent/feedback.py:257` — needs_clarification (257), rejected_by_agent|no_change (261), confirmed (309), accepted (702), stale (713), rejected_by_user (731), proposed (213)
- `sragent/todos.py:483` — 'clarified' / 'withdrawn'; log_answer 'applied' at 527/566/591/600, 'refused_stale' at 551
- `sragent/studies.py:375` — history() -> _changesets() emits 'status': cs.get('status') with no label mapping
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:491` — tone(): ok for done|accepted|...|confirmed, info for running|queued, bad for failed|interrupted|stopped, warn for proposed|open...; nothing for cancelled, rejected_*, stale, no_change, needs_clarification, clarified, withdrawn, applied
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1721` — proposals tab: badge(p.status, tone(p.status), 'dot') raw; decisions tab 1731: badge(x.status, tone(x.status)) raw
- `sragent/ui/server.py:412` — 'done'|'stopped'|'failed'; 'cancelled' at 435 and 444; 'interrupted' at 138
- `GUIDE.md:277` — Activity row lists job status as (queued, running, done, failed, cancelled)
- `sragent/ui/steps.py:60` — step status: needs you | done | ready to run | to do
