#### I81 · To-do items carry five parallel names (kind, id prefix, step, page anchor, jump-bar label vs heading); the module docstring lists 7 kinds while the code produces 12; the jump chips omit the fields group

*confirmed, cosmetic · low · human-loop · reasons: R1, R7*

To-do items carry parallel names that never collide in code but differ for the reader: kind 'manual_edit' has id prefix 'edited:', group/anchor 'edits', chip 'Your edits' (todos.py:231, 684; index.html:733, 753); kind 'eligibility' has id 'elig:' and step 'screen' (todos.py:153); kind 'fulltexts' sits on step 'fulltext' (166); kind 'flag' has anchor 'flags', chip 'Flagged points', heading 'Points the agent flagged' (index.html:732, 754); changeset, clarify and disagreement share anchor 'feedback', chip 'Agent answers', heading 'The agent's answers to your feedback' (731, 745); kind 'sync' has step 'synthesis' although its plan lists screen/extraction/rob work (todos.py:329-340); next_action returns anchors, not kinds (655-690). The module docstring (todos.py:8-15) lists 7 kinds while build emits 12 (also search 81, fields 99, manual_edit 231, disagreement 248, clarify 296). The jump-chip list (index.html:731-733) omits 'fields' although the card has id g-fields (741), and chip labels differ from headings ('Risk of bias' vs 'Risk of bias: points neither agent could settle', 1113). Naming/documentation only; no behaviour depends on the mismatch.

Scenario: All naming facts hold in the source but none changes behaviour. todos.py: docstring lists protocol, eligibility, fulltexts, flag, rob, changeset, sync (8-15); build emits ids/kinds 'protocol' (55-57), 'search' (81), 'fields' (99), 'elig:'/'eligibility' step 'screen' (153), 'fulltexts' step 'fulltext' (166), 'flag:'/'flag' (218), 'edited:'/'manual_edit' (231), 'disagree:'/'disagreement' (248), 'rob:'/'rob' (270), 'clarify:'/'clarify' (296), 'cs:'/'changeset' (316), 'sync' step 'synthesis' (340) although its plan covers screen/extraction/rob work (engine.plan summary at 329-336 'Planned: screen reassess x.., extraction extract x..'). next_action returns 'group' values 'protocol', 'search', 'feedback', 'eligibility', 'sync', 'edits', 'flags', 'rob' (todos.py:655-690), which goNext passes to goTodoGroup -> scrollToId('g-'+group) (index.html:659-664), so the group must match a card id, not a kind: 'edits' for kind manual_edit, 'feedback' for changeset+clarify+disagreement, 'flags' for kind flag. The jump chips are built from the groups list at 731-733 (protocol, search, feedback 'Agent answers', eligibility, sync 'Out-of-date outputs', fulltexts, edits 'Your edits', flags 'Flagged points', rob 'Risk of bias', ruled) - no 'fields', although the fields card has id g-fields (741); group headings differ: 'The agent's answers to your feedback' (745), 'Points the agent flagged' (754), 'Risk of bias: points neither agent could settle' (1113). The fields card is an always-optional collapsed details element with no count, so its absence from the count-filtered chips (filter(x => x[2])) is consistent with the chip rule, though inconsistent with the other optional cards (protocol-approved and search-ran are also absent when their count is 0). No code path depends on these names matching: scrolling, filtering and next_action all use the anchor ids consistently.

Evidence:
- `sragent/todos.py:8` — docstring lists 7 kinds; build also emits search (81), fields (99), manual_edit (231), disagreement (248), clarify (296) = 12 kinds
- `sragent/todos.py:153` — id 'elig:', kind 'eligibility', step 'screen' (issue cites 155)
- `sragent/todos.py:340` — sync to-do step 'synthesis' (issue cites 339); its context lists screen/extraction/rob work from engine.plan (329-336)
- `sragent/todos.py:684` — next_action group 'edits' for kind manual_edit; 'flags', 'feedback', 'sync' likewise anchors
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:731` — jump-chip groups list (no 'fields'); g-fields card at 741; headings at 745, 754, 1113; goTodoGroup at 664 scrolls to 'g-'+group

#### I83 · 'run' names six things (CLI command, the Run page, the job, the run file, the run folder, the evaluation run), and the Run page subtitle promises 'choose a model' while stage jobs hide the model selector

*confirmed, cosmetic · low · ui-frontend · reasons: R1*

'run' does name six things: the CLI commands `sragent run`/`sragent runs` (cli.py:295, 303), the Run page (TITLES.run, index.html:457), the job action 'run' (labelled 'Continue the review' 1229, 'Run' 1258, 'Run review up to ...' server.py:155, 'Run to here' 700), the YAML 'Run file' (KIND_LABEL 1749, 'Run files' 1849, 'Open another run file' 403), the 'Run folder' (402) and an evaluation 'run in eval/runs/' (1243). The claim that stage jobs hide the model selector is wrong: for run/pdfs/sync the single 'Agent model' select is replaced by stageCard() (1245, 1266-1289), a 'Models per stage' table with a model and effort select per stage saved as stage_models, so the subtitle 'choose a model' is satisfied; only the card header becomes 'Budget' and 'Test model' becomes 'Test models'. Remaining issue is vocabulary overlap only.

Scenario: The six uses of 'run' are all real: cli.py:295 `sub.add_parser("run")` and :303 `"runs"`; the Run page TITLES.run at index.html:457; the job action 'run' labelled 'Continue the review' in WHAT (1229), 'Run' on #runbtn (1258), 'Run review up to <stage>' in server.py:155 label_for, and 'Run to here' on the Overview (700); KIND_LABEL.run = 'Run file' (1749), the 'Run files' card (1849), 'Open another run file' (403); the sidebar runname title 'Run folder' (402); and 'Each evaluation is its own run in eval/runs/' (1243). The second claim does not hold: renderRun inserts `${STAGE_JOB(a.action) ? stageCard() : ""}` at 1245 immediately before the Budget card; stageCard() (1266-1289) renders a 'Models per stage' table with a model select and an effort select per stage, saved to the run file as stage_models ('Save to run file', 1288) plus a 'Test models' button. So for run/pdfs/sync the single 'Agent model' select is replaced by per-stage model choice, not hidden, and the subtitle 'choose a model' is met (GUIDE.md:274 documents exactly this: 'the model and reasoning effort per stage'). Only the card header changes to 'Budget' and the single 'Test model' button moves into the stage card.

Evidence:
- `sragent/cli.py:295` — sub-parser 'run'; 'runs' at 303
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:457` — TITLES.run = ['Run','Start the agent, choose a model and see the cost first']
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1245` — `${STAGE_JOB(a.action) ? stageCard() : ""}` renders the per-stage model table for run/pdfs/sync before the Budget card (1246)
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1266` — stageCard(): 'Models per stage' with a model select and an effort select per stage, 'Save to run file' (1288), 'Test models'
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1229` — WHAT: ['run','Continue the review',...]; 'Run to here' at 700; 'Run' button 1258
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:402` — runname title 'Run folder'; 'Open another run file' 403; KIND_LABEL run:'Run file' 1749; 'Run files' card 1849; 'its own run in eval/runs/' 1243
- `sragent/ui/server.py:155` — label_for('run') -> 'Run review up to <until>'
- `GUIDE.md:274` — Run row: 'the model and reasoning effort per stage ... saved in the run file as stage_models'

### Interface behaviour (5)

#### I25 · Stop cancels every queued job without confirmation and drops them from the history; Cancel removes one and keeps it; cancelJob toasts success unconditionally

*confirmed · medium · ui-backend · reasons: R8, R7*

App.stop (server.py:426-437) SIGTERMs the running job and marks every queued job 'cancelled' without an 'ended' time, then clears the queue without appending those jobs to self.history, so they vanish from Activity (jobs() 449-458) until a restart re-reads jobs.jsonl; App.cancel (439-447) removes one job, sets 'ended', records it and keeps it in history. stopRun (index.html:1345, called from the header button at 627 and the Run page at 1259) has no confirmation, and cancelJob (1710) toasts 'Job cancelled' without checking the response. The only stop controls are the whole running job (/api/stop) and one queued job (/api/cancel); there is no per-stage or per-item stop.

Scenario: Reviewer has one job running and two queued, then clicks 'Stop' in the header (index.html:627) or 'Stop the running job' on the Run page (1259): stopRun() (1345) POSTs /api/stop with no confirm dialog. App.stop (server.py:426-437) sets stop_requested, SIGTERMs the pid, then for each queued job sets status='cancelled' and _record()s it (no 'ended'), and clears the queue without appending to self.history. App.jobs() (449-458) builds 'queued' from self.queue (now empty) and 'done' from self.history (never received them), so the two queued jobs disappear from Activity; they only reappear after a UI restart, when _load_jobs (125-139) re-reads jobs.jsonl and shows them as 'cancelled' with no end time. By contrast App.cancel (439-447) removes one job, sets ended, records it and appends it to history, so a job cancelled by its row button (1688) stays visible. cancelJob (1710) does `await api('/api/cancel',{id}); toast('Job cancelled')` without reading the response; api() (486-489) returns {ok:false,...} on failure, so a cancel of a job that already started shows 'Job cancelled' anyway. The only stop routes are /api/stop (whole running job) and /api/cancel (one queued job); no per-stage or per-item stop exists in server.py or index.html (grep 'stop').

Evidence:
- `sragent/ui/server.py:426` — stop(): kill pid; for q in queue: status='cancelled', _record(q) (no ended); queue.clear(); nothing appended to history
- `sragent/ui/server.py:439` — cancel(): removes one queued job, sets ended, _record, history.append
- `sragent/ui/server.py:449` — jobs(): queued from self.queue, done from self.history, so stopped-queued jobs vanish until _load_jobs on restart (125-139)
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1345` — stopRun(): no confirm; header Stop button at 627 and Run page button at 1259 both call it
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1710` — cancelJob: toast('Job cancelled') regardless of the api() result; api() returns {ok:false} on error (486-489)

#### I100 · Client routing and counters: sidebar navigation bypasses the unsaved-config guard, run switching is a full reload via ?config= while views use hash routes, raw fetch/URL building bypasses the api()/U() helpers, and sidebar badge counts use different definitions than the pages they open

*confirmed · low · ui-frontend · reasons: R7*

The sidebar click handler (index.html:574) calls show(t) = nav(t) + activate(t) (538-539), and nav() uses pushState, so route() and its CF.dirty confirm (557-560) never run when leaving the YAML editor. The edits are not discarded at that moment (the editor is only hidden; CF.text keeps them) but they are lost without any prompt on the next re-entry through routing: Back/forward to #/configs/edit/<path> passes the guard (arg === CF.path), route() clears CF.shown (562) and renderConfigs() calls openEditor() (1800), which sets CF.dirty=false and reloads the file (1875-1886). Re-entering via the Configs link shows the confirm at that (late) moment instead. beforeunload (2156) still protects reloads. Run switching is a full reload via location.href='/?config=...' (openConfig 1786, acceptConfig 2010) with CFG read once (483); acceptConfig uses a raw fetch (2005) and study-page file links hand-build ?config= (1506-1508). The sidebar badge definitions (617-621) differ from the To-do page's groups ('ruled' from S.ruled, 'fields'/approved protocol flagged optional) but match the server's own 'open todo' definition (todos.py:634); that part is by design and cosmetic.

Scenario: Open a run file in the Configs editor and type: the textarea input handler (index.html:1904) sets CF.text and setDirty(true) (1908-1910). Click 'Overview' in the sidebar: the handler at 574 calls show('steps'); show() (539) = nav() + activate(); nav() (538) uses history.pushState, which fires no hashchange, and activate() (540-552) never calls route(), so the CF.dirty confirm at 557-560 is skipped. Nothing is destroyed yet: #v-configs is only hidden and CF.text keeps the edits. Then press the browser Back button: popstate -> route() with hash #/configs/edit/<path>; the guard is skipped because arg === CF.path (557), route() sets CF.shown = null (562) and activates configs -> renderConfigs() (1796-1805) -> openEditor(CF.arg) (1800) -> CF.dirty = false and the file is re-fetched and CF.text overwritten (1875-1886) -> the edits are gone with no prompt. If instead the user clicks 'Configs' in the sidebar, go('#/configs') reaches route() and the confirm appears then (on return rather than on leaving); Cancel pushes the edit hash back while the Overview stays visible. beforeunload (2156) still guards reloads. openConfig sets location.href='/?config=...#/steps' (1786) and acceptConfig likewise (2010) after a raw fetch('/api/config_accept?config=' + NOCFG.config) (2005); CFG is read once from location.search (483), so switching is a full reload. Study-page file links build '?config=' + encodeURIComponent(CFG) by hand (1506-1508) instead of U(). Sidebar counts (617-621): #todo-n = todos with !snoozed && !optional, #pdf-n = fulltexts files !present, #act-n = (current ? 1 : 0) + queued; the To-do page shows 'ruled' (from S.ruled, not a todo; 733) and 'fields' (optional:true, todos.py:99) and an approved protocol (optional, todos.py:61) that are not counted, which matches the server's own definition of 'open' todos in next_action (todos.py:634).

Evidence:
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:574` — nav click: `if (t === 'configs') go('#/configs'); else show(t);`
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:538` — nav(): history.pushState (no hashchange); show(): nav(t); activate(t) - route() not called
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:557` — route(): the only CF.dirty confirm; skipped when the target is #/configs/edit/<CF.path>; sets CF.shown = null at 562
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1800` — renderConfigs: `if (CF.sub === 'edit') return openEditor(CF.arg);`
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1875` — openEditor: CF.dirty = false; re-fetches /api/config_file and sets CF.text = f.text -> unsaved edits overwritten
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1908` — setDirty(v) sets CF.dirty from the textarea input listener (1904)
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:2156` — beforeunload guard on CF.dirty still covers reloads/close
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1786` — openConfig: location.href = '/?config=' + p + '#/steps'; acceptConfig 2010 same; raw fetch at 2005; CFG read once at 483

#### I101 · Cost confirmation and action-button vocabulary are inconsistent: only 'Redraft all' asks before spending (native confirm()), while Run/Re-run/Process PDFs/Update now do not; save/ask labels and button classes differ per card

*confirmed, cosmetic · low · ui-frontend · reasons: R7*

Only redraftRob asks before spending (native confirm() with the estimate, index.html:1375); route()/openConfig use confirm() for unsaved edits (558, 1783) and dupConfig uses prompt() (1867). Run (1258/1335), Re-run (1528/1656), Process added PDFs (1064/1382) and Update now (750/1169) start paid jobs without a dialog, but the claim that a reviewer cannot tell which controls cost money is an over-claim: Run has a live 'Estimated cost' card beside it (1257), Re-run shows 'about $X' next to the button (1528, 1651-1655), Update now's cost is on the Overview callout (703) plus a hint (752), no-LLM saves say '(no LLM)' and agent calls carry the bot icon; only Process added PDFs has no estimate, and the To-do sync card drops the '(about $X)' that todos.py:338-342 puts in sy.question. The save/confirm label inventory, the seven agent verbs and the mixed Edit-button classes are real but purely cosmetic vocabulary inconsistencies.

Scenario: Dialogs: confirm() in route() (index.html:558), redraftRob (1375, with the /api/estimate total) and openConfig (1783); prompt() in dupConfig (1867). No dialog on: the Run button (#runbtn 1258 -> startRun 1335-1344), Re-run (1528 -> doRerun 1656), Process added PDFs (1064 and the empty-state button -> processPdfs 1382-1390) and Update now (750 -> answerNow 1169). But cost is visible for most of them without a dialog: the Run button sits in the 'Estimated cost' card (1257-1258) that estimate() updates live; the Re-run button has #rrest 'about $X' beside it filled by studyEst() (1528, 1651-1655); Update now's cost is on the Overview callout 'Updating costs about $X' (703) and the hint '(a few cents with Gemini Flash)' (752) - though the To-do sync card renders sy.title, not sy.question which carries '(about $X)' (todos.py:338-342); Process added PDFs shows no estimate (renderPdfs/pdfPanel have none). No-LLM saves are labelled '(no LLM)' and agent calls carry the bot icon. Labels at the cited lines are real: 'Save criteria (no LLM)' 798, 'Save (no LLM)' 885/1542/1564/1591, 'Save and confirm (no LLM)' 1580, 'Save changes and confirm' 1099, 'Save N decisions' 1024, 'Save to run file' 1288, 'Confirm selected' 1109, 'Confirm this query' 935; verbs 'Ask' 802/928, 'Suggest alternatives' 923-924, 'Send to the agent' 1451/1614, 'Re-run' 1528, 'Ask the agent to re-assess instead' 1100, 'Redraft all risk-of-bias assessments' 759, 'Update now' 750; Edit buttons 'btn sm ghost' 795/1587, 'btn' 886, 'btn sm' 1566/1581.

Evidence:
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:558` — confirm() in route(); redraftRob 1375 (with estimated cost); openConfig 1783; prompt() dupConfig 1867
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1257` — 'Estimated cost' card with live estimate() directly above the Run button (1258); startRun 1335 has no dialog
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1528` — Re-run button with #rrest 'about $X' beside it (studyEst 1651-1655); doRerun 1656 no dialog
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:750` — Update now -> answerNow('sync','sync'); hint 'a few cents' at 752; Overview callout 703 'Updating costs about $X'; the card prints sy.title not sy.question (todos.py:338-342 puts '(about $X)' in question)
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1382` — processPdfs: /api/run action pdfs, no dialog and no estimate on the Full texts page (1040-1070)
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:798` — 'Save criteria (no LLM)'; 885/1542/1564/1591 'Save (no LLM)'; 1580 'Save and confirm (no LLM)'; 1099 'Save changes and confirm'; 1024 'Save N decisions'; 1288 'Save to run file'; 1109 'Confirm selected'; 935 'Confirm this query'
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:759` — 'Redraft all risk-of-bias assessments'; 'Ask' 802/928; 'Suggest alternatives' 923-924; 'Send to the agent' 1451/1614; 'Ask the agent to re-assess instead' 1100
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:795` — Edit buttons: btn sm ghost (795, 1587), btn (886), btn sm (1566, 1581)

#### I52 · Overview row controls are hidden, dead or positional: 'Run to here' is hover-only, 'Review N' anchors are missing for extract/snowball/report, the feedback row is always done, and the To-do page doubles as the settings page and the only home of 'Redraft all'

*confirmed, cosmetic · low · ui-frontend · reasons: R7, R1*

Overview row controls are inconsistent but not dead: 'Run to here' (index.html:700, class reveal) is hidden until hover or keyboard focus on hover-capable devices (CSS 280, inside @media (hover:hover); always visible on touch). goTodoStep (664-667) always opens the To-do page and scrolls only when a [data-step] anchor exists; anchors exist for plan, search, screen, synthesis, fulltext (redirected to #/pdfs), rob and every generic flag/manual-edit card (step from todos.py), and because 'Review N' is shown only for counted non-optional todos, every row that shows it has an anchor (extract included). Snowball has no Overview row at all (todos.py STEPS 26-30) though it is a pipeline stage and a Run select option; the Overview step key 'synthesis' differs from the pipeline stage key 'synthesize' (naming only). The feedback row is always done with no Run button (steps.py:51). The To-do page holds the approved protocol, data items and ran search as collapsed settings cards (738-743), lists the 'ruled' group ('No action needed', 1150) among the jump groups (731-733), and is the only home of 'Redraft all risk-of-bias assessments' (759). Links to a study's output differ by card: 'Study page' (1013), 'Report' + 'Study page' (1104), 'Open in report' in a new tab (1163).

Scenario: Verified: (1) 'Run to here' has class 'btn sm ghost reveal' (index.html:700); CSS at 280 is `@media (hover:hover){.stp .sa .reveal{opacity:0}...:hover/:focus-within{opacity:1}}`, so it is hidden until hover or keyboard focus on hover-capable devices and always visible on touch devices ('hover-only' over-claims). (2) goTodoStep (664-667): fulltext -> show('pdfs'); otherwise show('todo') then scrollIntoView on `#v-todo [data-step=step]` if found; the To-do page always opens, only the scroll is skipped when no anchor exists, so the button is never dead. data-step anchors: plan (793), search (920), screen (1022), synthesis (747, sync group), fulltext (754), rob (1112-1113), and generic cards with data-step=t.step (1161). 'Review N' on a row appears only when tcount has non-optional todos for that step (steps.py:13, 60); the only non-optional todos with step 'extract' are flag (todos.py:218) and manual_edit (231) cards, both rendered by card() with data-step='extract', so whenever the extract row shows 'Review N' an anchor exists (the 'fields' todo with step 'extract' at 99 is optional and not counted). The 'extract works only if a flag/manual-edit card happens to exist' point is therefore tautological, not a defect. (3) snowball has no Overview row at all (todos.py:26-30 STEPS lists 8 steps without snowball) although it is a pipeline stage (pipeline.py:86) and a Run select option (452). (4) data-step 'synthesis' matches the step key used by steps.py/todos.py consistently; only the pipeline stage key is 'synthesize' (naming). (5) feedback row: steps.py:51 (True, ..., None): always done, no Run-to-here; its 'Review N' anchors work via generic cards with step 'feedback' (todos.py:248, 296, 316). (6) To-do page: approved protocol (738-739), data items (740-741) and ran search (742-743) as collapsed <details>; 'ruled' listed in the jump groups (731-733) while its header says 'No action needed' (1150); 'Redraft all risk-of-bias assessments' exists only at 759 (grep redraftRob: 759 and definition 1372). (7) There is no literal 'See this output' label: eligibility rows have a 'Study page' SPA button (1013), RoB rows have both 'Report' (new tab) and 'Study page' (1104), generic cards have 'Open in report' (new tab, 1163). All real, all cosmetic/UX.

Evidence:
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:280` — @media (hover:hover) .reveal opacity 0; visible on :hover and :focus-within; always visible on touch
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:700` — Run to here button with class reveal
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:664` — goTodoStep: fulltext -> pdfs; else show('todo') and scroll if anchor found (never dead)
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1161` — generic card data-step=t.step: flag/manual_edit cards carry 'extract', 'feedback', 'synthesis', 'rob', 'screen'
- `sragent/todos.py:99` — 'fields' todo step 'extract' is optional (not counted in tcount) and rendered in g-fields without data-step
- `sragent/todos.py:26` — STEPS: 8 rows, no snowball row; pipeline REGISTRY has snowball (pipeline.py:86)
- `sragent/ui/steps.py:51` — feedback row: done True, run_until None
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:731` — groups include 'ruled' (count S.ruled); grpHead at 1150 says 'No action needed'

#### I99 · UI feedback inconsistencies: robConfirm and cancelJob render success without checking r.ok; reviewComment, ssClear, answerNow, robChange/robAgent, resolveComment are silent or inline-only; the study page and Activity sub-tabs do not reflect running jobs until a manual refresh

*confirmed · low · ui-frontend · reasons: R7*

robConfirm (index.html:1117-1124) renders a green 'saved' span and toasts 'Confirmed' without checking r.ok; the reachable failure is a server exception in /api/bulk, which do_POST answers as HTTP 500 {error} (server.py:1198-1199) so r.message is undefined and the toast still says 'Confirmed' (the agent_only refusal named in the issue is unreachable because todos.py:351-353 removes RoB todos in agent_only mode). cancelJob (1710) toasts 'Job cancelled' even when /api/cancel returns ok:false for a job that already started (server.py:1190, 439-449). reviewComment (1459-1463) gives an inline message only; ssClear via ssCall (954-962) and resolveComment (1665) toast only on failure; answerNow (1169-1173) writes to #m-<id> and refreshes after 400 ms; robChange/robAgent (1125-1137) write to a message target that the 500/800 ms re-render removes. doRerun (1656-1659) calls refresh() without force and the study page body is never re-rendered by polling (render() 639-645 has no studies branch; refresh() 588-591 reloads it only when a previous job ends), but the claim that no queued-job indicator appears is an over-claim: the sidebar/top-bar job widget (620-632) and the inline + toast 'queued (job X)...' message do appear. Activity sub-tabs other than jobs keep ACT.H until loadActivity() (tab open, 'Refresh' 1744, decide/cancel/resolve or a filter change). Severity low: the two false-success paths need a 500 or a cancel race and the immediate refresh shows the true state; the rest is inconsistent feedback style.

Scenario: robConfirm (index.html:1117-1124): after `const r = await api('/api/bulk', ...)` it unconditionally writes `<span class="saved">✓ ${esc(r.message)}</span>` and `toast(r.message || 'Confirmed')`. /api/bulk -> app.mutate -> _apply_op -> todos.bulk (server.py:1166-1167, 1072-1079); any exception there (e.g. todos.apply on a RoB node that no longer matches) is caught by do_POST's generic handler and answered HTTP 500 `{"error": ...}` (1198-1199); api() (486-489) returns that object, so r.ok and r.message are undefined, the span shows a green tick with empty text and the toast says 'Confirmed' while nothing was saved; refresh(true) then redraws the rows still unconfirmed. The specific agent_only path in the issue is NOT reachable from the UI: todos.py:351-353 keeps only 'fulltexts' todos in agent_only mode, so the RoB table is empty there. cancelJob (1710): `/api/cancel` returns {ok: app.cancel(id)} (server.py:1190-1191), false when the job already left the queue (cancel() 439-449 only removes queued entries), yet the UI toasts 'Job cancelled' regardless. reviewComment (1459-1463): msg('revcm', r) only, no toast/refresh (studyComment at 1660-1664 toasts and reloads). ssClear (980) -> ssCall (954-962): inline msg + refresh; toast only when !r.ok. answerNow (1169-1173): msg into #m-<id>, refresh(true) after 400 ms. robChange/robAgent (1125-1137): msg('robm-'+rid, r) then on success delete robEdit[rid] and refresh(true) after 500/800 ms -> renderTodo drops the edit block and the message with it; no toast. resolveComment (1665): toast only on failure. doRerun (1656-1659): msg + toast + refresh() without force; refresh() (581-593) re-renders the study page only when lastJobId changes and a previous job existed (`if (was && tab === 'studies')`), and render(fromPoll) (639-645) has no 'studies' branch, so the study page body never shows the queued job; the sidebar job widget #jobw/#topjob and #act-n (620-632) do update every 3 s poll (2166), and the inline/toast message 'queued (job X); the result appears in the To-do list for approval' (server.py:1184-1185) is shown. Activity: render(fromPoll) calls renderJobsCard(fromPoll) (1692-1695) which re-fetches only /api/jobs; ACT.H (proposals, comments, decisions, changes, usage) is refetched only by loadActivity() on tab activation, the 'Refresh' button (1744), decideCs, cancelJob, resolveComment or a filter change.

Evidence:
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1123` — robConfirm: `M.innerHTML = <span class=saved>${esc(r.message)}</span>; toast(r.message || 'Confirmed'); refresh(true)` with no r.ok check
- `sragent/ui/server.py:1198` — do_POST: `except Exception as e: return self._send(500, {'error': str(e)})` -> no ok/message keys; api() (index.html:486-489) returns that object
- `sragent/todos.py:351` — agent_only: `todos = [t for t in todos if t['kind'] == 'fulltexts']` -> no RoB table in agent_only, so that refusal path is unreachable from the UI
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1710` — cancelJob: `await api('/api/cancel',{id}); toast('Job cancelled')` unconditional; server.py:1190 returns {ok: app.cancel(id)} which is False once the job started
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1462` — reviewComment: msg('revcm', r) only; compare studyComment 1663 (toast + openStudy)
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:954` — ssCall: msg(#m-search) + refresh(true); toast only if !r.ok (used by ssClear 980)
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1169` — answerNow: msg into #m-<id>; setTimeout(refresh(true), 400)
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1129` — robChange/robAgent: msg('robm-'+rid) then delete robEdit[rid] + refresh(true) after 500/800 ms, so the row re-renders without the msg target

### Documentation versus code (7)

#### I51 · Declared graph edges differ from written edges: screen never depends on cfg:screening_rules/cfg:human_mode, rule/manual-created screen nodes lack crit:*, stale fulltext nodes are counted but never acted on, the report depends only on syn:*, and GUIDE 6.1 gives a third list

*not yet verified · high · graph-engine · reasons: R2*

graph.TYPES declares screen depends on cfg:screening_rules and cfg:human_mode (sragent/graph.py:41), but screen_one writes [rec, protocol] + crit:* (+ft) (screening.py:247-250) and rules.apply/exclude and actions.edit write [rec, protocol] for a new node (rules.py:102, 142; actions.py:126), so a rule-set or mode change accepted via config_lock versions the cfg node and makes nothing stale, and rule-made nodes never see criterion changes through _changed_criteria. graph.stale includes fulltext nodes (graph.py:135 excludes only setting/record) but engine.plan has no fulltext branch (engine.py:79-116), so a stale ft: is counted on the Overview and never resolved. The report node depends only on active synthesis ids (report.py:930-931) although it renders screening, extraction, RoB and search, and write_reports is called ad hoc (actions.edit only if a synthesis exists; todos.apply never except cs:accept), so report.md can be older than the store while the node is current. GUIDE.md:240 lists 'rec, protocol, criteria (+ ft)' and omits cfg:rob_tool for rob (243 vs graph.py:97-99).

Evidence:
- `sragent/graph.py:41` — TYPES screen: cfg:screening_rules, cfg:human_mode (never written)
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:247` — deps = [rec, protocol] + crit:* (+ src_node)
- `sragent/rules.py:102` — deps = sc['depends_on'] if sc else [rec, protocol]
- `sragent/graph.py:135` — stale() includes fulltext; engine.plan 79-116 has no fulltext branch
- `sragent/stages/report.py:930` — report depends_on = active synthesis ids only
- `GUIDE.md:240` — 6.1 screen dependency list differs from TYPES and from the code; rob row omits cfg:rob_tool (243)

#### I19 · human_decisions.jsonl content for the same decision depends on the entry point and on whether a job was running; queued edits/bulk/comments never show the 'queued' badge; agent-made flags and auto-accepts are attributed to the user

*confirmed · medium · human-loop · reasons: R4, R8*

human_decisions.jsonl is appended only by todos.log_answer (todos.py:385) and actions.edit (actions.py:225-227). The same decision therefore produces different records by entry point: /api/answer writes one '<kind>:<id>' applied line, preceded by a 'queued' line when a job was running (server.py:490-495); a RoB domain change made in the To-do editor writes two lines ('edit:rob:<rid>' from actions.edit and 'rob:<rid>' from apply, todos.py:522-528) while the same change from the study page writes one; /api/bulk logs per item except pdf_unwant (todos.py:604-608, provenance only); /api/comment and /api/comment_resolve write only an 'op:comment'/'op:resolve_comment' queued line when a job runs and nothing otherwise (actions.add_comment/resolve_comment write the changelog and provenance.jsonl only); /api/protocol, /api/search_strategy, /api/data_items, /api/rerun, /api/propagate, /api/run and every CLI command write nothing. state() marks a to-do pending only when its id equals a queued todo_id (server.py:855-857), so queued 'op:*' writes never show the badge; moreover only the protocol card (index.html:794) and the generic card (1162) render t.pending, so even a queued eligibility, RoB, sync or full-text answer shows no badge (the Activity page shows only a global pending count, 1701). submitTodo (1175-1181) has no running-job check, so the same answer can be picked and submitted again and is queued and applied twice. Separately, handle_feedback's 'pending review' and '-> agent:' flags (feedback.py:255, 263-264), _finish's rerun flag (actions.py:248) and commit()'s default actor on auto_accept (feedback.py:671, called at 249) all record actor='user' for agent-made changes, so the Activity 'You' filter (studies.history actor filter, index.html:1731) attributes them to the person.

Scenario: Only two places ever append to human_decisions.jsonl: todos.log_answer (sragent/todos.py:385, called at 527/551/566/591/600 and from server.py:493/514/544) and actions.edit (actions.py:225-227). Traced paths: (1) Reviewer answers a to-do via /api/answer while no job runs -> App.answer -> todos.apply -> one line {todo_id:'elig:R1', option, status:'applied'}. Same answer while a job runs -> App.answer appends to pending_answers and writes a 'queued' line first (server.py:490-495); _drain_answers then applies it and todos.apply writes the 'applied' line, so the same decision yields two lines. (2) Reviewer changes a RoB domain in the To-do 'Change' editor -> todos.apply branch opt=='change' with extra.changes (todos.py:522-528) -> actions.edit writes {todo_id:'edit:rob:R1', option:'manual_edit'} and then apply writes {todo_id:'rob:R1', option:'change'}: two lines for one decision. The same edit made on the study page (/api/edit -> App.mutate -> actions.edit) yields one line, plus a 'queued' line with todo_id 'op:edit' if a job was running (server.py:543-544). (3) /api/bulk -> todos.bulk: eligibility/restore_rule/pdf_unavailable/rob_confirm each log per item; pdf_unwant (todos.py:604-608) only calls provenance.record. (4) /api/comment -> App.comment -> App.mutate({_op:'comment'}): no job -> actions.add_comment (actions.py:31-44) writes the store changelog and provenance.jsonl, never human_decisions.jsonl; job running -> one 'queued' line with todo_id 'op:comment'. /api/comment_resolve identical with 'op:resolve_comment'; actions.resolve_comment (47-56) only writes the changelog. (5) /api/protocol (protocol_change 624-653), /api/search_strategy (655-702), /api/data_items (548-622), /api/rerun and /api/propagate (1177-1189 -> subprocess actions.rerun/propagate_from, which call provenance.record only), /api/run, and every CLI command (cli.py: cmd_accept/cmd_reject call provenance.record + feedback.accept/reject; no log_answer anywhere in cli.py) write nothing to the file. (6) state() computes pend = {a['todo_id'] for a in pending_answers} and sets t['pending'] = t['id'] in pend (server.py:855-857); queued ops carry todo_id 'op:edit'/'op:bulk'/'op:comment'/'op:resolve_comment', which never equal a to-do id, so no badge. Even for a queued /api/answer on 'elig:R1' or 'rob:R1' the badge is never drawn, because only the protocol card (index.html:794) and the generic card (1162) render t.pending; eligTable (998), robTable (1088), the sync card and pdfPanel do not. The Activity page shows only a global count 'N of your answers wait for the running job' (1701). (7) submitTodo (1175-1181) deletes answers[id] and refreshes; nothing checks S.job.current, so the reviewer can pick an option again and submit the same answer a second time; it is queued and applied again (for 'cs:' accept the second apply raises in feedback.accept and is logged 'failed' by _drain_answers 513-514). (8) Actor attribution: feedback.handle_feedback flags the target 'Feedback FB.. pending review' with actor='user' (feedback.py:255) and the disagreement flag '-> agent: ...' with actor='user' (263-264); actions._finish flags 'Re-run ... pending review' with actor='user', source='rerun' (actions.py:248); commit(ctx, sandbox, cs, actor='user') (671) is called with the default actor on auto_accept (249), so every node version it writes says actor 'user' although no person accepted. studies.history filters changelog entries by e['actor'] (studies.py:375-380) and the Activity 'changes' tab offers the filter 'You' = actor user (index.html:1731), which therefore lists these agent-made flags and auto-accepted versions.

Evidence:
- `sragent/todos.py:385` — log_answer is the only writer besides actions.edit; call sites 527 (rob change via actions.edit), 551 (refused_stale), 566 (end of apply), 591, 600 (bulk restore_rule / pdf_unavailable)
- `sragent/actions.py:225` — actions.edit appends {'todo_id': f'edit:{kind}:{rid|node}', 'option': 'manual_edit', ...}; add_comment (31-44) and resolve_comment (47-56) never touch the file
- `sragent/ui/server.py:490` — answer(): job running -> pending_answers.append + log_answer 'queued'; else todos.apply (which logs 'applied')
- `sragent/ui/server.py:543` — mutate(): job running -> log_answer with todo_id 'op:<_op>' and status 'queued'; no job -> _apply_op with no human_decisions line for comment/resolve_comment
- `sragent/ui/server.py:855` — pend = {a['todo_id'] ...}; t['pending'] = t['id'] in pend
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1162` — generic card renders t.pending badge; 794 protocol card; eligTable (998), robTable (1088), pdfPanel (1040) and the sync card never render it; submitTodo (1175-1181) has no running-job check
- `sragent/feedback.py:255` — ctx.store.flag(target, 'Feedback .. pending review', actor='user'); 263-264 disagreement flag actor='user'; 671 commit default actor='user', used at 249 on auto_accept
- `sragent/actions.py:248` — _finish: ctx.store.flag(target, '.. pending review', actor='user', source='rerun')

#### I48 · Estimate assumptions drift from the real calls: verifier priced at the agent role, output tokens from DEFAULTS only, long_context ignored, hard-coded sync/feedback/rerun counts, a fixed eval USD table for an old model setup, wrong default runs root

*confirmed · medium · models-config · reasons: R6, R1*

estimate_stages prices screen_verify/rob_verify/synthesis_verify with roles.verifier else the agent role (sragent/estimate.py:180-181); for screen_verify the real call uses the fulltext/screen stage model when no verifier is set (screening.py:329-330), and the eligibility_verify check (extraction.py:230) has no estimate row at all; rob_verify and synthesis_verify do use the agent role (synthesis.py:134, rob.py:110) so they are priced consistently. Output tokens come only from DEFAULTS by effort (history 'out' is collected at 61-63 but never read; 157); long_context pricing applied by llm.py:250-254 is ignored (159). history()'s default root Path('runs') cannot see runs/<kind>/<topic>/<run>, but every caller passes run_dir.parent (server.py:292,296), so that is dead code. server.estimate hard-codes pdfs/sync/feedback/rerun/propagate call counts (server.py:253-274) and returns a fixed per-suite USD table noted 'Gemini Flash agent, Gemini Pro judge' for eval whatever the config (275-281); ROB_FLAG_RATE 1.0 comes from one run (estimate.py:35). The index.html:1323 note is accurate about spend (cached lines carry usd 0.0). processPdfs on the PDFs page (index.html:1382) starts the job without an estimate although the Run page's 'Process added PDFs' option shows one; the To-do 'Update now' card does show '(about $X)' (todos.py:338); redraftRob shows its estimate in a native confirm() (1375).

Scenario: 1) Config with stage_models.fulltext = model X, roles.verifier unset, agent role = model A. remaining_calls adds screen_verify calls (estimate.py:100-104); estimate_stages prices them with role_cfg(cfg,'agent') = A (180-181). The real call in verify_screen uses ctx.hub.agent_for('fulltext') = X (screening.py:329-330). The extraction eligibility check (extraction.py:230, tag eligibility_verify) also runs on the fulltext model and has no estimate row at all. rob_verify and synthesis_verify are NOT mispriced: rob._verify and synthesis.verify_one use synthesis.checker = hub['verifier'] else hub['agent'] (rob.py:110, synthesis.py:134), the same as the estimate. 2) history() records mean 'out' tokens (estimate.py:61,63) but estimate() takes tout = out_tokens(stage, effort) from DEFAULTS only (157,167-170); h['out'] is never read. 3) LLM._cost switches to pricing.long_context when pin > threshold (llm.py:250-254); estimate() uses only input_per_m/output_per_m (159). 4) history(root=Path('runs')) default globs root/'*/usage.jsonl' (45-48), which does not reach runs/<kind>/<topic>/<run>/; but the only callers pass self.run_dir.parent (server.py:292,296), so the default is dead code, not a live bug. 5) server.estimate hard-codes counts: pdfs {screen_full_text:n, extract:n, rob:n, feedback:n} (253), sync synthesis_verify 3 (258-259), feedback {feedback:3} (269), rerun feedback 2 (272), propagate feedback 2 (274); eval returns a fixed per-suite USD table with the note 'Gemini Flash agent, Gemini Pro judge' regardless of the configured models (275-281). 6) ROB_FLAG_RATE = 1.0 with the comment 'aspirin review, Haiku: 40 of 40' (estimate.py:35). 7) index.html:1323's note reads 'Already spent in this run folder: $X. Cached calls are free and are not counted again' - that sentence describes spent_so_far (server.spent sums usd, which is 0.0 for cached lines, llm.py:108) and is accurate; the estimate itself prices every call as fresh, but the note does not claim otherwise. 8) processPdfs (index.html:1382-1389) posts /api/run without calling /api/estimate; however the Run page's 'Process added PDFs' option (WHAT list, index.html:1229-1231) does show the pdfs estimate via the shared estimate call at 1318. The To-do 'Update now' card DOES show a cost: todos.py:338 appends '(about $X)' from engine.plan's _cost (engine.py:180-200, which uses estimate_stages), and the Overview stale box shows 'Updating costs about $' (index.html:703). redraftRob shows its estimate inside a native confirm() (1372-1375).

Evidence:
- `sragent/estimate.py:180` — synthesis_verify/screen_verify/rob_verify priced with roles.verifier else role_cfg('agent'); other rows via stage_cfg(ROW_STAGE)
- `sragent/stages/screening.py:329` — real screen_verify checker: hub['verifier'] else agent_for('fulltext'|'screen') - the stage model, not the agent role
- `sragent/stages/synthesis.py:134` — checker(): hub['verifier'] else hub['agent'] - used by synthesis verify and rob._verify (rob.py:110), matching the estimate's pricing
- `sragent/estimate.py:157` — tout = out_tokens(stage, effort) from DEFAULTS; history 'out' (61,63) never used; 159 prices with input_per_m/output_per_m only
- `sragent/llm.py:250` — _cost applies pricing.long_context above threshold_tokens; the estimate ignores it
- `sragent/estimate.py:45` — history(root=Path('runs')) default; only callers pass run_dir.parent (server.py:292,296), so the default is unused
- `sragent/ui/server.py:253` — pdfs/sync/feedback/rerun/propagate call counts hard-coded (253-274); eval fixed USD table and 'Gemini Flash agent, Gemini Pro judge' note (275-281)
- `sragent/estimate.py:35` — ROB_FLAG_RATE = 1.0 from one run

#### I110 · Docs and UI copy promise behaviour the code does not have: README 'each step resumes', GUIDE 'start over with --fresh', Overview 'one row per step', per-job model scope, serialized writes, 'update now/process PDFs use the Run-tab model'; the CLI docstring omits half the subcommands; verify-screening defaults differ; UI help text is a third copy of the config docs with hard-coded model names

README.md:23 'each step resumes where it stopped' (plan rewrites protocol.yaml every run, search is one-shot); GUIDE.md:752 '--fresh ... the cache keeps it cheap' (I31); GUIDE.md:272 'one row per step' (eight rows without snowball/report, Screening runs to fulltext); GUIDE.md:296 immediate RoB removal (I29); configs/README.md:151 'applies to that job only' (I21); GUIDE.md:327 serialized writes (I24); GUIDE 7.1 'update now, process PDFs start a job with the model last chosen in the Run tab' (they are STAGE_JOBS, server.py:335). cli.py:1-17 (the argparse description) lists 14 of 22 subcommands, omitting rerun, propagate, reassess-rob, verify-screening, verify-rob, fetch-files, revalidate-terms, eval-audit, eval-summary and the eval suites e2e/stagewise/synthesis/claim2_rescore; cli verify-screening defaults to 'pending' while screening.verify_existing defaults to 'full_text' (cli.py:367; screening.py:377). index.html embeds config claims and defaults ('the checks row in roles.verifier' 1286, verify_rob note 760, defaults table 1756-1764) and model names ('a few cents with Gemini Flash' 752; catDefault 'gemini-3.8-flash' 2049) that go stale independently.

- `GUIDE.md:272` — Overview: 'one row per step ... Run to here'

#### I112 · Evaluation and test bookkeeping: cost attribution parses the human-readable run.log, _resume has a dead claim2 branch, `--suite judge` alone has no controls, RESULTS.md violates its own rule and its code column changed meaning, the mock LLM is keyed on exact prompt wording, and a golden test freezes processing of a file for an excluded record

*confirmed · low · cli-eval · reasons: R10*

Evaluation and test bookkeeping as stated, with two corrections. usage_totals attributes cost by regex-matching '=== <suite> ===' marker lines in the human-readable run.log (sragent/eval/run_all.py:288, written at 200 via Ctx.log), so a log-format change moves all cost to 'unattributed'. _resume iterates only ('claim1_partial.json',) and its name.startswith('claim2') branch (129-137) is dead. A fresh `--suite judge` run has no planted items from claim1/claim2 and an empty judge log, so controls() returns [] and judge_validation is empty (247-257; judge_validation.py:21-53), although cli.py:352-354 and GUIDE.md:369 offer it. docs/RESULTS.md line 3 and 8-9 admit only complete --suite all runs, yet row 2 (line 22) is a claim2-only run; the code column is git_commit or code_version[:7] (run_all.py:373) and in both rows (21-22) it names a commit different from the 'git …' commit written in the same row's note. tests/mock_llm.py routes answers on about 40 prompt-substring checks (not 31; 29-78 is the first block), so prompt rewording silently changes golden behaviour. test_golden_pdf_supplied_then_sync writes only G03.txt but the golden 'processed' list is ['G03','G04'] because build() also wrote G04.txt and G04 was excluded at title/abstract, so its file was never read (and its sha never recorded) during the pipeline; the golden freezes a one-time extra fetch_one for an excluded record's file at the first process_pdfs, not a file that counts as new every time (after that call the sha and local_file are recorded, fulltext.py:164-167, 211).

Scenario: (1) usage_totals (sragent/eval/run_all.py:276-311) reads out/run.log and matches r'^(\d\d):(\d\d):(\d\d) \[eval [^\]]+\] === (\w+) ===' (288); those lines come from ectx.log(f'[eval {run_id}] === {s} ===') at 200 and the HH:MM:SS prefix from Ctx.log (context.py:72-77). Every usage.jsonl entry is attributed to the latest marker at or before its ts, else 'unattributed' (308); renaming the marker or the time prefix sends all cost to 'unattributed'. (2) _resume (120-140) loops over the one-element tuple ('claim1_partial.json',) and inside tests name.startswith('claim2') (135) which can never be true; claim2_partial.json is deliberately kept (comment at 130), so the branch is dead code. (3) `sragent eval --suite judge` on a fresh run: todo = ['judge'] (166); the judge branch (247-257) collects planted items from R['results'].get('claim1')/('claim2'), both absent, so controls([]) (judge_validation.py:21-31) returns [] and known_answer_controls has n=0/accuracy None; the inter-judge sample (47-53) draws from judge.log_path which holds no decisions yet. --resume takes the suite from the manifest (153), so it does not supply items either. cli.py:352-354 and GUIDE.md:369 list 'judge' as a selectable suite. (4) docs/RESULTS.md:3 says one row per complete `--suite all` run and 8-9 say partial suites are not recorded; row 2 (line 22) has suites 'claim2' and status 'complete'. The code column is M.get('git_commit') or code_version[:7] (run_all.py:373; manifest writes both at 109); row 1 (line 21) shows code 495d549 while its note says 'git 43731ea', and row 2 shows 4a40da3 while its note says 'git 4c76e58', so the column and the note name different commits in both rows. (5) tests/mock_llm.py mock_fn routes on 40 `'...' in u` prompt-substring checks across the file (29-78 is the first block; the last are at 166-173), e.g. 'Draft a review protocol', 'Assess this record against each eligibility criterion', 'Extract the following items'; rewording a prompt changes which branch answers and alters the golden snapshots. (6) test_golden_pdf_supplied_then_sync (tests/test_golden.py:322-331) writes only G03.txt, but build() (113-114) already wrote a .txt for every FULLTEXTS entry including G04; in the golden G04 is excluded at title_abstract (I3 not met), so the pipeline never called fetch_one for it and never recorded its sha (fulltext.py:164-167); new_local_files (268-282) therefore lists G04 as new at the first process_pdfs (sync.py:16-19), giving processed ['G03','G04'] (tests/golden/pdf_supplied_then_sync.json:16-19). process_pdfs then runs fetch_one for G04 (sync.py:30), which records the sha and stores local_file (fulltext.py:211), so the file is not new on later calls: the frozen behaviour is a one-time extra fetch for an excluded record's file, not 'every time'.

Evidence:
- `sragent/eval/run_all.py:288` — regex over run.log suite markers; 'unattributed' fallback at 308; marker written by ectx.log at 200 with Ctx.log's time prefix (context.py:76-77)
- `sragent/eval/run_all.py:129` — for name in ('claim1_partial.json',): ... if name.startswith('claim2') at 135 is unreachable
- `sragent/eval/run_all.py:247` — judge branch builds planted from R['results'] claim1/claim2 of this run only; todo=[suite] at 166; resume takes suite from manifest at 153
- `sragent/eval/judge_validation.py:21` — controls([]) -> []; known_answer_controls n=0 accuracy None (37-44); inter-judge sample from judge.log_path (47-50)
- `sragent/cli.py:353` — eval --suite choices include 'judge'; GUIDE.md:369 lists it as runnable
- `docs/RESULTS.md:3` — 'complete' rule (3) and partial suites excluded (8-9); row 2 at 22 has suites 'claim2'; row 1 at 21 code 495d549 vs note 'git 43731ea'
- `sragent/eval/run_all.py:373` — code column = git_commit or code_version[:7]; manifest records both at 109
- `tests/mock_llm.py:29` — routing by prompt substrings; 40 '" in u' checks in the file (first block 29-78, last at 166-173)

### Dead code and phantom states (2)

#### I13 · The 'fulltexts' to-do answer (process / later / 'not obtainable' ids) is dead in the UI; the real buttons use other endpoints with a different log id, and 'later' discards the ids

*confirmed · low · human-loop · reasons: R4, R7*

todos.build emits a `fulltexts` to-do with options process/later and an `extra.unavailable` field (sragent/todos.py:169-186) and todos.apply implements them (453-461; human_decisions todo_id `fulltexts` at 566, provenance per id at 568-570), but index.html never posts todo_id 'fulltexts': pdfPanel (index.html:1040-1070) renders no option buttons and the real actions go to /api/bulk pdf_unavailable (1073; human_decisions id `pdf:<rid>`, todos.py:600) and /api/run {action:'pdfs'} (1382-1385; no human_decisions line). The to-do options and the 453-461 branch are therefore dead in the UI. For a direct API client, `opt == 'later'` is dispatched before any kind (todos.py:416-420) and _category returns None for 'later' (392-393), so ids sent with 'later' are neither stored in pdf_unavailable nor recorded in provenance (they remain only in the raw human_decisions line). Severity is low because the UI never exercises the dead path.

Scenario: todos.build (todos.py:169-186) emits the 'fulltexts' to-do with options process/later and extra.unavailable (show_if '*'); todos.apply implements them at 453-461 (pdf_unavailable, pdf_wanted pop, job {action:'pdfs'}), logs human_decisions todo_id 'fulltexts' (566) and provenance pdf_provision per id (568-570). In index.html the only readers of that to-do are renderTodo (753-754), renderPdfs (1362) and counters (617, 732); both render pdfPanel (1040-1070), which has no option buttons and never calls /api/answer with todo_id 'fulltexts' (grep: no such post). The real buttons: 'Not obtainable' -> pdfUnavailable -> POST /api/bulk {kind:'pdf_unavailable'} (1073) -> server.mutate -> todos.bulk (594-602) which logs one human_decisions line per id with todo_id 'pdf:<rid>' (600) and the same provenance pdf_provision; 'Process added PDFs' -> processPdfs -> POST /api/run {action:'pdfs'} (1382-1385) -> server.submit, which writes no human_decisions line. An API client sending {todo_id:'fulltexts', option:'later', extra:{unavailable:'r1,r2'}}: todos.apply takes the `if opt == 'later'` branch first (416-420) and only snoozes; the elif at 453 never runs, and _category('fulltexts','later') returns None (392-393) so no provenance either; the ids survive only inside the raw human_decisions line.

Evidence:
- `sragent/todos.py:169` — build: 'fulltexts' to-do with options process/later and extra.unavailable show_if '*'
- `sragent/todos.py:416` — apply: `if opt == 'later'` snoozes and skips every kind branch; fulltexts branch at 453-461 unreachable for 'later'
- `sragent/todos.py:392` — _category: opt 'later' -> None, so no pdf_provision provenance for ids sent with 'later'
- `sragent/todos.py:600` — bulk pdf_unavailable: log_answer todo_id f'pdf:{rid}' per id (vs 'fulltexts' at 566)
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1040` — pdfPanel: buttons are copy, Decide without it (pdfUnwant), Not obtainable (pdfUnavailable), Process added PDFs (processPdfs); no option buttons, no /api/answer
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1073` — pdfUnavailable -> /api/bulk {kind:'pdf_unavailable'}; 1072 pdfUnwant -> /api/bulk {kind:'pdf_unwant'}
- `sragent/ui/static/index.html:1382` — processPdfs -> /api/run {action:'pdfs', agent, extra_budget} (1385); no human_decisions line in server.submit

#### I50 · Dead stage machinery and a phantom flag source: engine.ORDER/STAGE_FOR, sync.update_synthesis and pipeline.enabled are unreferenced, and 'evidence_check' is handled by todos/report/feedback but produced nowhere while GUIDE says it is

*not yet verified · low · graph-engine · reasons: R1*

engine.ORDER and STAGE_FOR define a stage order and an action->stage map that nothing reads (sragent/engine.py:29-32; grep 0 refs); sync.update_synthesis has no caller (sync.py:80); pipeline.enabled is used only by tests (pipeline.py:95; tests/test_offline.py:1003). The 'evidence_check' flag source is read by todos.py:194, 215, report.FLAG_KIND (report.py:399) and feedback.EVIDENCE_FLAG_MARKS (feedback.py:271), but no code creates it: extraction.py:333-335 deliberately makes check 'none' and 'uncovered' markers beside the value, not flags, while GUIDE.md:452 says 'a value with no source sentence at all is still flagged'.

Evidence:
- `sragent/engine.py:29` — ORDER / STAGE_FOR constants, no other reference (grep)
- `sragent/sync.py:80` — update_synthesis: no caller (grep)
- `sragent/todos.py:194` — 'evidence_check' accepted; grep shows readers only
- `sragent/stages/extraction.py:333` — comment: markers, not flags

## 4. Candidate smells dropped when merging

- S205: LOCATE/DECOMPOSE prompt growing with review size is a scaling/performance observation, not an inconsistency between docs, code and UI.
- S254: Module-level mutable globals in claim2 are a code-quality concern (testability), not an inconsistency or hidden review state.
- S255: Eval hard-coding the emicizumab field names is a reusability wish for other benchmark topics, not an inconsistency in the current system.
- MRV: Every model call is one prompt and one JSON object: the model cannot fetch, count, re-read, validate or ask: A design observation / feature request for tool-using or multi-turn calls; its concrete sub-facts with evidence (EXTRACT_MERGE without the paper, SEARCH_REFINE without synonyms, unvalidated PLAN_REVISE concepts) are kept in I94.
